### [1] [Geometry of Noisy Quantum Many-Body Dynamics with Continuous Symmetries: Entanglement and Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11297)
*Marco Lastres,Sanjay Moudgalya*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有全局连续对称性（如U(1)和SU(2)）的噪声布朗模型中的酉量子动力学，重点关注Rényi纠缠熵以及流体和非流体关联函数。通过将平均晚期动力学映射到有效副本哈密顿量的低能物理，发现演化受基态流形的量子几何控制，该几何与k-交换子（系统k个副本的对称代数）直接相关。在相互作用系统中，k-交换子仅由系统对称性决定，与噪声演化的微观细节无关。利用含时变分原理（TDVP），作者为相互作用系统中连续对称性导致的亚弹道Rényi纠缠增长和非流体关联函数的异常衰减提供了简洁的几何解释，并将该行为与k-交换子流形中的奇点（源于连续局域对称性导致的冻结"空穴"态）紧密联系起来。这项工作为系统研究具有连续对称性的噪声系统（包括Haar随机电路）中的可观测量提供了通用的几何框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有连续全局对称性的噪声量子系统中的纠缠动力学和关联函数行为是一个重要问题。此前在U(1)对称系统中已识别出"空穴"（voids）态在可观测量动力学中的关键作用，但其背后的统一机制尚不明确。作者旨在提供一个通用的几何框架，统一解释相互作用系统中亚弹道Rényi纠缠增长和非流体关联函数的异常衰减现象，并阐明空穴态的深层作用。

Method: 作者将噪声布朗模型的平均晚期动力学映射到有效副本哈密顿量的低能物理中。关键步骤是识别k-交换子（k-commutants）——即k个副本系统的对称代数——其几何结构直接决定基态流形的量子几何。利用含时变分原理（TDVP），他们分析了k-交换子流形中的奇点（由连续局域对称性导致的冻结空穴态引起）如何导致纠缠的亚弹道增长和非流体关联函数的异常衰减。他们还将相互作用系统中阿贝尔与非阿贝尔连续对称性、以及自由费米子系统的情形进行了对比，这些系统在k-交换子几何上存在差异。

Result: 研究发现，在相互作用系统中，k-交换子仅由系统对称性决定，与噪声演化的微观细节无关。Rényi纠缠的亚弹道增长和非流体关联函数的异常衰减均源于k-交换子流形中的奇点，这些奇点来自连续局域对称性导致的冻结空穴态。不同系统（阿贝尔/非阿贝尔连续对称性的相互作用系统与自由费米子系统）因k-交换子几何不同而表现出不同行为。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个通用的几何框架，用于系统研究具有连续对称性的噪声系统（包括Haar随机电路）中的可观测量。该框架统一了此前在U(1)系统中识别的空穴态的作用，并揭示了k-交换子的量子几何作为控制纠缠增长和关联函数衰减的核心机制。

Abstract: We study unitary quantum dynamics in noisy Brownian models with global continuous symmetries, such as $U(1)$ and $SU(2)$, focusing on Rényi entanglement entropies and hydrodynamic and non-hydrodynamic correlators. By mapping the averaged late-time dynamics to the low-energy physics of effective replica Hamiltonians, we find that the evolution is controlled by the quantum geometry of their ground-state manifolds, which is directly related to the geometry of $k$-commutants---the symmetry algebra of $k$ replicas of the system. In interacting systems, these $k$-commutants are generically determined solely by the symmetries of the system, independent of microscopic details of the noisy evolution. This allows us to use the time-dependent variational principle (TDVP) to provide simple geometric explanations for the sub-ballistic Rényi entanglement growth and the anomalous decay of non-hydrodynamic correlators in interacting systems with continuous symmetries. We find this behavior to be intimately connected to singularities within the $k$-commutant manifolds, arising from frozen ``void'' states in the Hilbert space that exist due to continuous on-site symmetries. This also demystifies the important role of voids in the dynamics of these observables, previously identified in $U(1)$ symmetric systems. We compare these behaviors in interacting systems with Abelian and non-Abelian continuous symmetries and in free-fermion systems, which differ in the geometry of their $k$-commutants. Ultimately, this work provides a general geometric framework for systematically studying observables in noisy systems with continuous symmetries, including Haar-random circuits.

</details>


### [2] [Operational identifiability of false-vacuum decay rates in the quantum Ising chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11339)
*Boliang Yu,Ruixin Zhou,Hang Su*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文针对一维量子Ising链中假真空衰变的实时张量网络模拟，提出了一种多层级识别框架，用以从有限时间量子动力学中分离可观测衰变与估计器及有限尺寸有效性问题，建立可靠的有限时间衰变系数并指出热力学速率诠释所需的额外验证条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限时间量子动力学中提取热力学成核速率，必须将可观测衰变与估计器误差及有限尺寸效应分离开来，否则所得速率缺乏可靠的热力学诠释基础。

Method: 发展了一种多层级识别框架，应用于一维量子Ising链的实时张量网络模拟。利用两个参数点上的相干双扭结振幅，半定量预测无限链存活几率与磁化动力学；通过匹配键维数收敛判据分析微观最近邻键响应，并采用晶格分辨的WKB作用量对比相干气泡谱计算。

Result: 在十二个非空分析区间的参数点上，存活系数具有0.902的中位晶格-理论比，磁化-面积斜率比区间为0.809--0.953；真空-对相干性在满足匹配键维数收敛判据的七个参数点中贡献60.0--81.5%；晶格分辨WKB作用量将固定前置因子差异的中位数降至4.13%。

Conclusion: 跨层次的定量一致性、依赖于可观测量的约化模型误差，以及仍需有限尺寸验证的热力学速率诠释可以共存；该框架为可靠的有限时间衰变系数及其热力学速率解释提供了清晰的验证路径。

Abstract: Extracting a thermodynamic nucleation rate from finite-time quantum dynamics requires separating observable decay from estimator and finite-size validity. We develop a multilevel identification framework to real-time tensor-network simulations of false-vacuum decay in the one-dimensional quantum Ising chain. Across twelve parameter points with non-empty analysis intervals, the same coherent two-kink amplitudes semi-quantitatively predict both infinite-chain survival and magnetization dynamics: the survival coefficient has a median lattice-to-theory ratio of 0.902, while the magnetization-area slope ratios span 0.809--0.953. By contrast, the microscopic nearest-neighbour bond response is coherence dominated: vacuum--pair coherence contributes 60.0--81.5\% within the seven parameter points satisfying the matched-bond-dimension convergence criterion, while substantial late-window slope discrepancies remain that cannot be removed by a scalar normalization. The framework establishes reliable finite-time decay coefficients and identifies the additional finite-size and branch-validation requirements for a bulk thermodynamic rate interpretation. Within the two-kink model, a lattice-resolved WKB action reduces the median fixed-prefactor discrepancy with the coherent-bubble spectral calculation to 4.13\%. Quantitative cross-level consistency, observable-dependent reduced-model error, and a thermodynamic-rate interpretation that remains subject to finite-size validation can therefore coexist.

</details>


### [3] [A piston-like polymer stochastic heat engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11507)
*Yi-Jui Chiu,Cheng-Hung Chang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于聚合物的随机热机，通过将聚合物拉入和拉出窄通道的循环过程实现热功转换，其力-位置图类比于传统热机的压力-体积图，并在数值上验证了与通用低耗散边界、卡诺效率恢复以及功和功率的低耗散标度等特性的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体随机热机虽被视作宏观活塞-气缸热机的微观类似物，但由于实际限制，它们远非直接的力发生器。本研究旨在引入一种更接近活塞-气缸热机运行方式的聚合物基随机引擎，以克服这一局限。

Method: 提出了一种聚合物基随机引擎：在变温条件下，通过循环过程将聚合物拉入和拉出窄通道，实现热到功的转换。做功直接从力-位置图中的循环轨迹获得，类比传统热机的压力-体积图。通过数值模拟考察其效率与功率等热力学特性。

Result: 数值结果表明：聚合物引擎与通用低耗散效率边界一致；在再生条件下可恢复卡诺效率；功和功率呈现低耗散标度行为。尽管其自由度数量远高于胶体引擎，仍遵循了此类系统的若干特征特性。

Conclusion: 该聚合物引擎成功模拟了活塞-气缸热机的运作方式，兼具实际力生成能力和随机热机的通用热力学特性，为微观热机设计与实现提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Colloidal stochastic engines are often regarded as microscopic analogues of macroscopic pis ton cylinder heat engines. However, although they share some underlying physical principles, such systems remain far from being direct force generators from a practical perspective. Motivated by this limitation, the present study introduces a polymer-based stochastic engine that more closely mimics the operation of piston cylinder engines. In this setup, heat is converted into work through a cyclic process in which a polymer is pulled into and out of a narrow channel under varying tem peratures. The work performed by the engine can be directly obtained from the cyclic trajectory in the force position diagram, analogous to the pressure volume diagram in traditional heat engines. Despite its much higher number of degrees of freedom compared to colloidal engines, the polymer en gine nevertheless follows several characteristic features observed in such systems. Numerical results demonstrate consistency with universal low-dissipation bounds for e ciency, recovery of Carnot e ciency under regeneration, and low-dissipation scaling of work and power.

</details>


### [4] [Ideal heat engine cycles at maximal efficiency -- the ideal gas and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11966)
*Gregory Behrendt,Sebastian Deffner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了在给定热机循环中，哪种工作介质能使效率最大化。通过对与摩尔体积和温度呈多项式关系的亥姆霍兹势进行推导，得到了理想斯特林、奥托和布雷顿循环的精确表达式，发现最大效率普遍由工作介质内能关于温度线性变化的系统实现，包括理想气体、经典谐振子和橡胶带的唯象模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上人们直觉认为理想气体是最好的工作介质，但作者指出实际情况更为复杂。作者希望从基础热力学势出发，系统性地回答在特定热机循环下何种工作介质能实现最高效率这一根本性问题。

Method: 作者从一般化的、在摩尔体积和温度上呈多项式依赖的亥姆霍兹势出发，推导出理想斯特林、奥托和布雷顿三种循环的精确效率表达式，并通过比较不同工作介质（包括理想气体、经典谐振子、橡胶带唯象模型等）的效率来寻找最优解。

Result: 研究发现，最大效率具有普遍性特征，即由内能对温度呈线性依赖的热力学系统实现。理想气体是其中的一个特例，但并非唯一，经典谐振子和橡胶带的唯象模型同样属于这类能够达到最优效率的工作介质。

Conclusion: 最大化热机效率的关键不在于工作介质是否为理想气体，而在于其热力学性质是否满足关于温度的线性内能关系，这一结论将多种看似不同的物理系统统一在一个框架之下。

Abstract: Given a particular heat engine cycle, what is the optimal working medium that results in the highest efficiency? While one might jump to the conclusion that it must surely be the ideal gas, the situation is actually more intricate. Starting with a general Hemlholtz potential that depends polynomially on molar volume and temperature we derive exact expressions for the ideal Stirling, Otto, and Brayton cycles. We find that quite universally the maximal efficiency is achieved for thermodynamics systems, whose working mediums are linear in temperature. This includes the ideal gas, but also classical harmonic oscillators and pheonmenological models of the rubber band.

</details>


### [5] [Macroscopic fluctuation theory for the multi-time statistics of current in non-stationary diffusive systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12119)
*Sabyasachi Chowdhury,Kapil Sharma,Sandeep Jangid,Tridib Sadhu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展宏观涨落理论（MFT）的涨落流体动力学框架，研究了扩散系统在无限直线上非稳态下的多时间统计性质。对于非相互作用晶格气体和硬核布朗点粒子这两个最简单情形，给出了MFT的显式解，得到多时间大偏差统计；对于一般系统则通过微扰方法求解MFT，得到双时间关联的显式结果。关键发现是：此前在平坦初始条件下观察到的电流涨落与分数布朗运动之间的联系，在阶梯初始条件下不再成立。该水动力学结果通过与微观动力学求解及数值模拟相互验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平衡物理中电流涨落统计是核心研究课题，但以往工作大多聚焦于单时间统计，多时间统计的推广研究相对匮乏。现有文献主要报道了平坦初始条件下电流涨落与分数布朗运动的关系，但该联系在更一般的初始条件下是否成立尚不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将宏观涨落理论（MFT）的涨落流体动力学框架扩展到多时间统计。针对非相互作用晶格气体和硬核布朗点粒子求MFT显式解；对一般系统采用微扰方法求解MFT并推导双时间关联。通过求解微观动力学（非相互作用气体和对称简单排他过程）及数值模拟对水动力学结果进行独立验证。

Result: 获得了两类可解系统（非相互作用气体与硬核粒子）的多时间大偏差统计显式解，以及一般系统的双时间关联表达式。发现分数布朗运动与电流涨落的联系仅在平坦初始条件下成立，阶梯初始条件下该联系被破坏。微观求解与数值模拟均与水动力学结果一致。

Conclusion: MFT成功推广至多时间统计领域，为扩散系统非稳态下的多时间涨落研究提供了统一框架。初始条件对电流涨落的时间关联结构有本质影响，阶梯初始条件下系统不再表现出分数布朗运动行为。该工作为理解非平衡系统中多时间统计性质开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The statistics of current fluctuations has long been a central object of study in non-equilibrium physics. Most existing work has focused on one-time statistics, while their multi-time generalisation remains comparatively less explored. We address this gap by extending the fluctuating hydrodynamics framework of Macroscopic Fluctuation Theory (MFT) to study multi-time statistics in the non-stationary state of a diffusive system on an infinite line. For the simplest cases of a non-interacting lattice gas and hard-core Brownian point particles, we present explicit solution of the MFT leading to multi-time large-deviation statistics. For generic systems, the MFT is solved perturbatively, yielding explicit results for two-time correlations. These reveal that the connection between current fluctuations and fractional Brownian motion, previously observed for flat initial conditions, does not persist for step initial conditions. We independently verify these hydrodynamic results by solving the corresponding microscopic dynamics for the non-interacting gas and for the symmetric simple exclusion process. Additional confirmation comes from numerical simulations.

</details>


### [6] [Shortcuts to Parameter Sweeps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12154)
*Chi Xiang,Guodong Cheng,Geng Li*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出STPS（参数扫描快捷方式）方法，利用辅助控制将概率密度沿预定义的瞬时稳态族进行输运，从而在单次有限时间参数扫描中获得完整参数区间上的连续响应曲线，适用于平衡态和非平衡稳态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广泛的参数范围内高效评估平稳参数敏感性，对于识别有影响力的训练数据、拟合力场和预测材料响应至关重要，但标准的逐点方法需要重复的松弛和采样，计算成本高昂。

Method: STPS通过工程化的控制策略，利用辅助控制将概率密度沿规定的一系列瞬时稳态进行输运，在有限时间参数扫描中基于协方差响应关系从单次受控扫描估计整个参数区间上的连续响应曲线。该方法适用于平衡态和非平衡稳态系统，包括平稳分布未知的系统，并可直接利用高维环境中的平稳样本实施。

Result: 在单粒子和相互作用的多体系统上的数值测试表明，STPS产生的响应曲线与参考结果高度一致，验证了该方法的准确性。

Conclusion: STPS为随机模拟中的连续敏感性分析提供了高效、基于样本的框架，能够在单次扫描中获得完整的参数响应曲线，显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: Efficient evaluation of stationary parametric sensitivities over broad parameter ranges is important for identifying influential training data, fitting force fields, and predicting material responses, but standard pointwise approaches require repeated relaxation and sampling. Here we introduce Shortcuts to Parameter Sweeps (STPS), an engineered control strategy that uses an auxiliary control to transport the probability density along a prescribed family of instantaneous stationary states during a finite-time parameter sweep. This enables the continuous response curve over the full parameter interval to be estimated from a single controlled sweep using covariance-based response relations. STPS applies to both equilibrium and nonequilibrium steady-state systems, including those with unknown stationary distributions, and can be implemented directly using stationary samples in high-dimensional settings. Numerical tests on single-particle and interacting many-body systems show that STPS yields response curves in close agreement with reference results. These findings establish STPS as an efficient, sample-based framework for continuous sensitivity analysis in stochastic simulations.

</details>


### [7] [Finite-depth scaling and an exact Bernoulli-leaf identity for the min-plus process on the binary tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12295)
*José Ricardo G. Mendonça*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模蒙特卡洛模拟（有效树深达N=60）验证了min-plus过程（一种二叉树上的随机凝聚-湮灭型过程）的关键性质：在p=1/2非对称√N侧验证了Beta(2,1)拉伸指数极限；在次临界区确认了封闭形式P(X∞=1)=(1-2p)/(1-p)；并针对Bernoulli(q)初始条件变体发现了一个精确的封闭恒等式，该恒等式精确定位了吸收态相变点pc=1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: min-plus过程是数学、物理学和计算机科学中作为极大值型递归分布方程的可解实例而受到关注。作者希望通过大规模模拟在有限深度上验证其理论极限结果，特别是Chen-Duquesne-Shi和Morfe最近提出的随机齐次系统分类下的行为，并为相关递归方程的大规模模拟提供实用方法。

Method: 采用自主提出的层次递归（level-wise recursion）和基于FFT的预计算叶表（FFT-based precomputed leaf table）技术，在有效树深N=60的规模上进行大规模蒙特卡洛模拟。对标准min-plus过程在p=1/2、p<1/2和p>1/2情形下分别验证理论预测，同时对Bernoulli(q)初始条件变体在p=1/2时的封闭恒等式进行精确验证。

Result: 在有效树深N=60下：(1) 在p=1/2非对称√N侧提供了Beta(2,1)拉伸指数极限的有限深度佐证；(2) 次临界区确认了封闭形式P(X∞=1)=(1-2p)/(1-p)在蒙特卡洛误差内成立；(3) 超临界区记录到超过初等下界(2p)^N的均值增长；(4) 对Bernoulli(q)变体发现P(X_N=0)=q的精确恒等式，定位相变在算子混合概率pc=1/2处而非初始零密度处，并显示正数部分的条件分布在q变化时会发生显著形变。

For $G=\operatorname{SL}_n$, the uniform sharp exponent is $n-1/n$. For orthogonal groups in dimensions $n=2,3$, with $\operatorname{char}(k)\neq 2$, the uniform sharp exponent is $n/2$.

Abstract: We compute explicit defining equations for eight fake quadrics arising as $\mathbb Z/2\times\mathbb Z/4$-covers of two singular $\mathbb Z/2$-Godeaux surfaces obtained in earlier work of the second author. Starting from explicit equations for the universal covers of the Godeaux surfaces, we reconstruct the relevant character eigenspaces and determine the homogeneous ideals of the bicanonical models of the eight fake quadrics in $\mathbb P^8$. All eight models are defined over $\mathbb Q$. We prove that the surfaces are pairwise non-isomorphic and rigid. Combined with the non-product result established in the earlier work, this gives the first explicit projective models of fake quadrics which are not isogenous to a product of curves.

*Lev Borisov,Carlos Rito*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造了三维射影空间P³中具有18个普通尖点（ordinary cusps）的五次曲面。作者从Barth–Rams关于含12个尖点（3-可分子集）的五次曲面描述出发，通过特殊化接触三次曲面沿两条斜线奇异得到16个尖点的族，并在小有限域上找到18个尖点的例子。主要构造基于五次曲面具有两个Barth–Rams分解，对应两组12个尖点交于7个点；作者证明了此类曲面在模空间中包含一个6维分量，其一般成员具有17个尖点，从而可高效地在有限域上寻找18个尖点的成员。最后通过Newton–Hensel提升和LLL重构将其中一个曲面提升到特征零，得到定义在22次数域上的五次曲面，并验证其恰有18个普通尖点且无其他奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在P³中构造具有大量普通尖点的五次曲面是代数曲面模空间研究中的重要问题。此前已知的构造方法（如Barth–Rams分解）只能保证产生有限数量的尖点，而寻找具有18个（甚至更多）普通尖点的五次曲面是一个具有挑战性的开放问题，尤其是在特征零下。

Method: 论文采用多条途径：(1) 从Barth–Rams对含12个尖点的五次曲面（3-可分子集）的描述出发，通过特殊化两个接触三次曲面沿两条斜线奇异，构造16个尖点的族；(2) 基于承认两个Barth–Rams分解的五次曲面（两组12个尖点交于7点），证明该轨迹在模空间中含6维分量；(3) 利用上述6维分量在有限域上高效搜索具有18个尖点的成员；(4) 对有限域上找到的例子，利用Newton–Hensel提升和LLL格基约化重构将其提升到特征零，获得定义在22次数域上的曲面。

Result: 成功构造了P³中具有18个普通尖点且无其他奇点的五次曲面，并在小有限域上找到例子，同时将该例子提升到特征零，得到定义在22次数域上的具体曲面。

Conclusion: 本文通过两次Barth–Rams分解的交叉构造与有限域搜索结合特征零提升，在P³中构造出具有18个普通尖点的五次曲面，证明了这类曲面的存在性，并提供了有效的算法性构造途径。

Abstract: We construct quintic surfaces in the three-dimensional projective space $\mathbb P^3$ with $18$ ordinary cusps. Our starting point is the Barth--Rams description of quintics containing a $3$-divisible set of $12$ cusps. A specialization in which the two contact cubics are singular along two skew lines produces a family with $16$ cusps, and examples with $18$ cusps can be found over small finite fields. Our main construction is based on quintics admitting two Barth--Rams decompositions. The corresponding sets of $12$ cusps meet in $7$ points, and we prove that the locus of quintics admitting two such decompositions contains a $6$-dimensional component in the moduli space whose general member has $17$ cusps. This makes it possible to find members with $18$ cusps efficiently over finite fields. We lift one of these surfaces to characteristic zero using Newton--Hensel lifting and LLL reconstruction, obtaining a quintic over a number field of degree $22$. We verify that this surface has $18$ ordinary cusps and no other singularities.

</details>

### [26] [Variational Parameter Calibration with Physics-Aware Latent-Space Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11435)
*Qiyao Zhou,Xujia Zhu,Pierre Joli,Yu Cong,Sibo Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理感知的神经网络潜空间框架，用于参数化动力系统的降阶前向建模与变分参数估计。该方法通过自编码器构建从物理参数到流场的可微代理模型，在离线训练时利用观测监督增强潜变量与系统参数的相关性，在线逆问题则通过代理诱导的观测算子直接在参数空间求解。在两个CFD基准上验证表明，仅靠重建精度不足以支撑逆建模，观测监督可提升潜空间的可分性和时间组织性，且在含噪、低分辨率、随机掩蔽和分块部分观测等实际测量条件下具有更好的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化动力系统的前向和逆向来建模需要代理模型不仅要具备状态预测准确性，还要能有效支持参数校准。然而，目前缺乏一个系统性的端到端可微公式化框架，将基于深度学习的降阶代理模型与变分参数估计有机耦合，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于自编码器的物理感知潜空间框架：通过可微代理模型将物理参数映射为预测流场（前向过程），离线训练时引入可观测监督促使潜变量保留与系统参数相关的信息，在线阶段则利用代理诱导的观测算子在参数空间中求解逆问题（变分参数估计）。

Result: 在两个CFD基准上，该方法在参数校准误差和变异性方面优于标准代理模型；量化潜空间分析显示观测监督改善了案例级可分性与潜变量的时间组织性；在含噪、低分辨率、随机掩蔽和分块部分观测等现实测量条件下均展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 重建精度本身不足以支撑逆建模，因为缺乏端到端可微性或物理感知会削弱变分参数校准的效果。引入观测监督的潜空间框架能够同时提升前向预测和反向参数估计的性能，为降阶建模与参数反演提供了统一的可微求解范式。

Abstract: Forward and inverse modeling of parametric dynamical systems requires surrogate models that are not only accurate for state prediction, but also informative for parameter calibration. However, a systematic end-to-end differentiable formulation for coupling deep-learning-based reduced-order surrogates with variational parameter estimation remains underdeveloped. In this work, we introduce a physics-aware neural-network-based latent-space framework for reduced-order forward modeling and variational parameter estimation. The proposed autoencoder-based approach yields a differentiable surrogate that maps physical parameters to predicted flow fields through a latent representation. The observable supervision is used during offline training to encourage the latent variables to retain information correlated with system parameters, while the online inverse problem is solved in the parameter space through the surrogate-induced observation operator. The method is evaluated on two computational-fluid-dynamics benchmarks. The results show that reconstruction accuracy alone is insufficient for inverse modeling, owing to the lack of end-to-end differentiability or physics awareness for variational parameter calibration. Quantitative latent-space analysis further shows that observable supervision improves case-level separability and temporal organization of latent representations. Experiments with realistic measurement settings, including noisy, low-resolution, randomly masked, and block-wise partial observations, demonstrate the robustness of the proposed framework and show that it generally reduces calibration error and variability compared with the standard surrogate models.

</details>


### [27] [FarSky: Task-Aware Latent-Space Coupling for Generative Intra-Hour Solar Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11254)
*Yann Fabel,Bijan Nouri,Milon Miah,Niklas Blum,Luis F. Zarzalejo,Julia Kowalski,Robert Pitz-Paal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FarSky，一种基于生成式框架的太阳辐照度预测方法。它通过多任务自编码器学习天空图像的共享潜在表征，再利用潜空间扩散模型生成未来潜在状态并解码为辐照度预测。方法在西班牙Almería的多年ASI数据集上训练，在两个独立测试集上超越了持久性、端到端和生成式基线，预测技能最高提升11个百分点，斜坡事件检测F1分数超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的太阳辐照度预测对光伏电力可靠并网至关重要。虽然全天空成像仪（ASI）结合深度学习方法已显著提升小时级预测精度，但现有方法大多局限于确定性预测，难以预测斜坡事件（ramp events）。因此需要一种能够提供概率预测且更好捕捉极端变化的方法。

Method: FarSky包含两大核心组件：1）多任务自编码器，同时学习图像重建和辐照度估计的共享潜在表征；2）潜空间扩散模型（latent diffusion model），以近期观测为条件生成未来潜在状态，再从中直接解码出辐照度预测。通过随机采样天然获得概率预测。框架基于西班牙Almería太阳能平台的多年度ASI数据集开发。

Result: 在两个独立测试数据集上，FarSky在确定性和概率预测性能上均达到最优，预测技能比基线最高提升11个百分点；在斜坡事件检测方面显著优于现有方法，F1分数超过60%。

Conclusion: 研究证明将生成模型与任务感知的潜空间耦合相结合，能够为太阳辐照度预测带来显著优势，尤其在概率预测与斜坡事件捕捉方面显示出巨大潜力。

Abstract: Accurate solar irradiance forecasting is essential for the reliable integration of photovoltaic power into modern electricity grids. All-sky imagers (ASI) provide high-resolution observations of clouds, making them well suited for intra-hour forecasting. Recent deep learning approaches have substantially improved forecast accuracy but are often limited by deterministic predictions and a reduced capability to anticipate ramp events. This work proposes FarSky, a generative forecasting framework that leverages latent-space coupling to learn task-aware representations of sky images. A multi-task autoencoder first learns a shared latent representation for image reconstruction and irradiance estimation. A latent diffusion model then generates future latent states conditioned on recent observations, from which irradiance forecasts are directly decoded. Probabilistic forecasts are inherently obtained through stochastic sampling. The framework is developed using a multi-year ASI dataset acquired at the Plataforma Solar de Almería, Spain, and evaluated on two independent test datasets against persistence, state-of-the-art end-to-end, and generative forecasting approaches. FarSky achieves the best overall deterministic and probabilistic forecasting performance, improving forecast skill by up to 11 percentage points. Furthermore, it substantially improves ramp event detection over existing methods, achieving F1-scores above 60%. These results demonstrate the potential of combining generative models with task-aware latent-space coupling for solar forecasting.

</details>


### [28] [RECAST: A Machine-Learning Framework for Correction and Super-Resolution of Coarse-Grid PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11572)
*Maryam Reza,Farbod Faraji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RECAST是一个机器学习框架，通过在粗网格数值时间步进循环内加入学习到的误差修正，并重建对应的细网格状态，来恢复粗网格PDE模拟中丢失的准确性和空间保真度。在6个一维PDE系统上（粗化8-16倍，1000步闭环 rollout），相比未修正的粗网格求解器将时间平均相对误差降低约50-92%，并在5000步rollout中优于当代粗校正架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗网格数值求解器能大幅降低时间依赖PDE模拟的计算成本，但欠分辨（under-resolution）会同时降低解的轨迹精度和空间保真度。作者希望在不放弃粗网格高效演化的前提下，恢复丢失的求解精度。

Method: RECAST包含两个核心组件：1）在数值时间步进循环内部进行学习到的误差修正（learned correction），修正粗网格轨迹；2）从修正后的粗网格历史状态中重建对应的细网格状态（super-resolution reconstruction）。两者结合实现粗网格演化和细网格保真的兼得。

Result: 在六个一维PDE系统（涵盖输运、扩散、色散、反应和波动力学）上，使用空间网格粗化8-16倍和1000步闭环rollout，RECAST与细网格参考解保持紧密对齐，将时间平均相对误差降低约50-92%。框架还展现了对外推PDE参数值的泛化能力，并在5000步rollout中优于当代粗校正架构，实现更低的误差和更好的长时程一致性。

Conclusion: RECAST证明学习到的误差修正与重建能力可以在大幅粗化的PDE演化中不伴随相应的解保真度损失，为面向高维数值模拟的机器学习加速提供了概念验证路径，具有在科学与工程领域广泛应用的潜力。

Abstract: Coarse-grid numerical solvers can substantially reduce the computational cost of time-dependent PDE simulation, but under-resolution often degrades both the trajectory and the spatial fidelity of the solution. We introduce RECAST (Recurrent Error Correction And Super-resolution of coarse-grid Trajectories), a machine-learning framework designed to restore this lost accuracy while retaining coarse-grid evolution. RECAST combines learned correction within the numerical time-stepping loop with reconstruction of the corresponding fine-grid state from the corrected coarse history. We evaluate the framework on six one-dimensional PDE systems spanning transport, diffusion, dispersion, reaction, and wave dynamics, using spatial grids coarsened by factors of 8-16 and 1000-step closed-loop rollouts from unseen initial conditions. Across the test cases, RECAST remains closely aligned with the fine-grid reference solutions and reduces time-averaged relative error by approximately 50-92% compared with the corresponding uncorrected coarse-grid solvers. Additional tests show generalization to unseen PDE parameter values, while comparison with a contemporary coarse-correction architecture shows that RECAST achieves lower error and better long-horizon agreement with the fine-grid reference over 5000-step rollouts. These results demonstrate that the learned correction and reconstruction capabilities of RECAST can enable substantially coarser PDE evolution without the corresponding loss of solution fidelity, providing a proof-of-concept route toward machine-learning acceleration of higher-dimensional numerical simulations across science and engineering.

</details>


### [29] [Why AI Detection Fails for Academic Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11256)
*Jonathan A. Karr,Grigorii Khvatskii,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过对照实验证明，商业AI检测器无法区分轻量AI润色与完整AI草稿，且误报率高（润色文本64-80%被标记），而未经修改的原文仅9-15%被标记。经Undetectable AI"人性化"处理后，检测器的漏检率超过96%。这说明诚实使用AI辅助编辑的作者的受惩戒风险反而高于使用规避工具的作弊者，因此检测分数不应作为学术不端的唯一证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高校普遍使用商业AI检测器来维护学术诚信，但这些检测器无法区分AI编辑辅助（通常符合机构规范）与完整LLM草稿（可能构成不当行为），可能将两者都判定为学术不端。作者旨在量化这一政策失灵的程度。

Method: 作者选取已发表的英文摘要作为研究样本，覆盖四个学科领域，时间跨度2013-2015年（AI前的纯人类文本）与2023-2025年（AI时代的文本）。在tau=0.50的代理人类/AI标签下，对"仅润色摘要"的轻量AI辅助编辑进行检测，使用Pangram和GPTZero两种商业检测器，并测试Undetectable AI的规避效果。同时分析长token密度和学术词汇表（AWL）密度与检测分数的关联。

Result: 轻量AI润色文本被标记的比率高达64-80%；未经修改的2023-2025原文被标记率仅9-15%，其中非STEM领域（人文社科）的误报率显著高于STEM领域（p<0.001）；被标记评分的升高与长token密度和AWL密度相关，与作者意图无关。经Undetectable AI人性化处理后，检测器标记率降至4%以下，漏检率超过96%。

Conclusion: 当前AI检测器的分数不具备足够的可靠性来作为学术不端的独立证据。合规使用AI辅助编辑的作者反而面临比使用规避工具更高的惩戒风险，形成了"诚实者受罚、规避者漏网"的反向激励。检测结果应仅作为辅助参考，不得单独构成学术不端证据。

Abstract: Institutions use commercial AI detectors for academic integrity, yet detectors cannot distinguish AI editing from full LLM drafts and may treat both as misconduct. In a controlled study of published English abstracts (four domains; 2013 to 2015 vs. 2023 to 2025), we quantify this policy failure under proxy human/AI labels at tau=0.50. Light "refine abstract only" edits, a proxy for guideline-compliant AI assistance, are flagged at 64 to 80% (Pangram/GPTZero). Unmodified 2023 to 2025 originals are flagged at 9 to 15%, with non-STEM rates far above STEM (p<0.001); elevated scores track long-token and Academic Word List density, not authorship intent alone. After Undetectable AI humanization, evasion is near-total: fewer than 4% of AI-labeled rewrites remain flagged (post-humanization detection rate <4%; FNR >96%). Honest AI-editing results in a higher sanction risk than humanizer-assisted evasion. Therefore, detector scores should not serve as standalone misconduct evidence.

</details>


### [30] [Drift and Dependence: Layer-wise Information-Theoretic Bounds for Replay-Based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11690)
*Tieliang Gong,Zhongbo Zhang,Wen Wen,Yong-Jin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个逐层信息论框架来分离持续学习中重放(replay)机制的两个耦合效应：有限记忆带来的经验代理与优化轨迹耦合，将泛化差距分解为表示漂移项与优化依赖性项，并给出Wasserstein松弛与SGLD实例化两个可操作细化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习需在不遗忘旧任务的情况下吸收新任务，重放是缓解灾难性遗忘最有效的方法之一，但其泛化行为受两个耦合效应塑造——有限记忆的经验代理和缓冲区、当前数据与最终假设间的共享优化轨迹——而现有分析将它们折叠进单一假设级量，无法清晰分离两者作用。

Method: 构建逐层信息论框架：将期望泛化差距分解为重放诱导表示漂移项与优化依赖性项，后者进一步分解为稳定性、塑性、交互和残差耦合分量。采用Wasserstein松弛处理支持域不匹配下的漂移项，得到深度相关漂移-灵敏度权衡并识别需稳定的内层；通过SGLD实例化优化项，将其化简为轨迹级log-行列式预算，引出曲率感知的梯度对齐统计量。

Result: 受控实验与基准实验验证了预测的记忆缩放规律、内层漏斗结构以及对齐信号与遗忘之间的联系。

Conclusion: 该框架为持续学习中的重放泛化行为提供了逐层可解释的分解，并给出可用于实践中稳定内层和在线监测任务级遗忘的可操作工具。

Abstract: Continual learning must absorb new tasks without erasing old ones, and replay---mixing a small buffer of past examples into current training---is among the most effective remedies for catastrophic forgetting. Yet its generalization behavior is shaped by two coupled effects that existing analyses fold into a single hypothesis-level quantity: finite memory replaces each past distribution with an empirical proxy, and repeated reuse couples the buffer, the current data, and the final hypothesis through a shared optimization trajectory. We develop a layer-wise information-theoretic framework that separates these effects at every depth. Our main result decomposes the expected generalization gap into a replay-induced representation drift and an optimization-dependence term, the latter further resolved into stability, plasticity, interaction, and residual-coupling components. Two refinements make the framework operational. A Wasserstein relaxation of the drift term, valid under support mismatch, yields a depth-dependent drift--sensitivity trade-off whose minimizer identifies which interior layer to stabilize. An SGLD instantiation of the optimization term reduces it to a trajectory-level log-determinant budget, exposing a curvature-aware gradient-alignment statistic that serves as an online diagnostic of task-wise forgetting. Controlled and benchmark experiments confirm the predicted memory scaling, the interior funnel, and the alignment signal's link to forgetting.

</details>


### [31] [Basin: Efficient and Extensible Numerical Optimization in Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11279)
*Johan Larsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Basin 是一个用于 Rust 编程语言的数值优化库，提供统一的接口来表述和求解最小化问题，涵盖广泛的求解器目录并原生支持约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值优化是科学计算中的基础任务（如模型拟合、模拟校准、机器学习训练、工程参数优化），但 Rust 生态中缺乏一个提供统一、一致方式来表述和求解此类问题的成熟库。

Method: Basin 通过提供单一一致的接口来表述优化问题，并集成广泛的求解器目录，同时提供一流的约束支持（first-class support for constraints）。

Result: 论文描述了 Basin 的设计与功能，作为 Rust 数值优化库，其核心成果是提供了一套完整的优化问题表述与求解方案。

Conclusion: Basin 为 Rust 用户提供了一致、全面的数值优化解决方案，覆盖从问题定义到求解的完整流程，并特别重视约束处理能力。

Abstract: Basin is a numerical optimization library for the Rust programming language. Numerical optimization is the task of finding the inputs that minimize a function, and it is a fundamental element across the sciences: fitting a model to data, calibrating a simulation, training a machine learning model, or choosing engineering parameters that minimize cost. Basin gives users a single, consistent way to both state and solve such problems, with a broad catalog of solvers and first-class support for constraints.

</details>


### [32] [MOON: Multi-Objective OrthoNormalized Updates for Multitask Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11749)
*Shiji Zhou,Kunlin Lyu,Lei Zhang,Ruodong Wang,Yifan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOON方法，在矩阵几何（谱-核范数）下进行多目标优化的梯度操作，而非传统欧几里得空间中的参数向量拉平操作，从而获得更陡峭的下降方向，提升多任务学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标优化方法在欧几里得几何下将模型参数拉平成向量进行梯度操作，忽略了现代架构（如Transformer）中参数固有的矩阵结构，导致欧氏空间中的梯度操作不一定产生矩阵几何下的最陡下降方向，限制了优化效率。

Method: 基于矩阵值参数的最陡下降理论，MOON在谱-核范数几何下执行梯度操作，并使用正交归一化处理后的梯度进行参数更新。理论分析在光滑非凸目标下建立了Pareto驻留度量的收敛率：确定性设置为O(T^{-1/2})，随机梯度设置为O(T^{-1/4})。

Result: 在多种基准测试上的实验结果表明，MOON在优化效率和最终多任务性能上均持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 利用矩阵几何特性进行梯度操作能够比欧氏空间中的梯度操作获得更优的下降方向，从而提升多任务学习的优化效率与最终性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has demonstrated significant success in multi-task learning by mitigating task conflicts through gradient manipulation. However, most existing methods flatten model parameters into vectors and perform gradient manipulation under Euclidean geometry, thereby overlooking the matrix structure prevalent in modern architectures such as Transformers. In this paper, we show that gradient manipulation in Euclidean space does not generally yield the steepest descent direction under matrix geometry, potentially limiting optimization efficiency. Drawing from the theory of steepest descent for matrix-valued parameters, we propose MOON (Multi-Objective OrthoNormalized Updates), which performs gradient manipulation under spectral--nuclear norm geometry and uses the orthonormalized manipulated gradient for parameter updates. Theoretically, for smooth non-convex objectives, we establish convergence of the averaged Pareto-stationarity measure at rates of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ in the deterministic setting and $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ under stochastic gradients. Empirical results across various benchmarks show that MOON consistently improves both optimization efficiency and final multi-task performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/KunlinLyu/MOON.

</details>


### [33] [Federated Learning for Distributed CNC Tool Wear Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11281)
*Afsana Khan,Morris Stallmann,Marcin Pietrasik,Charis Kouzinopoulos,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在CNC刀具磨损预测中的应用，通过在模拟客户端上分布刀具轨迹数据来模拟联邦学习场景，结果表明联邦学习性能接近集中式学习且显著优于本地客户端模型，可支持分布式CNC制造环境中的协作式刀具磨损预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刀具磨损预测对CNC加工的质量和工艺可靠性至关重要，但实际工业环境中加工数据呈分布式分布，且各机器、站点或组织之间存在数据共享限制。机器学习方法虽在该任务中展现出潜力，但受限于数据孤岛问题，无法集中训练高质量模型。

Method: 将刀具轨迹数据分布到模拟客户端上，构建联邦学习场景；训练联邦模型，并与集中式参照模型和本地客户端基线模型进行对比评估。

Result: 联邦学习达到了与集中式学习接近的性能水平，同时相较于仅使用本地数据的客户端模型有显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够在分布式CNC制造环境中实现协作式刀具磨损预测，兼顾了数据隐私保护与模型性能，为工业场景落地提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tool wear prediction is an important task in CNC machining, where accurate monitoring of tool condition supports product quality and process reliability. Machine learning methods have shown potential for this task, but their use in industrial environments is limited by the distributed nature of machining data and by restrictions on data sharing between machines, sites, or organizations. Federated learning offers a suitable framework for this setting by enabling collaborative model training without transferring raw operational data. This paper investigates federated learning for CNC tool wear prediction. Tool trajectories are distributed across simulated clients to represent a federated learning scenario. The federated models are compared against centralized references and local client baselines. Results show that federated learning achieves performance close to centralized learning and improves significantly over local client models. These findings indicate that federated learning can support collaborative tool wear prediction in distributed CNC manufacturing environments.

</details>


### [34] [Terminal Symmetry as a Decision Resource: Statewise Refinement for Anytime Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11318)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"终端对称性"作为有序构建任务中的可复用决策资源，通过"运输-细化-验证"(transport-refine-certify)框架将过程证据、终端对应关系和实现状态证据统一为决策信号，并实例化方法\method{}，在CAD装配、小程序、精确填充等任务中显著提升任意时刻AUC，并在GRN OOD场景中取得最低验证器成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多顺序构建任务在完成时呈现精确对称性，但执行过程仍是指向性的、依赖历史的。现有方法未充分利用终端对称性这一结构信号来指导构建过程中的决策。作者希望将这种"终点对称"转化为可复用的决策资源，而非仅作为完成后的校验条件。

Method: 提出"决策-资源"视角下的终端对称性分解：(1)运输——过程证据提供方向性结构，终端对应将结构跨等价结果传递；(2)细化——实现状态证据在转移后更新当前决策相关性；(3)验证——固定验证器认证执行。\method{}实例化为：片段固定的运输过程结构、状态受限过程秩、接受转移后刷新的状态相关残差秩，以及序数秩交汇（top-k集合恰好为两个提议前缀的并集）。该交汇在前缀覆盖下提供完成保证，并达到对应前缀信息模型下的最紧验证器查询上界。

Result: 在CAD装配、Mini-Programs和精确填充三类任务中，状态级刷新分别将任意时刻AUC提升最多6.77、21.75和8.68个百分点。在来自官方GRN OOD场景的1,135个目标移除episodes上，\method{}在三种尺度下均取得最低的平均封顶验证器成本，优于GRN和CDGS风格规划器。状态级信号在聚合和调度器组织之间具有可迁移性。

Conclusion: 终端对称性可以转化为有序构建过程中的可复用决策资源。通过transport-refine-certify的解耦框架及\method{}的具体实例化，证明了利用终端结构信号能够系统性提升构建任务的决策质量和验证效率。两状态构建还预测了转移后动态-静态的严格分离，揭示了该框架的理论深度。

Abstract: Many sequential construction tasks exhibit exact symmetry at completion while their execution remains directed and history-dependent. We develop a decision-resource view of terminal symmetry: process evidence supplies directionality, terminal correspondence transports that structure across equivalent outcomes, realized-state evidence refines its current decision relevance after transitions, and a fixed verifier certifies execution. This decomposition yields transport--refine--certify. \method{} instantiates the principle with an episode-fixed transported process structure, its state-restricted process rank, a state-dependent residual rank refreshed after accepted transitions, and an ordinal rank meet whose top-$k$ set is exactly the union of the two proposal prefixes. The meet provides a completion guarantee under prefix coverage and attains the tight worst-case verifier-query bound under the corresponding prefix information model; a two-state construction predicts a strict post-transition dynamic--static separation. Across CAD assembly, Mini-Programs, and exact-fill packing, statewise refresh improves anytime AUC by up to $6.77$, $21.75$, and $8.68$ points, respectively. On 1,135 target-removal episodes from the official GRN OOD scenes, \method{} attains the lowest mean capped verifier cost at all three scales among the compared GRN and CDGS-style planners. The statewise signal also transfers across aggregation and scheduler organizations. Terminal symmetry thereby becomes a reusable decision resource for directed construction.

</details>


### [35] [Kernel Methods for Learning Operators with Multiple Inputs and Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11831)
*Adrien Weihs,Chunyang Liao,Jingmin Sun,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个通用的基于核的编码器-解码器算子学习框架，支持多输入多输出和多算子学习，具有闭式训练与推断，在五类参数化PDE上达到竞争性或最先进精度，同时比神经算子方法更轻量高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限维对象之间的映射学习是科学机器学习中的核心挑战，现有神经算子方法虽表现好但训练和推断成本较高，需要一种兼具数学可处理性和计算效率的替代方案。

Method: 构建一个将观测、表示、学习和重构分离的通用核基编码器-解码器框架，将其推广到多输入多输出算子学习场景，并提出KernelMO家族——包含算子值核和积空间核两种互补形式的核方法。理论上证明了收敛率由最具挑战性的组成近似问题决定而非总体问题维度。

Result: 在五族参数化偏微分方程上，该方法达到竞争性或最先进的预测精度，同时相比神经算子架构和深度学习模型显著降低了训练和推断成本。

Conclusion: 该框架为算子学习提供了一种数学上可解析、计算上高效的轻量级替代方案，核方法的闭式解特性使其在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算开销。

Abstract: Learning mappings between infinite-dimensional objects is a central challenge in scientific machine learning. We introduce a general kernel-based encoder-decoder framework for operator learning that separates observation, representation, learning, and reconstruction. We develop this framework for multi-input, multi-output operator learning, where operators map between products of potentially distinct function spaces. Our approximation theory shows that, although the number of inputs and outputs can increase, the convergence rate is governed by the most challenging constituent approximation problem rather than the overall problem dimension. The framework leads to practical kernel methods with closed-form training and inference, combining mathematical tractability with computational efficiency. We further specialize the approach to multiple operator learning by introducing KernelMO, a family of kernel methods with complementary operator-valued and product-space formulations. Across five families of parametric partial differential equations, the proposed methods achieve competitive or state-of-the-art predictive accuracy while reducing training and inference costs relative to neural operator architectures and deep learning based models, offering an efficient and lightweight alternative.

</details>


### [36] [Contextual Quality-Diversity Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning for HVAC Control in Tropical Commercial Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11324)
*Tran Le Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种上下文质量-多样性进化强化学习控制器（CQD-ERL），用于热带水冷式制冷机组的监督控制。该控制器不收敛于单一标量化策略，而是维护一个由数据驱动的运行上下文（每日天气和负荷工况聚类）与上下文无关行为描述符联合索引的专业化策略产品档案，由无梯度进化算子和软演员-评论家策略梯度算子共享同一回放缓冲区填充。每个动作在执行前都经过确定性安全屏蔽过滤。控制器在一个代表新加坡商业建筑潜在负荷、冷却塔逼近温度和湿度约束的两层降阶环境中训练，并在全年回测中与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习方法通常收敛于单一标量化策略，难以应对制冷机组控制中多变的天气、负荷和湿度等复杂运行场景，且缺乏可解释性和安全性保证，因此需要一种能够保持策略多样性并兼顾安全约束的控制方案。

Method: 采用质量-多样性（Quality-Diversity）框架，维护一个按运行上下文（每日天气与负荷工况聚类）和行为描述符联合索引的策略产品档案；融合无梯度进化算子与软演员-评论家策略梯度算子，二者共享一个回放缓冲区；在执行前通过确定性安全屏蔽过滤每个动作；在两层降阶环境中训练，并通过全年回测评估。

Result: 控制器在针对新加坡商业建筑的全年回测中表现优于ASHRAE Guideline 36基线，验证了其在热带气候下制冷机组监督控制中的有效性。

Conclusion: CQD-ERL通过将质量-多样性进化策略与强化学习结合，并引入安全屏蔽，能够在复杂多变的制冷系统控制场景中保持策略多样性，同时提升控制性能并保障运行安全，为暖通空调系统的智能控制提供了一种新思路。

Abstract: This paper proposes a contextual quality-diversity evolutionary reinforcement-learning controller, CQD-ERL, for the supervisory control of a tropical, water-cooled chiller plant and its associated air side. Rather than converging to a single scalarised policy, the controller maintains a product archive of specialised policies indexed jointly by a data- driven operating context, a cluster of daily weather and load regime, and a context-invariant behaviour descriptor, filled by a gradient-free evolutionary operator and a soft-actor-critic policy-gradient operator that share one replay buffer. Every action is filtered through a deterministic safety shield before execution. The controller is trained on a two-tier reduced-order environment representing the latent load, cooling-tower approach and humidity constraints of a Singapore commercial building, and is evaluated over a full annual backtest against an ASHRAE Guideline 36 baseline.

</details>


### [37] [Confidence Calibration of Deep Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12100)
*Coby Penso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对高风险应用中的深度学习模型置信度校准问题展开研究，重点解决标签噪声和无监督域适应两大实际场景下的校准挑战，并进一步提出了隐私保护下的保形预测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高安全关键应用中，可靠的置信度估计与预测本身同等重要。然而现有置信度校准方法通常假设可获得干净的验证数据，这在现实中往往不成立——标签噪声和域偏移广泛存在，导致传统校准方法产生误导性的置信度估计。

Method: 论文提出三个核心方法：(1) 针对标签噪声，利用估计的噪声模型重建无噪声的置信度估计，建模噪声标签与干净标签分布之间的关系，并扩展至噪声感知的保形预测(CP)方法以估计干净的符合性分数；(2) 针对无监督域适应(Unsupervised Domain Adaptation)，通过源域性能与域差异来估计目标域准确率，实现无需目标标签的校准；(3) 针对隐私保护场景，提出局部差分隐私(LDP)的保形预测框架，在保证有效不确定性量化（覆盖率）的同时维持隐私保证，并平衡隐私、计算可行性和预测可靠性。

Result: 论文所提方法在标签噪声、域适应和隐私保护三种挑战性场景下均实现了有效的置信度校准和不确定性量化，弥合了校准理论与安全关键实际部署之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该研究为高安全关键应用中的深度学习模型提供了可靠、隐私保护且对噪声鲁棒的预测保障，是校准理论向实际部署应用推进的重要贡献。

Abstract: In high-stakes applications, reliable confidence estimates are as important as the predictions themselves. Confidence calibration ensures that predicted probabilities reflect the likelihood of correctness, making it essential for safe deployment of deep learning models. However, existing methods typically assume access to clean validation data, which is often unrealistic due to label noise and domain shifts. This thesis develops methods for improving calibration under these conditions.
  First, we address calibration under label noise. Standard methods can produce misleading confidence estimates when labels are unreliable. We propose a framework that uses an estimated noise model to reconstruct noise-free confidence estimates by modeling the relationship between noisy and clean label distributions. We extend this approach to Conformal Prediction (CP), which provides set-valued predictions with guaranteed coverage. Our noise-aware CP method estimates clean conformity scores despite label noise, enabling reliable uncertainty quantification. Next, we study calibration in unsupervised domain adaptation, where a model trained on a labeled source domain is adapted to an unlabeled target domain. Since labeled target data are unavailable, we estimate target-domain accuracy from source performance and domain discrepancies, enabling calibration without target labels. We also consider privacy-preserving settings in which user labels and model outputs must remain protected. We propose a locally differentially private conformal prediction framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification while maintaining privacy guarantees and balancing privacy, computational feasibility, and prediction reliability.
  Our results bridge calibration theory and practical deployment in safety-critical applications, contributing to reliable, privacy-preserving, and noise-resilient neural network predictions.

</details>


### [38] [Long-Horizon Forecasting of Complete Financial Statements with Forma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11327)
*Travis L. Johnson,Jiannan Jiang,Soumyabrata Chaudhuri,Yihao Chen,Lauren Falvey,Donal O'Cofaigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProForma-20Q基准与Forma模型，用于1-20个季度前的完整财务报表预测。Forma将报表视为(账户,季度,数值)元组集合，通过掩码元组高斯似然最大化进行训练，在所有对比方法中表现最优，且预测区间覆盖良好，并支持无需重训练的情景分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作未能在一年以上的时间窗口内联合预测完整财务报表，而贴现现金流估值中大部分企业价值恰恰位于这一窗口之外。缺乏可复现的长期完整报表预测基准，阻碍了该领域的研究进展。

Method: Forma是一种Transformer架构，将财务报表读取为(账户、季度、数值)元组的集合，通过最大化掩码元组高斯似然进行训练。发布ProForma-20Q基准，包含78个报表行项目、1-20个季度前瞻预测，以变化空间R²作为评分标准。

Result: Forma在各项对比中均取得最优表现，超越了经典机器学习、链式梯度提升、零样本时序基础模型及前沿大语言模型。其优势随预测期延长而扩大，高斯预测区间从未欠覆盖。预测近乎满足会计恒等式，且可无统计显著精度损失地恢复精确一致性。

Conclusion: 专业化的针对性训练模型胜过通用规模的基座模型，长期财务报表预测可行且有效。Forma的元组接口支持无需重训练的情景分析，固定未来收入路径可锐化其他报表项的预测，为估值场景提供了实用的建模工具。

Abstract: Specialist training beats generalist scale when forecasting financial statements. To our knowledge, no prior work jointly forecasts complete financial statements beyond one year, yet in a discounted-cash-flow valuation most firm value sits past that window. We release ProForma-20Q, a reproducible benchmark for forecasting 78 statement line items 1-20 quarters ahead, for anonymized firms, from past statements and an industry code, scored by change-space $R^2$. On it, Forma, a transformer that reads statements as sets of (account, quarter, value) tuples and maximizes a masked-tuple Gaussian likelihood, beats every competitor we field: classical machine learning, chained gradient boosting, a zero-shot time-series foundation model, and frontier large language models. Its lead widens with horizon, where valuation needs accuracy most, and its Gaussian predictive intervals never under-cover. Forma's forecasts nearly satisfy accounting identities; exact coherence is recoverable at no statistically significant accuracy cost. Its tuple interface supports scenario analysis without retraining, and we show that pinning future revenue paths sharpens the rest of the statement.

</details>

*Bohan Zhang,Anqi Ni,Yixin Wang,Paramveer S. Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT)，一种无需权重更新的解码时训练-free方法，通过计算监督残差并利用dropout诱导的跨前缀传输算子将其迁移到当前提示，在LaMP个性化基准上以不到7%的等效计算量接近SFT性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）是标准的LLM适配方法，但在个性化等场景下，每个作者需要独立的权重访问、优化、存储和重训，成本过高。需要一种无需权重更新的方法来近似SFT的分布效果。

Method: WFT在作者的训练序列上计算监督残差，通过从dropout诱导的跨协方差中估计的跨前缀传输算子，将这些残差传输到当前提示。该算子捕获一个上下文的扰动如何传播到另一个上下文的预测，用logit空间修正替代基于梯度的参数更新。

Result: 在三个LaMP个性化基准上，WFT取得最佳平均性能，在单任务上匹配或超越SFT，并在平均性能上优于其他轻量级基线。在预算控制的比较中，WFT用不到7%的等效计算量接近SFT性能。logit级分析显示WFT与SFT诱导的logit偏移在95%的next-token概率质量上余弦相似度达0.875。

Conclusion: WFT能够在不修改模型权重的情况下捕捉监督适配的分布效果，为个性化等场景提供了一种计算高效的SFT替代方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is a standard approach for adapting LLMs to a target distribution, but in settings such as personalization, where each author requires separate weight access, optimization, storage, and retraining, its costs become prohibitive. We propose Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT), a training-free decoding-time method that approximates the distributional effect of SFT without weight updates. WFT computes supervised residuals on an author's training sequence and transports them to the current prompt through a cross-prefix transport operator estimated from dropout-induced cross-covariance. The operator captures how a perturbation at one context propagates to predictions at another, replacing gradient-based parameter updates with logit-space corrections. On three LaMP personalization benchmarks, WFT achieves the best average performance across datasets, matches or exceeds SFT on individual tasks, and outperforms other lightweight baselines on average. In a budget-controlled comparison, WFT approaches SFT performance using less than 7% of the effective computation. Logit-level analysis shows a cosine similarity of 0.875 between the logit shifts induced by WFT and SFT over 95% of the next-token probability mass, suggesting that WFT captures the distributional effect of supervised adaptation without modifying model weights.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文首次将校准模型（calibration models）应用于真实工业场景——城市水处理厂，用于离线超参数选择，弥补了以往仅在简单仿真环境中评估的不足。作者评估了包括带拉普拉斯距离度量的k近邻模型在内的多种校准模型方法，应用于高维、非平稳的传感器数据进行nexting预测任务，证明这些模型能生成真实的长时域rollout并恢复有意义的超参数灵敏度趋势，同时探讨了模型在年度长数据集上的扩展性、对预训练智能体微调学习率选择的支持以及分布偏移下的稳健性。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在真实系统部署中的关键障碍之一是超参数选择，尤其在缺乏仿真器且在线实验成本高昂的情况下。已有工作提出的基于离线数据训练校准模型以近似环境动力学、实现离线超参数选择的方法，仅在简单仿真环境中被评估过，尚未在真实工业场景中验证，因此需要探索其在实际应用中的可行性。

Method: 在城市水处理厂这一真实工业环境中，基于高维、非平稳的传感器数据，评估多种校准模型方法（包括采用拉普拉斯距离度量的k近邻模型）用于nexting预测任务；进一步分析这些模型在年度长数据集上的表现、对预训练智能体微调学习率选择的支持能力，以及在分布偏移条件下的稳健性。

Result: 校准模型能够生成逼真的长时域rollout，并能恢复有意义的超参数灵敏度趋势；模型可扩展到年度长度的数据集，能够支持预训练智能体的微调学习率选择；但在分布偏移下的稳健性方面仍存在挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作为使用离线动力学模型支持真实环境中的强化学习部署提供了概念验证，证明了校准模型从仿真走向真实工业场景的可行性，同时指出了未来需要解决的实际工程挑战（如分布偏移下的稳健性问题）。

Abstract: A key obstacle to deploying reinforcement learning in real-world systems is hyperparameter selection, particularly when simulators are unavailable and online experimentation is costly. Prior work has proposed calibration models trained on offline data to approximate environment dynamics and enable offline hyperparameter selection, but these methods have so far been evaluated only in simple simulated settings. In this paper, we present the first application of calibration models in a real-world industrial setting: a municipal water treatment plant. We evaluate several calibration model approaches, including a k-nearest neighbors model with a Laplacian distance metric, on high-dimensional, non-stationary sensor data for nexting prediction tasks. Our results show that these models can generate realistic long-horizon rollouts and recover meaningful hyperparameter sensitivity trends. We further examine how calibration models scale to year-long datasets, how they support the selection of fine-tuning learning rates for pre-trained agents, and how robust they are under distribution shift. Overall, our findings provide a proof of concept for using offline dynamics models to support RL deployment in real-world environments, while highlighting important practical challenges for future work.

TL;DR: 本文提出一个市场信息感知的自适应框架，将Chronos-2时序基础模型迁移到日前电价预测任务中。通过构建多源市场信息接口并对齐7天价格上下文与供需、备用、检修等变量，训练仅更新约1%参数的源域门控低秩适配器，无需目标市场标签。在中国四个省级日前现货市场上的留一市场实验表明，该框架相比零样本Chronos-2在MAE/RMSE上平均降低6.24%/7.99%，相比纯Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%，为数据稀缺市场提供了实际可行的迁移路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电价预测对市场参与者至关重要，但由于价格波动大、市场特异性强且与预期系统状态紧密相关，预测难度高。现有监督方法高度依赖特定市场的历史数据，难以用于新兴或数据稀缺的市场，亟需一种能跨市场迁移且不需要目标市场标签的预测方案。

Method: 1) 构建多源市场信息（MSMI）接口，将7天价格上下文与结算前供需、备用、检修、发电机容量和联络线变量对齐；2) 在源域训练门控低秩适配器（gated LoRA），仅更新约1%的模型参数，无需目标市场标签；3) 门控机制根据备用充裕度和运行状态信号对冻结的源适配器进行缩放；4) 采用留一市场（leave-one-market-out）协议评估跨市场迁移能力。

Result: 在中国四个省级日前现货市场上，所提框架相比市场信息感知的零样本Chronos-2，平均MAE/RMSE降低6.24%/7.99%；相比普通Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%。消融实验显示，学习全局标量或随机门初始化无法复现该增益，且相对Source-LoRA的额外提升有限。

Conclusion: 市场结构化输入与状态依赖的门控LoRA组合为数据稀缺的电力市场提供了一条实用的迁移路径。研究结果强调市场信息接口和自适应门控机制在基础模型跨市场迁移中的价值，同时指出了当前改进幅度的局限性，为进一步优化门控设计指明了方向。

Abstract: Electricity price forecasting is crucial for market participants but remains difficult because prices are volatile, market-specific, and closely tied to anticipated system conditions. Existing supervised methods depend largely on market-specific historical data, limiting their use in newly established or data-scarce markets. This paper proposes a market-information-aware adaptation framework that transfers the Chronos-2 time-series foundation model to day-ahead electricity price forecasting. It first constructs a multi-source market information (MSMI) interface aligning 7-day price context with pre-clearing supply--demand, reserve, maintenance, generator-capacity, and intertie variables, and then trains a source-domain gated low-rank adapter (LoRA), updating about $1\%$ of model parameters without target-market labels. The gate scales the frozen source adapter according to reserve-tightness and operating-state signals. A leave-one-market-out protocol is adopted for evaluating cross-market transferability. Experiments on four Chinese provincial day-ahead spot markets show that the proposed framework reduces the average MAE/RMSE by $6.24\%/7.99\%$ relative to market-information-aware zero-shot Chronos-2 and by $3.05\%/3.52\%$ relative to vanilla Source-LoRA. Experiments show that the gain is not reproduced by a learned global scalar or by random gate initialization, while the additional improvement over Source-LoRA is limited. These results suggest that market-structured inputs and state-dependent gated LoRA can provide a practical transfer path for data-scarce electricity markets.

</details>


### [42] [Lifecycle-Optimal Tokenization: Vocabulary Size as a Deployment-Regime-Dependent Infrastructure Parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11361)
*Rima Mittal,Ankit Gubrani,Satyanarayana Kakollu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出词表大小（vocabulary size）不应被固定为常数，而应根据部署场景选择成本最优值。通过形式化生命周期总成本函数，并在A10G和A100两类GPU上实验，发现推理最优词表随服务批量大小在32k到524k之间变化（16倍差异），而生命周期最优词表与训练最优词表在生产部署中差异可达16倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词表大小是LLM基础设施中的基础设计选择，但通常依据惯例在训练时固定，而非基于部署分析。现有实践缺乏对词表大小在推理阶段成本影响的系统量化，导致部署成本可能远高于最优值。

Method: 形式化总部署成本为生命周期成本函数 C_lifecycle(V) = C_train(V) + λ·C_infer(V, B)，其中λ为推理量、B为服务批量大小。在两类GPU（A10G，ridge≈117 FLOP/byte；A100，ridge≈183 FLOP/byte）上跨内存受限到计算受限区间进行受控实验，测量不同词表大小下的训练与推理成本及质量（BPB）。

Result: (1) 推理最优词表随服务批量从32k（B=1）变化到524k（B≥64），变化达16倍，由V×d未嵌入矩阵读取的摊销驱动；(2) 在1.3-2.3B模型规模下质量最优词表为65k，确认词表偏好随规模变化；(3) 生命周期最优词表与训练最优词表在生产部署中差异可达16倍。最优范围内质量近似不变（BPB差异<2%）。

Conclusion: 词表大小本质上是一个纯系统优化问题，在测量范围内对质量无损失。提供可操作的容量规划建议：设备端部署（B=1）应使用V≈32k；数据中心服务（B≥64，λ≥10）应使用V≈131k-262k。

Abstract: Tokenizer vocabulary size is a foundational design choice in large language model (LLM) infrastructure, yet it is typically fixed at training time based on convention rather than deployment analysis. We show that the cost-optimal vocabulary is not a constant but a function of the serving regime. We formalize total deployment cost as $C_{lifecycle}(V) = C_{train}(V) + λ\cdot C_{infer}(V, B)$, where $λ$ is inference volume and $B$ is the serving batch size. Through controlled experiments on two GPU families spanning the memory-bound to compute-bound regimes (A10G, ridge $\approx$ 117 FLOP/byte; A100, ridge $\approx$ 183 FLOP/byte), we demonstrate: (1) the inference-optimal vocabulary shifts 16x with serving batch, from 32k at $B=1$ to 524k at $B=64+$, driven by amortization of the $V \times d$ unembedding matrix read; (2) at 1.3-2.3B model scale, quality (bits per byte, BPB) is optimized at $V=65$k, confirming scale-dependent vocabulary preference; (3) the lifecycle-optimal vocabulary diverges from training-optimal by up to 16x for production deployments. Quality is approximately invariant across the optimal range ($<$2% BPB spread), making vocabulary a pure systems optimization with no quality penalty in the measured range. Our results provide actionable capacity planning guidance: on-device deployments ($B=1$) should use $V \approx 32$k; datacenter serving ($B \geq 64$, $λ\geq 10$) should use $V \approx 131$-262k.

</details>


### [43] [PAIR: Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting for Adaptive Rollout Allocation in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11368)
*Pixel Nomand,Elena Voss,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAIR（Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting）方法，解决了RLVR中自适应预算分配器的统计失配问题。作者发现未裁剪的留一法组相对分数梯度本质上是二阶U统计量（成对比度），而非独立的点贡献之和。PAIR将短轨迹前缀视为顶点、梯形成对项视为边构建对比图，通过前缀预测器预测正确性和剩余token成本，用凸优化设计在期望后缀token预算下选择正延续概率，并对每个边按其联合包含概率的逆加权。理论上在条件独立同策略rollout和未裁剪未标准化目标下，该估计器对完整候选对梯度是design-unbiased的。实验在Qwen3-1.7B/4B上相比最强点式分配器分别提升平均准确率+1.2和+1.4，同时比完整组GRPO节省51%和52%的生成token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR的大部分计算开销来自生成大量长推理轨迹组。现有分配器基于点式的难度或效用概念为prompt、rollout或token分配预算，但作者识别出一个统计失配：未裁剪的留一法组相对分数梯度并非独立的点贡献之和，而是rollout对之间的二阶U统计量。完成一个rollout会揭示其与所有其他已完成的rollout之间的对比，而自适应端点选择会改变哪些成对项可被观测，因此需要一种能够感知成对结构并校正包含偏差的分配策略。

Method: PAIR方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 将短rollout前缀建模为顶点，成对梯度项建模为对比图中的边；(2) 一个仅基于前缀的预测器用于估计正确性和剩余token成本；(3) 一个凸优化设计层，在期望后缀token预算约束下选择正延续概率。每个由已完成顶点诱导的边按其记录的对数联合包含概率的逆进行加权。在条件独立同策略rollout和未裁剪未标准化目标下，该估计器对完整候选对梯度是design-unbiased的。

Result: 在计算匹配的RLVR运行中（Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3-4B），PAIR相比最强的点式分配器，平均准确率分别提升+1.2和+1.4，同时相比完整组GRPO减少了51%和52%的生成token。冻结种群估计器审计证实未加权的自适应选择存在偏差，而成对包含校正能够在匹配后缀成本下恢复完整对目标。

Conclusion: 本文通过揭示RLVR梯度估计中固有的二阶U统计量结构，证明了点式预算分配在统计上不匹配组相对目标。PAIR通过成对感知的包含重加权，在理论上有design-unbiased保证，在实践中显著降低生成token成本的同时提升准确率，为自适应推理预算分配提供了一个更合理的统计学框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) spends most of its compute generating groups of long reasoning trajectories. Recent allocators reduce this cost by assigning budgets to prompts, rollouts, or tokens according to a pointwise notion of difficulty or utility. We identify a statistical mismatch: the unclipped leave-one-out group-relative score gradient is not a sum of independent point contributions, but a second-order U-statistic over pairs of rollouts. Completing one rollout therefore reveals contrast with every other completed rollout, and adaptive endpoint selection changes which pair terms are observable. We introduce PAIR (Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting), which treats short rollout prefixes as vertices and pair-gradient terms as edges of a contrast graph. A prefix-only predictor estimates correctness and remaining token cost; a convex design chooses positive continuation probabilities under an expected suffix-token budget; and each edge induced by completed vertices is inverse-weighted by its logged joint inclusion probability. Under conditionally independent on-policy rollouts and an unclipped, unstandardized objective, the resulting estimator is design-unbiased for the complete candidate-pair gradient. Across compute-matched RLVR runs on Qwen3-1.7B/4B, PAIR improves average accuracy by +1.2 and +1.4 over the strongest pointwise allocator while using 51% and 52% fewer generated tokens than full-group GRPO. A frozen-population estimator audit confirms that unweighted adaptive selection is biased, whereas pair-inclusion correction recovers the complete-pair target at matched suffix cost.

</details>


### [44] [Towards an approach to multivariate outlier detection for District Heating System data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11375)
*Rajko Turudija,Dušan Stojiljković,Milan Zdravković,Marko Ignjatović*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测试了多种多变量离群点检测方法（Z-score、马氏距离、PCA、孤立森林、Hotelling T-squared检验）用于区域供热系统变电站的传输热能数据异常检测，并结合外部环境温度作为辅助变量。经专家评估后，采用PCA、孤立森林和Hotelling三种方法的集成方法（三者一致同意的离群点）作为最终方案，旨在发现异常运行以降低天然气消耗和CO2排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在识别区域供热系统的非正常运行状态，通过异常检测发现节能机会，进而减少中央供热厂的天然气消耗及CO2排放。同时需考虑特定领域情境（如零传输能量时间点不代表离网运行），需要多变量方法同时纳入环境温度因素。

Method: 对某变电站的传输热能数据（结合外部环境温度）应用五种离群点检测方法：单变量Z-score（作为基准）、马氏距离、主成分分析（PCA）、孤立森林（Isolation Forest）和Hotelling T-squared检验。采用集成方法作为最终方案，即仅选取三种有效方法（PCA、孤立森林、Hotelling）一致检测出的离群点。

Result: 实验结果表明PCA、Isolation Forest和Hotelling T-squared方法能提供相关有效的离群点检测结果，而Z-score和马氏距离方法效果不佳。经领域专家讨论确认后，采用集合三种有效方法的一致检测结果作为最终离群点判定。

Conclusion: 基于多方法一致性的集成离群点检测方法适用于区域供热系统的异常运行识别，可有效辅助发现节能潜力，为降低天然气消耗和碳排放提供决策支持。

Abstract: In this paper, we test different methods for multivariate detection of outliers in the data of transmitted heat energy in the selected substation of local District Heating System, by also considering outside ambient temperature, namely Z-score (univariate, as a benchmark), Mahalanobis distances, Principal Component Analysis (PCA), Isolation Forest and Hotelling's T-squared test. The overall research aims at uncovering irregular plant operation, with a wider objective of identifying the opportunities for reducing the consumption of gas in central heating plants as well as the CO2 emission. The proposed approach considers specific domain circumstances, such as irrelevance of zero transmit-ted energy timepoints as indication of off-grid plant. The outcomes of the different methods are discussed with domain experts. It was concluded that PCA, Isolation Forest and Hotelling method provide relevant results. Finally, we adopt the ensemble method (selection based on the agreement of all three methods on the detected outliers) as the final approach.

</details>


### [45] [Reoptimization Algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11383)
*Zhen Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究上下文赌博机背包问题（Contextual Bandits with Knapsack）的新算法。该问题中，有有限类型的顾客、产品和资源，每个产品由固定的资源组合制成，资源容量有限。决策者需要将每个到达的顾客分配给一组产品中的一个。每次分配会产生随机奖励，该奖励是顾客和产品特征的未知线性函数加上噪声项。目标是联合学习平均奖励函数并做出在线分配，以最小化相对已知奖励函数的最优策略的期望收入损失。作者提出了一种自然简单的UCB算法扩展并应用重新优化技术，实现了O((ln T)^3/T)的平均遗憾，显著优于文献中动态定价问题基于重新优化的O(1/√T)界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上下文赌博机背包问题中，需要在在线学习未知奖励函数的同时考虑资源容量的约束。现有的基于重新优化技术的算法只能达到O(1/√T)的遗憾界，作者希望提出更高效的算法以获得更优的遗憾保证。

Method: 提出一种简单自然的UCB（Upper-Confidence-Bound）算法扩展，并结合重新优化（re-optimization）技术。该算法在每个时间步利用置信上界估计奖励函数，并通过重新优化求解当前约束条件下的最优分配策略。

Result: 该算法实现了O((ln T)^3/T)的平均遗憾，其中T为时间跨度。这一结果显著优于文献中基于重新优化的动态定价问题所达到的O(1/√T)遗憾界。

Conclusion: 通过结合重新优化技术，UCB算法家族能够在该问题上取得远优于现有方法的遗憾界，证明了重新优化在上下文赌博机背包问题中的有效性。

Abstract: We study new algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack. In these problems, there are finitely many types of customers, products, and resources. Each product is made from a fixed combination of resources, and resources have finite capacity. A decision maker must assign each arriving customer one out of a set of multiple possible products. Every assignment of a customer to a product will generate a random reward, which equals an unknown linear function of customer and product features, plus a noise term. The objective is to jointly learn the mean reward function, and to make online assignments to minimize the expected revenue loss relative to an optimal policy that knows the reward function. We propose a natural and simple extension of the Upper-Confidence-Bound (UCB) family of algorithms and apply re-optimization techniques. We show that by taking advantage of re-optimization, our algorithm achieves an average regret of $O(\frac{(\ln T)^3}{T})$ where $T$ is the horizon length. Our bound significantly reduces the $O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}})$ bound in the literature for closely related dynamic-pricing problems that are based on re-optimization.

</details>


### [46] [Mechanism Design for Generative Engines: From Exploitation toward Win-Win Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11390)
*Chen Xu,Zitian Guo,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成式搜索引擎生态中由引用激励机制引发的"引用战争"问题，提出了一种基于可验证内容奖励的平台-创作者机制VCR，通过同时惩罚可疑重写和奖励可核实事实内容来对齐创作者激励与答案可信度，在三个基准上平均超过最强基线12.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式引擎将引用作为分配注意力与价值的关键机制，导致内容提供者有动机优化生成引擎引用（GEO），而平台需保证答案质量与可信归属，双方产生策略性紧张。现有防御机制会被适应性的GEO攻击所规避，攻击者通过引用导向的重写降低文档质量并引入无依据的主张，因此需要一种能够从根本上对齐双方激励机制的新型防御方案。

Method: 将供应商-平台交互建模为带部分监控的重复Stackelberg博弈；通过局部最优响应分析识别引用竞争趋于惰性静止结果的条件；在此基础上提出VCR（Verifiable-Content Rewards）机制，该机制不仅惩罚可疑重写，还对呈现可核查事实内容的改写给予信用奖励，从而将创作者激励与答案可信度对齐。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，VCR在净防御效用分数上一致达到最大值，平均超过最强基线12.1个百分点，并且在经验等价准则下实现双赢结果。

Conclusion: VCR机制能够有效缓解生成引擎生态中的引用战争问题，通过将奖励从纯粹的引用获取转向可验证的内容质量，实现了平台防御能力与创作者利益的协同优化。

Abstract: Generative engines are reshaping the web ecosystem by making citations a key mechanism for allocating attention, attribution, and downstream value. This creates a strategic tension: content providers are incentivized to optimize for model citation, while platforms must preserve answer quality and trustworthy attribution. We show that this tension can escalate into citation wars. In repeated simulations, state-of-the-art generative engine optimization (GEO) attacks adapt to conventional defenses by producing citation-seeking rewrites that degrade document quality and introduce unsupported claims. To study this problem, we formulate the supplier--platform interaction as a repeated Stackelberg game with partial monitoring. A local best-response analysis identifies when citation competition approaches an inert stationary outcome. Motivated by this finding, we propose a platform--creator mechanism called VCR based on verifiable-content rewards. Rather than only penalizing suspicious rewrites, the platform also credits rewrites that surface checkable factual substance, aligning creator incentives with answer trustworthiness. Experiments on three benchmarks show that VCR consistently achieves the largest Net defense-utility score, outperforming the strongest baseline by an average of 12.1 percentage points, and produces a win--win outcome under our empirical equivalence criterion.

</details>


### [47] [Unmasking Toxic Mimicry in Medical Offline Reinforcement Learning for ICU Sepsis Management via Counterfactual Clinical Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11410)
*Hangqi Ren,Junyi Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实临床审计（CCA）框架，用于检测离线强化学习智能体在ICU治疗决策中的"毒性模仿"失败模式。通过对MIMIC-III数据库中训练的两个智能体（MedDT和HCT-RL）进行基于脓毒症存活运动指南的生理扰动压力测试，发现MedDT在乳酸水平升高时反而降低血管升压药剂量，违背复苏指南，而HCT-RL能保持生理一致性响应，揭示了统计拟合与临床安全性之间的系统性错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离线RL评估指标（MSE和FQE）仅评估行为模仿程度，无法检测"毒性模仿"——即智能体复制如舒适护理过渡期间停止治疗等有害模式。医疗RL需要能评估临床安全性的新评估标准，而非仅仅关注统计拟合。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Clinical Audit (CCA)框架，通过对生理状态进行锚定在SSC指南上的反事实扰动来压力测试RL智能体。审计对象为MedDT（Medical Decision Transformer）和HCT-RL（Historical Causal Transformer），后者使用因果动作屏蔽、倾向性重要性加权和保守Q学习。

Result: CCA揭示MedDT在乳酸水平升高时反而降低血管升压药剂量，与复苏指南相矛盾；而HCT-RL维持了生理一致的反应。这表明仅凭统计拟合好的模型可能在临床上不安全。

Conclusion: 反事实审计应作为医疗强化学习必要的评估标准，以弥补传统统计评估指标的不足，确保RL智能体的临床安全性。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) offers considerable promise for optimizing ICU treatment decisions, yet standard evaluation metrics Mean Squared Error (MSE) and Fitted Q-Evaluation (FQE) assess only behavioral imitation and cannot detect Toxic Mimicry, a failure mode in which agents replicate harmful patterns such as treatment withdrawal during comfort-care transitions. Using the MIMIC-III database, we propose the Counterfactual Clinical Audit (CCA) framework, which stress-tests RL agents through physiological perturbations anchored in Surviving Sepsis Campaign (SSC) guidelines. We audit a Medical Decision Transformer (MedDT) and a Historical Causal Transformer (HCT-RL), the latter employing Causal Action Shielding, propensity-based importance weighting, and Conservative Q-Learning. CCA reveals that MedDT paradoxically reduces vasopressor dosage as lactate escalates, contradicting resuscitation guidelines, while HCT-RL maintains physiologically consistent responses. These findings expose a systemic misalignment between statistical fit and clinical safety, supporting counterfactual audits as a necessary evaluation standard for medical RL.

</details>


### [48] [Diffusion-Based Data-Driven Assortment Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11419)
*Junyi Liao,Xiaohui Jiang,Zhengwei Tong,Ethan X. Fang,Vahid Tarokh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于引导式离散扩散的模型无关组合优化框架，用于解决收益管理中的品类组合优化问题。该方法将品类组合表示为二进制向量，通过学习反向扩散过程进行随机搜索，并引入收益奖励引导机制来平衡探索与利用，从而避免显式组合枚举，在模型误设和高维场景下仍能恢复近似最优解，并能生成多样化且高性能的品类组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的品类组合优化依赖参数化选择模型（如MNL及其变体），虽然计算上易于处理，但对模型误设敏感，且难以捕捉复杂的客户行为。需要一种模型无关、鲁棒且可扩展的优化方法。

Method: 提出模型无关的引导式离散扩散框架：将品类组合编码为二进制向量，通过学习反向扩散过程进行随机搜索以避免显式组合枚举；引入奖励引导机制，利用期望收益估计来偏置局部转移，从而平衡探索与利用。

Result: 实验表明该方法能持续识别高质量品类组合，在模型误设下保持鲁棒性，在高维设置中常能恢复近似最优解；且扩散的生成特性使其能产出多样化且高性能的组合，超越了单一确定性解。

Conclusion: 生成式建模有望成为数据驱动决策中组合优化的可扩展且鲁棒的新范式，为品类组合优化提供了超越传统参数化选择模型的替代路径。

Abstract: Assortment optimization is a fundamental problem in revenue management, typically addressed using parametric choice models such as the multinomial logit (MNL) and its variants. While these models enable tractable formulations, their performance is sensitive to model misspecification and often struggles to capture complex customer behavior. In this paper, we propose a model-agnostic framework for assortment optimization based on guided discrete diffusion. We represent assortments as binary vectors and perform stochastic search via a learned reverse diffusion process, avoiding explicit combinatorial enumeration. To incorporate decision objectives, we introduce a reward-guided mechanism that biases local transitions using estimates of expected revenue. This allows the method to effectively balance exploration and exploitation during generation. Empirically, we show that the proposed approach consistently identifies high-quality assortments and remains robust under model misspecification, often recovering near-optimal solutions in high-dimensional settings. Moreover, the generative nature of diffusion enables the production of diverse high-performing assortments, offering flexibility beyond a single deterministic solution. These results highlight the potential of generative modeling as a scalable and robust paradigm for combinatorial optimization in data-driven decision-making.

</details>


### [49] [Analysis of Federated Aggregation under Model Poisoning and Backdoor Attacks: A Reconstructed Cross-Dataset and Cross-Architecture Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11423)
*Soumya Mazumdar,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文重建了一个包含500个单元的联邦聚合方法评估矩阵，涵盖五种聚合方法、五个数据集、五种架构和四种条件（clean、sign-flipping、Gaussian、BadNets），并通过对执行日志的审计、指标实现审计和脚手架代码审计，发现Trimmed Mean在干净条件下表现最佳，Krum在恶意攻击条件下表现最佳，同时识别了BadNets指标实现中的触发逻辑问题（TTLR而非传统ASR）和FedPARETO脚手架中潜在的模型-聚合不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习聚合方法的稳健比较需要同时考虑预测性能、威胁定义、指标语义和执行来源（provenance），而现有比较往往缺乏对这些维度的联合严格审视。本研究旨在通过重构一个完整评估矩阵，并审计执行日志、指标实现和代码脚手架，确保比较的可靠性和可解释性。

Method: 作者重建了一个5×5×5的评估矩阵（五种聚合方法×五个数据集×五种架构），在四种条件下记录结果。对454个原始运行和36个修复/重跑执行的成功日志进行识别，10个SVHN单元仅有摘要性来源。进一步对BadNets指标实现进行审计，验证所有测试输入在目标标签计数前均被触发；对FedPARETO脚手架进行代码审计以检查聚合权重与更新之间的潜在不一致。

Result: Trimmed Mean在干净条件下取得最高宏平均准确率（76.02%）和最低平均任务内排名（1.70）；Krum在符号翻转和高斯配置下均取得最高准确率。当分析限制在21个全部方法-条件组合均有原始成功日志的任务对时，相对排名保持不变。BadNets审计发现该指标实为触发目标标签率（TTLR）而非传统排除目标的攻击成功率；FedPARETO脚手架存在聚合权重应用于被单独污染的更新而预测摘要对应未污染本地模型的潜在差异。

Conclusion: 由于规范矩阵中每个单元仅含单一标识种子，且攻击和配置的确切谱系不完整，研究结果应被视为在记录配置范围内的描述性比较，而非统计估计或关于稳健性的普适性声明。

Abstract: Robust comparisons of federated aggregation methods require joint consideration of predictive performance, threat definitions, metric semantics, and execution provenance. A 500-cell seed-1 evaluation matrix was reconstructed across five aggregation methods, five datasets, five architectures, and four recorded conditions: clean, sign-flipping, Gaussian, and BadNets. Successful execution logs were identified for 454 original runs and 36 repaired or rerun executions, whereas 10 clean SVHN cells were supported by summary-only provenance. Trimmed Mean achieved the highest clean macro-mean accuracy (76.02%) and the lowest mean within-task rank (1.70). Krum attained the highest recorded accuracy under both sign-flipping and Gaussian configurations. These relative rankings remained unchanged when analysis was restricted to 21 task pairs for which original successful logs were available for every method-condition combination. Audit of the supplied BadNets metric implementation established that every test input is triggered prior to target-label counting; consequently, the retained metric represents Triggered Target-Label Rate (TTLR) rather than a conventional target-excluding attack success rate. An audit of the supplied FedPARETO scaffold further identified a pathway in which predictive summaries may characterize an uncorrupted local model while the aggregation weight is applied to a separately corrupted update, introducing a potential discrepancy between reported predictive outcomes and the updates used for aggregation. The canonical matrix contains a single identified seed for each cell, and exact attack and configuration lineage is incomplete. Accordingly, the findings should be interpreted as descriptive comparisons within the recorded configurations and not as statistical estimates or universal claims regarding robustness.

</details>


### [50] [Click2Poly: A VLM for vector mapping buildings and walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11424)
*Nicolas Girard,Jawher Ben Abdallah,Arno Gobbin,Liuyun Duan,Sacha Lepretre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Click2Poly 是一个基于 Florence-2 视觉语言模型的人工智能辅助工具，以 QGIS 插件形式实现人机协同的建筑物和墙体矢量地图编辑，通过用户点击直接编辑矢量图层，加速制图标准下的手动质量控制与修正流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑物和墙体的精确矢量测绘对地理空间应用至关重要，但传统方法费时费力。虽然深度学习已改进自动提取，但由于制图标准要求，仍需人工进行质量控制和复杂案例修正，这一手动步骤成为效率瓶颈。

Method: 扩展 Florence-2 视觉语言模型，使模型能够响应用户点击并直接编辑建筑物或墙体矢量图层。通过人机协同交互方式（human-in-the-loop），以 QGIS 插件形式部署到真实生产环境中。

Result: 在真实生产环境中，Click2Poly 作为 QGIS 插件显著加快了建筑物和墙体矢量图层的手动编辑速度。

Conclusion: Click2Poly 展示了一种将视觉语言模型与人机协同交互结合的可行方案，有效加速了制图级矢量编辑的手动工作流，为地理空间数据生产提供了高效的 AI 辅助工具。

Abstract: Accurate vector mapping of buildings and walls is critical for geospatial applications but remains a labor-intensive process. While recent deep learning methods have improved automatic extraction, in order to meet cartographic standards they always require a human to perform quality control and fix complex cases in the extraction. We present Click2Poly, a human-in-the-loop AI assistant designed to speed up this manual step. Extending the Florence-2 Vision Language Model (VLM), Click2Poly responds to user clicks by editing the building or wall vector layer directly. Implemented as a QGIS plugin, Click2Poly speeds up the manual editing of building and wall vector layers in a real-world production environment.

</details>


### [51] [Three Tokens Force Exponential Feature Rank in Nonnegative Kernel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11427)
*Vicente Opazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明核注意力（kernel attention）与全注意力（full attention）在表达能力上存在指数级差距：在Min-IP布尔输入任务上，单个归一化非负核注意力头要处理所有三token序列且误差低于1/2，需要2^Ω(m)个特征；而稠密softmax注意力仅需m维分数和常数温度即可解决。此外，针对确定性多头多层草图模型，还证明了与独立答案数量和字母表大小相关的转录本下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核注意力通过将序列压缩为固定维度草图来近似全注意力，但人们对这种压缩在表达能力上的代价缺乏理论刻画。作者希望从计算复杂度/表达能力的角度，严格对比全注意力与核注意力的能力边界。

Method: 1) 在Min-IP布尔输入上构造任务，证明rank-one归一化核注意力可完全解决长度≤2的序列；2) 通过对抗性/信息论论证，证明任何非负核注意力头若要在所有三token序列上误差<1/2，必须使用2^Ω(m)个特征；3) 证明稠密softmax以m维分数和常数温度即可解决同一任务；4) 推广到位置相关token映射和因果最终查询；5) 对确定性多头多层草图模型（cross-token通道为有限字母表）建立转录本下界，与独立答案线性相关、与字母表大小对数相关。

Result: 核注意力在第一个包含两个竞争候选的上下文长度上就出现指数级表达差距；三token场景下核注意力需要指数级特征，而softmax仅需线性维度；下界随上下文长度增长趋近精确的2^m特征实现；确定性草图模型的转录本下界随答案数线性增长、随字母表大小对数增长。

Conclusion: 全注意力与核注意力之间的表达差距是指数级的，且这种差距在最基本的长度三序列上即已显现。核注意力因其固定维度草图机制，在需要区分竞争候选的任务上存在根本性的表达能力瓶颈，而稠密softmax注意力在相同场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: Full attention exposes every token pair, whereas kernel attention compresses a sequence into a fixed-dimensional sketch. We show that this distinction becomes exponential at the first context length containing two competing candidates. On Min-IP over Boolean inputs, rank-one normalized kernel attention solves every sequence of length at most two exactly. In contrast, any single normalized nonnegative kernel-attention head that succeeds on all three-token sequences with error strictly below $1/2$ requires $2^{Ω(m)}$ features, even with arbitrary finite-dimensional tokenwise values and an arbitrary query-dependent affine readout. Dense softmax solves the same task with $m$-dimensional scores and constant temperature. The conclusion survives position-dependent token maps and a causal final query. As context length grows, the lower bound approaches the exact $2^m$-feature realization. Separately, for deterministic multihead, multilayer sketch models whose cross-token channels have finite alphabets, we prove a transcript lower bound linear in the number of independent answers and logarithmic in their alphabet size.

</details>


### [52] [Adversarial Resilience of Poisson-Process Submodular Maximization over Matroids: From Robust Offline Optimization to Full-Bandit Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12134)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在一般拟阵约束下的非负子模最大化问题，提出了Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process (SGS-Poisson)算法的对抗鲁棒性定理。该算法在不修改泊松强度、单元素交换规则或恶意丢弃步骤的前提下，对非单调目标保留1/e的极限近似因子，对单调目标保留1-1/e的极限近似因子，且仅在控制值预言机误差为ξ时损失O(kξ)。该结果进一步通过offline-to-online归约，推导出针对一般拟阵约束子模奖励的全带CMAB算法，实现精确的极限近似-遗憾因子1/e和1-1/e，遗憾界为Õ(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子模最大化在机器学习、推荐系统等众多领域有广泛应用，而现实场景中算法通常只能访问带有噪声或有偏差（受控）的价值预言机而非精确值。已有工作缺乏在一般拟阵约束下、对任意受控预言机具有鲁棒性的离线算法分析，同时也缺少将此类结果有效转化为在线全带CMAB算法的理论保证。

Method: 作者提出了SGS-Poisson（Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process）算法，这是一种结合了泊松过程强度、单元素交换规则以及恶意（spiteful）丢弃步骤的贪心交换框架。关键贡献是建立了对抗鲁棒性（adversarial resilience）定理：在任意受控价值预言机⁠f̂满足|⁠f̂(S)-f(S)|≤ξ的条件下，算法不改变结构参数即可保持极限近似因子。随后利用offline-to-online归约将该离线结果转化为全带CMAB（组合多臂老虎机）在线学习算法。

Result: 在任意受控预言机下，SGS-Poisson对非单调目标实现期望值至少(1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)的近似保证，对单调目标实现至少(1-1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)，调用预言的次数为Õ(nk²ε⁻²)。由此推导出的全带CMAB算法在处理一般拟阵约束子模奖励时，获得精确的极限近似-遗憾因子1/e与1-1/e，遗憾界为Õ(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})。

Conclusion: 本文首次在一般拟阵约束下证明了SGS-Poisson算法对受控价值预言机的对抗鲁棒性，无需修改泊松强度、交换规则或丢弃步骤即可保持最优极限近似因子。该结果打通了从鲁棒离线算法到高效在线CMAB学习的完整通道，为含噪声环境下的一般拟阵子模优化提供了理论基石，遗憾上界的n^{1/5}因子也优于若干现有工作的典型速率。

Abstract: We study nonnegative submodular maximization subject to a general matroid when the offline algorithm is given an arbitrary controlled value oracle. Our main result is an adversarial resilience theorem for the Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process (SGS-Poisson): without modifying its Poisson intensity, single-element exchange rule, or spiteful drop step, the algorithm retains limiting approximation factors $1/e$ for non-monotone objectives and $1-1/e$ for monotone objectives. More precisely, under every controlled oracle $\widehat f$ satisfying $|\widehat f(S)-f(S)|\le ξ$ for every set $S$, our implementation returns a feasible set with expected value at least $(1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$ and $(1-1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$, respectively, using $\widetilde O(nk^2\varepsilon^{-2})$ oracle calls. As a consequence, the offline-to-online reduction yields full-bandit CMAB algorithms for general matroid-constrained submodular rewards with exact limiting approximation-regret factors $1/e$ and $1-1/e$ and $\widetilde O(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})$ regret.

</details>


### [53] [AutoGrable: What Is a Good Graph for a Table?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11431)
*Tamara Cucumides,Floris Geerts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoGrable 提出了一种无需训练图模型的准则，用于从表格和关系数据库自动构造图结构。其核心思想是：在最小表到图抽象中，每行是一个节点，GNN（受限于 1-WL）看到的仅是行划分为颜色细化类的结果。构造好坏取决于该划分是否分离不同标签的行且不拆分同标签的行。AutoGrable 通过标签对齐风险分数来筛选候选列子集，无需物化图或训练 GNN，即可贪心地搜索子集空间，得到适用于单表和主外键模式的图结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图学习预设了一张图的存在，但表格和关系数据库本身没有图结构。应用 GNN 需要人工或启发式决定哪些实体作为节点、如何连接以及通过哪些关系，这是繁琐且昂贵的过程。现有方法要么依赖手工设计、要么依赖模式启发式、要么需要对每个候选图训练模型后挑最优，计算开销大。本文希望给出一个无需训练图模型的准则，能够廉价地判断哪个图表构造对预测任务有利。

Method: 本文提出了一个理论准则：在最小表到图抽象中，每行是一个节点，受限于 1-WL 的消息传递 GNN 实际上只感知行集合上的一个划分（按颜色细化类）。好的构造应满足：划分能够分离标签不同的行，且不拆分标签相同的行。AutoGrable 将此准则转化为构造过程：对于入射构造（incidence constructions），划分由所选列决定，因此建图转化为选列问题。用一个标签对齐风险分数来衡量候选列子集——即在其划分块上最优常量预测器的留出风险，并施加占据项（occupancy term）惩罚块过于稀疏的情况。该分数不需要物化图，也不需要训练 GNN，因此可以贪心且廉价地搜索列子集空间，得到适用于单表和主外键模式的 grable 结果。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 在候选图空间上，该分数能丢弃大量候选图而保留最优者；(2) 在受控任务中，AutoGrable 能恢复生成标签的列；(3) 在固定预测器下的真实任务中，AutoGrable 优于固定、随机和任务感知的构造方法；(4) 它是所有对比方法中唯一可以在图毫无帮助时主动拒绝构建图的方法。

Conclusion: AutoGrable 表明，表格上的图构造可以绕过训练模型，仅依据行划分与标签对齐程度这一准则来高效完成。它既能廉价搜索候选构造空间并保留最优结果，又能灵活应对单表和主外键模式，甚至在无图有利时拒绝建图，为表数据上的 GNN 应用提供了一种轻量、可解释且有效的自动化构造方案。

Abstract: Graph learning presupposes a graph, and tables and relational databases do not come with one. Applying a GNN to them requires deciding which entities become nodes, which of them to connect, and through which relations---a decision made by hand, by schema heuristics, or by training a model on every candidate graph and keeping the best. We give a criterion that requires no trained graph model. In the minimal table-to-graph abstraction each row is a node, so a message-passing GNN, bounded by 1-WL, sees a construction only as a partition of the rows into colour-refinement classes: a construction is good for a task when that partition separates rows with different labels and does not split rows that share one. AutoGrable turns this criterion into a construction procedure. For incidence constructions the partition is fixed by the selected columns, so building a graph reduces to choosing them, and we score a candidate subset by a label-alignment risk: the held-out risk of the best predictor constant on its blocks, penalised by an occupancy term measuring how thinly the blocks are populated. The score materialises no graph and trains no GNN, so AutoGrable can search the space of subsets greedily and cheaply, and returns the resulting grable for single tables and for foreign-key schemas alike.
