Method: 提出Redistribution-based Cost Inference (RCI)框架：首先将稀疏的终止反馈转化为稠密逐步骤成本（通过回报分解/return decomposition），然后在增强的数据集上训练约束离线策略。理论上证明了回报等价再分配（return-equivalent redistribution）保持可行策略集和CMDP中的最优Lagrangian不变，即变换在理论上是无损的，同时在实践中使成本评论家学习更具良好条件性。

Result: 在高速公路驾驶和机器人操作实验中，RCI相比稀疏和基于分类器的基线大幅降低了违规率，并且对异构数据集组成和标签噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RCI框架成功地将时间信用分配问题转化为稠密成本推理问题，在理论上无损地将稀疏终止反馈转换为可用的稠密成本，并在实践中提升了安全约束学习的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Safe offline RL typically assumes access to dense per-step cost annotations, but in practice supervisors provide only trajectory-level stop-feedback: a binary signal at the first unsafe transition, with no per-step attribution. We frame this as a temporal credit assignment problem and propose the Redistribution-based Cost Inference (RCI) framework, which converts sparse stop-feedback into dense per-step costs via return decomposition, then trains a constrained offline policy on the augmented dataset. We show that return-equivalent redistribution preserves the feasible policy set and the optimal Lagrangian in a CMDP, establishing that the transformation is lossless in theory while yielding better-conditioned cost critic learning in practice. Experiments on highway driving and robotic manipulation demonstrate substantially lower violation rates than sparse and classifier-based baselines, with robustness to heterogeneous dataset compositions and label noise.

</details>


### [121] [AI4AI at Test-Time: Strong-to-Weak Capability Transfer via Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12307)
*Cheng Qian,Wenting Zhao,Liangwei Yang,Heng Wang,Jielin Qiu,Heng Ji,Silvio Savarese,Huan Wang,Shelby Heinecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"强到弱脚手架"(strong-to-weak scaffolding)方法，探索在不更新参数的情况下，通过测试时的推理框架(inference-time harness)将强模型的能力迁移到弱模型。在四个心智理论(Theory-of-Mind)基准上，该方法使目标模型平均性能从0.49提升至0.91，性能近乎翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的知识蒸馏方法通常需要在训练时更新小模型参数（如teacher forcing、on-policy distillation等）。作者质疑：能力迁移是否可以在测试时而非训练时发生？即能否用强模型构建推理时的辅助框架来提升弱模型的表现，而无需任何参数更新。

Method: 核心方法是"强到弱脚手架"：一个更强的"建造者"(builder)模型利用5%的验证数据，通过多轮迭代来精炼其推理框架(harness)，最终在完整测试集上评估该框架。框架通过将不稳定的模型推理卸载到确定性代码、基准特定的路由(routing)和严格的答案格式约束来实现能力提升。

Result: 测试时能力迁移极为有效，目标模型平均性能从0.49提升到0.91，接近翻倍。消融分析显示增益主要来自将不稳定推理卸载到确定性代码、基准特定路由和严格的答案格式执行，而非鼓励目标模型更广泛推理或采样。建造者模型的推理努力单调提升框架质量，平台效应相对于建造者模型自身能力较为温和，弱目标模型获益最大。

Conclusion: 推理时框架设计是传统训练时蒸馏的重要补充，使得强模型能够在不需要重训练的情况下将认知结构迁移到弱模型，为知识迁移提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recent work on distillation transfers the capabilities of large models to smaller ones often by updating the latter's parameters, through teacher forcing, on-policy distillation, and related training-time methods. In this paper, we ask whether such transfer can instead occur at test time. We study strong-to-weak scaffolding: whether a stronger builder model can construct inference-time harnesses that help a weaker target model solve tasks more reliably without any parameter updates. Using four representative Theory-of-Mind benchmarks, each builder model uses 5% of the data as a validation set to iteratively refine its harness over multiple rounds, after which the finalized harness is evaluated on the full test set. Empirically, this form of test-time capability transfer is highly effective, nearly doubling average target-model performance from 0.49 to 0.91. Our analysis shows that the gains come primarily from offloading unstable model reasoning into deterministic code, benchmark-specific routing, and strict answer-format enforcement, rather than from encouraging the target model to reason more extensively or sample more broadly. We further find that builder-model reasoning effort improves harness quality monotonically, platform effects are modest relative to the builder model's own capability, and weaker target models receive the largest gains. These results suggest that inference-time harness design is an important complement to conventional training-time distillation, enabling strong models to transfer cognitive structure to weaker models without retraining.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Fine-Tuning Generative Models for Extreme Events via CVaR-Penalized Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11544)
*Thejani Gamage,Hyemin Gu,Zhizhen Zhang,Ziyu Chen,Markos Katsoulakis,Luc Rey-Bellet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出CVaR-GPA算法，一种鲁棒且尾部无关的生成模型微调方法，通过学习重尾分布来捕捉极端事件，无需目标分布尾部特性的先验知识或估计。该方法通过CVaR惩罚项恢复尾部欠采样区域过早消失的速度场，实现对更重尾目标的传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准生成模型（如GAN、扩散模型）的Lipschitz输运映射会保留轻尾源分布的尾部行为，导致无法学习重尾分布和捕捉极端事件。此外，传统方法需要对目标分布的尾部特征有先验知识或估计，而这在实际中往往不可得。

Method: CVaR-GPA是Lipschitz正则化KL散度与CVaR散度项惩罚的Wasserstein梯度流。Lipschitz正则化KL散度在最小假设下实现鲁棒学习，CVaR惩罚恢复尾部欠采样区域过早消失的速度。作者从Rockafellar-Uryasev表示中推导CVaR的第一变分次梯度，以在经验测度上定义该流（经典密度公式在此失效）。算法无需访问预训练模型架构，通过动能截止准则自适应当前时间步长。

Result: 在合成各向同性/各向异性Student-t目标分布、Neal漏斗分布以及真实高维Fama-French 25投资组合数据集上，CVaR-GPA在重尾目标的全局准确性和尾部准确性上均显著优于预训练基线。

Conclusion: CVaR-GPA提供了一种无需尾部先验知识、不依赖模型架构的通用微调框架，能够有效将轻尾源分布的生成模型引导至重尾目标分布，显著提升极端事件的捕捉能力。其有界非Lipschitz速度场是对标准Lipschitz输运映射的重要理论突破。

Abstract: We propose CVaR-penalized Generative Particle Algorithm (CVaR-GPA), a robust, tail-agnostic algorithm for fine-tuning generative models to learn heavy-tailed distributions and capture extreme events, requiring no prior knowledge or estimation of the target's tail characteristics. The method is the Wasserstein gradient flow of the Lipschitz-regularized Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence penalized by a Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) discrepancy term: the Lipschitz-regularized KL divergence enables robust learning under minimal assumptions on the target distribution, while the CVaR penalty restores the velocity that otherwise vanishes prematurely in the under-sampled tails. The penalized flow admits a bounded but non-Lipschitz velocity field. This departs from the Lipschitz transport maps of standard generators, which preserve the tail behavior of a light-tailed source, and enables transport toward heavier-tailed targets. To define this flow on empirical measures, we derive the first-variation subgradients of CVaR from its Rockafellar-Uryasev representation, valid precisely where the classical density-based formula fails. The particle algorithm CVaR-GPA fine-tunes the output samples of any pre-trained model, without access to its architecture, and runs on an adaptive time horizon set by a kinetic-energy stopping criterion rather than a preset depth. On synthetic isotropic and anisotropic Student-$t$ target distributions, Neal's funnel distribution, and the real-world high-dimensional Fama-French 25 portfolio dataset, CVaR-GPA dramatically improves global and tail accuracy on heavy-tailed targets over the pre-trained baseline.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Predict-then-Optimize Framework for Public Transport Line Redesign under Fluctuating Traffic Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11405)
*Zihao Guo,Andrea Araldo,Faycal Touzout,Mounim El-Yacoubi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于"预测-然后-优化"范式的公共交通网络主动重构方法，在高交通波动下使用NSGA-III算法重新设计PT线路，并通过Jaccard边重叠约束保证运营可行性。在Mandl基准和北京大规模网络上，该方法相比传统的跳站策略能显著提升用户出行效率（北京网络平均出行时间改善达25.8%）并降低成本，同时保持85%以上的线路重叠和完整的OD连通性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公共交通线路设计假设名义交通条件，实际运营中常出现大范围或反复的交通波动，导致性能大幅下降。现有自适应机制（如跳站）多为被动反应式干预，在剧烈波动下效果不足，增量调整难以奏效，因此需要探索更深层次的结构性重新设计来保持系统性能。

Method: 采用预测-然后-优化范式：首先利用扩散卷积循环神经网络（DCRNN）在真实数据上训练产生交通预测，并构建预测误差的统计模型以评估不准确性；然后基于预测结果使用非支配排序遗传算法III（NSGA-III）对PT线路进行主动重构；通过强制原始与重构网络之间保持高Jaccard边重叠来确保运营可行性和避免过度结构变更。

Result: 在Mandl基准和北京大规模网络上的计算结果表明：控制性PT线路重构在高交通波动下带来显著的用户侧性能提升和运营成本降低，同时限制拓扑变化。北京网络在高变异性下平均出行时间改善最高达25.8%，同时保持超过85%的线路重叠。与破坏许多OD对连通性的跳站基线相比，该方法保留了完整的OD连通性。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持从静态规划转向持续性和自适应的PT网络设计范式。相比被动反应的跳站等传统适应机制，主动的、预测驱动的结构性线路重构在应对大范围或反复交通波动时更为有效，能够同时兼顾用户性能、运营成本和网络拓扑稳定性。

Abstract: Public Transport (PT) lines are traditionally designed to optimize performance under nominal traffic conditions. In practice, operating conditions frequently deviate from nominal ones, leading to substantial performance deterioration. Existing adaptation mechanisms typically rely on reactive interventions, such as stop-skipping, which are insufficient under large or recurrent traffic fluctuations. In such contexts, incremental adjustments may not suffice. This paper evaluates the potential of deeper structural redesigns to preserve performance.
  We propose a method to proactively redesign appropriate parts of PT networks under high traffic fluctuations that would otherwise deteriorate operator and user performance. We adopt a predict-then-optimize paradigm in which PT lines are reconfigured based on traffic forecasts using the Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm III (NSGA-III).
  To ensure operational feasibility and avoid excessive structural changes, we enforce high Jaccard edge overlap between the original and redesigned networks. To assess prediction inaccuracies, we construct a statistical model of errors from a well-established deep learning predictor, the Diffusion Convolutional Recurrent Neural Network, trained on real-world data.
  Computational results on Mandl's benchmark and the large-scale Beijing network show that controlled PT line redesign yields substantial user-centric performance gains and operational cost reductions under high traffic fluctuations while limiting topological changes. On the Beijing network under high variability, average travel time improves by up to 25.8% while preserving over 85% line overlap. Unlike stop-skipping baselines, which break connectivity for many OD pairs, our redesign preserves full OD connectivity. These results support a shift from static planning toward continuous and adaptive PT network design.

</details>


### [124] [Convex-Concave Interpolation and Application of PEP to Bilinear-Coupled Saddle-Point Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11412)
*Valery Krivchenko,Alexander Gasnikov,Dmitry Kovalev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为复合鞍点问题的性能估计问题（PEP）提出了插值条件，涵盖凸-凹函数、双线性函数及具有双线性耦合的复合函数，并构建了用于一阶方法的PEP框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEP方法能够精确刻画算法的最坏情况性能，但其可处理的表述形式依赖于必要且充分的插值条件。现有插值条件主要针对凸函数，缺乏针对鞍点问题（如凸-凹函数、双线性函数等）的插值条件，限制了PEP在一阶方法分析中的应用。

Method: 作者为凸-凹函数、双线性函数以及具有双线性耦合的复合函数推导了必要且充分的插值条件，并基于这些条件构建了复合鞍点问题的一阶方法的PEP表述。

Result: 成功给出了三类函数的插值条件，并据此建立了复合鞍点问题上一阶方法的PEP框架。

Conclusion: 该工作将PEP方法扩展至鞍点问题领域，使得可以精确计算复合鞍点问题上一阶算法的最坏情况性能。

Abstract: The Performance estimation problem (PEP) approach reformulates finding the exact worst-case performance of an algorithm as the solution to an optimization problem. Tractable formulation of the problem requires necessary and sufficient interpolation conditions. We present the interpolation conditions for convex--concave functions, bilinear functions, and composite functions with bilinear coupling. We also construct PEP for first-order methods for composite saddle-point problem.

</details>


### [125] [A Wolfe-Type Spectral Conjugate Gradient Method for Nonsmooth Convex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11689)
*Jauny,Gourav Kumar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Moreau-Yosida正则化的Wolfe型谱共轭梯度方法，用于非光滑凸优化问题。该方法将受保护的谱参数与Dai-Kou型共轭参数相结合，并采用与正则化产生的不精确梯度兼容的Wolfe型线搜索。作者证明了全局收敛性，并在额外强凸假设下证明了R-线性收敛速率。在标准非光滑优化基准和大规模问题上的实验表明，该方法整体上具有竞争力，在大多数测试问题上媲美或优于现有方法，同时也指出了当前实现的一些具体局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非光滑凸优化问题广泛出现在机器学习、信号处理等领域，而经典共轭梯度法通常针对光滑问题设计，难以直接处理非光滑目标函数。Moreau-Yosida正则化提供了一条将非光滑问题转化为光滑问题的途径，但其产生的不精确梯度与传统线搜索不兼容，需要专门设计的方法来高效求解此类问题。

Method: 将Moreau-Yosida正则化引入非光滑凸优化，提出Wolfe型谱共轭梯度方法：(1) 结合受保护的谱参数（safeguarded spectral parameter）与Dai-Kou型共轭参数；(2) 设计适合正则化产生的不精确梯度的Wolfe型线搜索；(3) 在强凸假设下证明全局收敛性和R-线性收敛速率。

Result: 在标准非光滑优化基准和大规模问题上，与多种现有共轭梯度和束方法（bundle-type methods）比较，所提方法在大多数测试问题上表现具有竞争力，匹配或优于现有方法。同时识别并讨论了当前实现中存在的若干具体局限。

Conclusion: 所提出的Wolfe型谱共轭梯度方法为大规模非光滑凸优化提供了一种高效且具有理论保证的求解方案，在多数问题上优于或媲美现有方法。尽管存在一些实现层面的局限性，整体上该方法具有较强的实用价值和发展潜力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a Wolfe-type spectral conjugate gradient method for nonsmooth convex optimization, built on the Moreau-Yosida regularization of the objective function. The method combines a safeguarded spectral parameter with a Dai-Kou-type conjugate parameter, and uses a Wolfe-type line search compatible with the inexact gradients that the regularization produces. We establish global convergence of the method, together with an R-linear convergence rate under an additional strong-convexity assumption. The method is evaluated on standard nonsmooth optimization benchmarks and on large-scale problems, and compared against several existing conjugate gradient and bundle-type methods. The results show that the proposed method performs competitively overall, matching or outperforming existing methods on most problems tested, while a few specific limitations of the current implementation are also identified and discussed

</details>


### [126] [Tight Nonasymptotic Local Convergence of Sinkhorn-Knopp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11760)
*Wenzhi Gao,Zhaonan Qu,Yinyu Ye,Madeleine Odell*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了矩阵缩放问题的Sinkhorn-Knopp（SK）算法，首次给出了与其渐近Jacobian分析相匹配的非渐近局部线性收敛分析，证明了在连通性条件下SK是双随机矩阵缩放的多项式时间算法，并在此基础上提出了加速变体，将稠密矩阵上的一阶矩阵缩放算法复杂度从O(n^{7/3}/ε^{2/3})改进到O(n^{9/4}/√ε)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sinkhorn-Knopp算法及其变体在矩阵缩放问题中有着广泛的全局收敛性研究，但其局部线性收敛行为尚未被充分理解。现有文献中对SK的局部收敛分析主要依赖渐近Jacobian-based论证，缺乏非渐近的定量分析，这限制了对其实际运行复杂度的精确刻画。

Method: 本文通过提供SK算法的首个非渐近局部收敛分析来填补这一理论空白。该分析在特定连通性条件下建立了与渐近Jacobian论点相匹配的收敛速率，从而证明SK是双随机矩阵缩放的多项式时间算法。作者还利用所开发的分析工具揭示了SK的局部次优性，并提出了加速变体来改进算法性能。

Result: 论文取得了三项主要成果：（1）首次提供了SK的非渐近局部线性收敛分析，其速率与既有渐近结果一致；（2）证明了在连通性条件下SK是多项式时间算法，并展示了SK的局部次优性； （3）对于稠密矩阵，将一阶矩阵缩放算法的复杂度从O(n^{7/3}/ε^{2/3})改善为O(n^{9/4}/√ε)。

Conclusion: 该工作显著推进了对Sinkhorn-Knopp算法局部收敛行为的理论理解，弥合了渐近分析与非渐近分析之间的差距，并通过加速变体和改进的复杂度上界为矩阵缩放问题提供了更高效的算法路径。

Abstract: We revisit the Sinkhorn-Knopp (SK) algorithm for the matrix scaling problem. Despite extensive literature on the global convergence of SK and its variants, its local linear convergence behavior remains less understood. We address this gap by providing the first nonasymptotic local analysis of SK that matches the rate obtained from existing asymptotic Jacobian-based arguments. We show that under certain connectivity conditions, SK is a polynomial-time algorithm for doubly stochastic matrix scaling. With the developed tools, we showcase the local suboptimality of SK and provide accelerated variants. Finally, for dense matrices, we improve the complexity of existing first-order matrix scaling algorithms from $O(\tfrac{n^{7/3}}{\varepsilon^{2/3}})$ to $O(\tfrac{n^{9/4}}{\sqrt{\varepsilon}})$.

</details>


### [127] [Data-Driven optimal control via Koopman operators and Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11808)
*Guuyuan Chen,Gaosheng Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的稳定流形（DD-SM）方法，将Koopman算子表示学习与Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman（HJB）方程的几何稳定流形方法相结合，实现从原始轨迹数据中端到端的最优反馈控制综合，无需系统动力学的先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HJB方程求解最优反馈控制通常需要精确的系统动力学模型和数值求解，但实际应用中系统动力学往往未知或难以精确建模。作者旨在从纯数据出发，在没有先验动力学知识的情况下合成最优反馈控制器，并同时保证理论上的误差界和闭环稳定性。

Method: 方法核心包括：(1) 在统一对称子空间分解（SSD）与扩展动态模态分解（EDMD）框架下构造增广控制系统，联合逼近漂移场、控制矩阵及其空间导数；(2) 推导不变和非不变字典空间下的概率有限样本误差界，获得HJB方程的可证明精确近似特征系统；(3) 利用Lyapunov-Perron算子与ODE扰动分析，证明数据驱动稳定流形随训练数据增长具有单调递减的半全局误差；(4) 建立闭环指数稳定性并量化最优性间隙；(5) 开发自适应数据生成与深度神经逼近的高效算法流程，控制信号输出在1毫秒以内。

Result: 理论结果包括：数据驱动稳定流形误差单调递减、闭环指数稳定性、最优性间隙可量化且可通过提高模型精度收紧。实验在改进的van der Pol振荡器上验证了方法的有效性，且控制信号可在1毫秒内输出。

Conclusion: DD-SM方法为无模型最优反馈控制提供了一种兼具理论保证（误差界、稳定性）与实际计算效率（毫秒级控制输出）的端到端数据驱动框架，展示了Koopman算子理论与几何方法在数据驱动控制中的有效融合。

Abstract: This paper presents a data-driven stable manifold (DD-SM) method, which integrates Koopman operator representation learning with the geometric stable manifold approach to Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equations, enabling end-to-end optimal feedback control synthesis from raw trajectory data without prior knowledge of system dynamics.
  We construct an augmented control system under a unified symmetric subspace decomposition (SSD) and extended dynamic mode decomposition (EDMD) framework for joint approximation of the drift field, control matrix and their spatial derivatives, and derive probabilistic finite-sample error bounds for invariant and non-invariant dictionary spaces to yield a provably accurate approximate characteristic system of HJB equation. Via Lyapunov-Perron operator and ODE perturbation analysis, we prove the data-driven stable manifold achieves monotonically decreasing semi-global error with growing training data. We further establish closed-loop exponential stability and quantify the optimality gap, both tightenable by refining model accuracy. An efficient algorithm pipeline with adaptive data generation and deep neural approximation is developed, outputting control signals within 1 millisecond. Experiments on a modified van der Pol oscillator verify the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [128] [Joint Identifiability and Conditioning in Finite-Horizon Continuous-Time Inverse LQR with Unknown Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11932)
*Meiling Yu,Yuan-Hua Ni,Lei Jiang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域连续时间逆LQR问题，从闭环状态-输入轨迹中同时恢复未知的系统矩阵(A,B)和二次代价权重矩阵。有限时域引起的时变最优增益作为内生激励机制，使得联合恢复成为可能。作者通过三个可计算的条件指标量化该机制，建立联合可辨识性条件，并开发了条件感知的采样数据重建方法和凸半定规划识别方案，同时提供非渐近扰动界和一致性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优控制（IOC）旨在从专家行为观测中推断agent的底层代价函数。现有工作大多假设无限时域或系统矩阵已知，限制了实际应用。本文要解决的关键问题是：在有限时域、系统矩阵和代价均未知的场景下，如何仅从闭环状态-轨迹观测中联合辨识(A,B)和代价权重矩阵，而非仅恢复行为等价的替代解。

Method: 1) 提出三个可计算的条件指标：状态丰富度、增益变化丰富度、结构化代价算子的单射性，用于量化有限时域内生激励机制。2) 基于这些指标建立联合可辨识性条件，确保恢复真实系统矩阵和代价权重而非行为等价替代。3) 开发条件感知的采样数据重建方法，从噪声测量中重建增益K(·)和闭环动态矩阵Ac(·)，以闭式方式恢复(A,B)，并通过凸半定规划识别二次权重。4) 在亚高斯观测噪声下推导非渐近扰动界和一致性。

Result: 建立了有限时域逆LQR的联合可辨识性理论条件，保证了真实参数而非行为等价解的唯一恢复；提出了完整的三阶段重建方法（增益重建→系统矩阵闭式恢复→代价权重凸半定规划识别）；给出了依赖条件指标的非渐近扰动界，并证明了亚高斯噪声下方法的一致性。

Conclusion: 有限时域内生激励（时变最优增益）为逆LQR联合辨识提供了结构机制，三个条件指标可量化该机制的有效性，据此设计的重建方法在理论上保证恢复真实参数，数值实验验证了理论结果及指标的诊断价值。

Abstract: Inverse Optimal Control (IOC) aims to infer the underlying cost functional of an agent from observations of its expert behavior. This paper studies the finite-horizon continuous-time inverse LQR problem from closed-loop state--input trajectories, where both the system matrices and the quadratic cost are unknown. The finite horizon induces a time-varying optimal gain, and this endogenous excitation serves as the structural mechanism that makes joint recovery possible. We quantify this mechanism through three computable conditioning indices, which measure state richness, gain-variation richness, and injectivity of a structured cost operator. Using these indices, we establish joint identifiability conditions for the inverse problem considered here. Crucially, these conditions guarantee recovery of the ground-truth system matrices $(A,B)$ and the true cost weighting matrices, rather than merely a behaviorally equivalent surrogate. We also develop a conditioning-aware sampled-data reconstruction method that reconstructs the gain $K(\cdot)$ and the closed-loop dynamics matrix $A_c(\cdot)$ from noisy measurements, recovers $(A,B)$ in closed form, and identifies the quadratic weights through a convex semidefinite program. We further establish the non-asymptotic perturbation bounds and the consistency of the full reconstruction method under sub-Gaussian observation noise, with explicit dependence on the same conditioning indices. Numerical experiments support the theory and illustrate the diagnostic value of the conditioning indices.

</details>


### [129] [Adaptive Bregman Proximal Stochastic Gradient with a Stabilized Barzilai--Borwein Step Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12009)
*Chenhan Jin,Shengze Xu,Binghui Xie,Kaiwen Zhou,Fan Jia,James Cheng,Tieyong Zeng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出Ada-BPSG，一种无需线搜索的Bregman近端随机梯度方法，通过SAGA梯度表与稳定的Barzilai-Borwein步长估计相结合，实现方差缩减复合优化，在凸、非凸及相对二次增长设定下均获得收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPSG方法在目标函数几何不满足欧氏光滑性时具有优势，但性能高度依赖步长选择：原始随机曲率估计波动剧烈，而线搜索则引入重复近端计算开销。因此需要一个无需线搜索且对初始步长不敏感的BPSG变体。

Method: 将SAGA梯度表与稳定化Barzilai-Borwein候选步长相结合；通过中位数（mediant）聚合增量割线信息，使接近奇异值的局部比率获得较小权重；显式安全护栏将曲率估计转化为保证收敛的有界步长序列。理论分析基于相对光滑性与分量级方差控制。

Result: 凸目标下证明O(n/K)遍历收敛率；相对二次增长条件下证明重启线性收敛率；非凸设定下提供Bregman近端残差的O(1/K)界。在逻辑回归和稀疏非负矩阵分解实验中，Ada-BPSG在达到较低目标值的同时，对初始步长的敏感性显著低于标准方差缩减基线，且无需线搜索。

Conclusion: Ada-BPSG通过稳定化BB步长与SAGA方差缩减的耦合，有效解决了BPSG对步长敏感的关键缺陷，在无需线搜索的前提下实现了理论收敛保证和实际性能的兼顾，适用于几何不满足欧氏光滑性的复合优化问题。

Abstract: Bregman proximal stochastic gradient (BPSG) methods bring variance-reduced composite optimization to objectives whose geometry is poorly captured by Euclidean smoothness. Their performance, however, remains sensitive to the step size: raw stochastic curvature estimates can fluctuate sharply, whereas line searches add repeated proximal evaluations. We introduce Ada-BPSG, a line-search-free BPSG method that couples the SAGA gradient table with a stabilized Barzilai--Borwein (BB) candidate. A mediant aggregates incremental secant information so that nearly singular local ratios receive little weight, and an explicit safeguard translates the resulting curvature estimate into the bounded step-size sequence required for convergence. This design yields a direct analytical chain from relative smoothness and component-wise variance control to convergence in finite-dimensional normed spaces. We prove an $O(n/K)$ ergodic rate for convex objectives, a restarted linear rate under relative quadratic growth, and an $O(1/K)$ bound for a Bregman proximal residual in the nonconvex setting. On logistic regression and sparse nonnegative matrix factorization, Ada-BPSG combines low objective values with substantially less sensitivity to the initial step size than standard variance-reduced baselines, while avoiding line search.

</details>


### [130] [Direct Acceleration of Stochastic Root-Finding Without Variance Reduction and Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12043)
*TaeHo Yoon,Nicolas Loizou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出dual-anchor加速机制，将其扩展到随机根查找问题，避免了anchor-based方法在随机设置下因误差累积导致的退化问题，实现了O(ε⁻³)和强单调情形下Õ(ε⁻²)的复杂度，且不需要方差缩减或批处理增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性根查找问题的加速方法（如anchor-based/Halpern型方法）已达成最优收敛率，但这些方法在随机设置下会因误差累积而失效，通常需要增大批大小或引入方差缩减技术。本文旨在寻找能在随机设置下自然推广的加速机制，避免上述复杂处理。

Method: 本文提出dual-anchor（对偶锚点）加速机制，该机制与anchor-based方法不同，在随机设置下不会产生误差累积问题。算法采用与迭代步数无关的固定批大小，无需方差缩减或双重循环递归正则化，适用于期望意义下满足cocoercivity（余强制）的随机根查找问题和满足square-nonexpansivity（平方非扩张性）的不动点问题。

Result: 对于随机根查找（或不动点）问题，在余强制（或平方非扩张）期望条件下，算法以固定批大小实现O(ε⁻³)复杂度。对于强单调算子，同一算法达到更优的Õ(ε⁻²)复杂度，在ε依赖性上接近理论下界。

Conclusion: 研究表明dual-anchor加速机制是随机加速优化中一种有前景的新框架，它克服了anchor-based方法在随机设置下的缺陷，以更简化的算法结构实现了接近最优的复杂度。

Abstract: Acceleration for deterministic root-finding problems has been extensively studied in recent years; specifically, the anchor-based, or Halpern-type methods achieve optimal convergence rates with respect to the operator norm. However, acceleration via these methods does not directly carry over to stochastic setting due to accumulation of errors, unless one enforces diminishing variance via increasing batch sizes or variance reduction techniques. In this work, we show that another class of acceleration, namely the dual-anchor mechanism, extends to the stochastic setting without such error accumulation, in contrast to anchor-based algorithms. Consequently, we cleanly achieve $O(ε^{-3})$ complexity with iteration-independent batch size, without any variance reduction or double-loop recursive regularization, for stochastic root-finding (resp. fixed-point) problems with cocoercivity (resp. square-nonexpansivity) in expectation. For strongly monotone operators, the same algorithm attains a sharper $\widetilde{O} (ε^{-2})$ complexity, nearly matching the lower bound in terms of $ε$-dependence.

</details>


### [131] [A Multiscale Primal-Dual Interior-Point Relaxation Method for Large-Scale Optimal Transport Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12060)
*Shengyu Sun,Rui-Jin Zhang,Ruoyu Diao,Yu-Hong Dai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种多尺度原始-对偶内点松弛方法（MSIPRM）来解决大规模最优传输问题，通过多尺度外层框架在渐进精细化层级上构建稀疏子问题，并以内点松弛法（IPRM）作为内层求解器，可处理包含数万亿传输变量的实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模最优传输问题涉及大量传输变量，导致内存和计算成本过高，现有方法难以应对超大规模实例。

Method: 采用多尺度外层框架，在渐进更细的层级上构建标准OT问题；每层通过基于上一层级解支撑初始化的自适应细化活动集求解，生成一系列紧密相关的稀疏子问题；内层使用不需要严格内点迭代的IPRM，可利用上一子问题解作为热启动；通过简化Schur补系统高效求解牛顿方向；并开发了基于IPRM近似解的有效支撑识别策略。

Result: 建立了Schur补矩阵的条件数估计，分析了算法的全局和局部收敛性质；数值实验表明MSIPRM在计算效率和可扩展性方面优于现有求解器，能够处理全公式包含数万亿传输变量的实例。

Conclusion: MSIPRM通过多尺度稀疏化与内点松弛相结合，有效解决了大规模最优传输问题中的内存和计算瓶颈，展现出显著的可扩展性和竞争力。

Abstract: Large-scale optimal transport (OT) problems involve a vast number of transport variables, leading to prohibitive memory and computational costs. To address these challenges, we propose a multiscale primal-dual interior-point relaxation method (MSIPRM). The multiscale outer framework constructs a hierarchy of standard OT problems at progressively finer levels. At each level, the OT problem is solved over a sequence of adaptively refined active sets initialized based on the solution support at the previous level. This yields a sequence of closely related sparse subproblems, thereby substantially reducing memory requirements. The primal-dual interior-point relaxation method (IPRM) serves as the inner solver for each sparse subproblem. Since IPRM does not require strictly interior iterates, it can readily use the solution of the previous subproblem as a warm start. To efficiently obtain the Newton direction, we solve a reduced Schur complement system derived from the normal equations. Furthermore, we develop an effective support-identification strategy based on the approximate solutions obtained by IPRM. We establish condition number estimates for the Schur complement matrices and analyze the global and local convergence properties of the algorithm. Numerical experiments on large-scale test problems demonstrate the computational efficiency and scalability of MSIPRM and show that it compares favorably with existing solvers. In particular, MSIPRM can handle instances whose full formulations contain trillions of transport variables.

</details>


### [132] [Competitive Analysis of Stock-based Thresholds via Prophet Inequalities in Continuous Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12073)
*Jiashuo Jiang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间下K单位在线资源分配问题，其中顾客到达服从非齐次泊松过程，价值分布随时间变化。作者聚焦于仅依赖剩余库存的阈值策略（stock-based threshold policies），并针对多单位先知基准（selecting best K realized values in hindsight）进行竞争比分析。他们通过类型覆盖对偶（type-covering dual）将问题转化为PoisOPTRe_K，并证明敌意到达具有cutoff和late-filling结构，从而得到精确的四参数公式（两阈值）和有限嵌套区间表示（一般阈值）。计算得到K=2时两阈值竞争比为0.6269，K=3时三阈值竞争比为0.6816。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管最优动态策略通常同时依赖于剩余库存和剩余时间，但实践中更简单、更吸引人的策略是仅依赖于库存数量的阈值策略。本文旨在严格评估这类简化的stock-based阈值策略在多单位先知基准下能提供怎样的竞争比保证，即在忽视日历时间的情况下，简单的阈值策略能实现多强的先知不等式。

Method: (1) 通过类型覆盖对偶（type-covering dual）重构问题。(2) 将对偶分析中的无限维非凸优化问题（因为对手可选择时变的到达率和价值过程，而策略性能对随机库存轨迹呈非线性依赖）约化为泊松优化问题PoisOPTRe_K。(3) 证明最坏情形解的结构性质：敌意到达具有cutoff和late-filling结构，从而对两阈值情形得到精确的四参数公式，对一般阈值情形得到有限嵌套区间表示。这些约化使竞争比可直接计算。

Result: 对于K=2，两阈值策略的竞争比为0.6269；对于K=3，三阈值策略的竞争比为0.6816。结果表明简单的stock-based阈值策略尽管完全忽略日历时间，仍能获得强先知不等式保证。

Conclusion: 本文展示了仅基于库存的简单阈值策略在连续时间非齐次泊松到达和时变价值分布下，能够实现强大的先知不等式保证。通过新的竞争分析框架解决无限维非凸对偶难题，并将问题精确约化为可计算的有限参数优化问题，从理论上证明了stock-based阈值策略的实用性。

Abstract: We study a continuous-time $K$-unit online resource allocation problem with nonhomogeneous Poisson arrivals and time-varying valuation distributions. While the optimal dynamic policy generally depends on both the remaining inventory and the time left in the horizon, we focus on a simpler and practically appealing class of stock-based threshold policies, whose limited number of thresholds depend only on the number of units remaining. We evaluate these policies against the multi-unit prophet benchmark, which selects the best $K$ realized values in hindsight. Our main contribution is a new competitive-analysis framework for stock-based thresholds in continuous time. We first reformulate the problem through a type-covering dual. The central challenge for analyzing the dual is that the dual is both infinite-dimensional and non-convex: the adversary can choose time-varying arrival and valuation processes, while the policy performance depends nonlinearly on the stochastic inventory trajectory.
  We overcome these challenges by reducing the continuous-time adversarial problem to a Poisson optimization $PoisOPTRe_K$, and then proving sharp structural properties of its worst-case solutions. In particular, adversarial arrivals admit cutoff and late-filling structures, which yield an exact four-parameter formulation for two thresholds and a finite nested-interval representation for general thresholds. These reductions make the guarantees directly computable. For example, for two thresholds, we obtain a competitive ratio $0.6269$ for $K=2$; with three thresholds, we obtain the ratio $0.6816$ for $K=3$. In this way, we show that simple stock-based thresholds achieve strong prophet-inequality guarantees despite ignoring calendar time.

</details>


### [133] [Parallel Machine Scheduling with a Singler Server and Loading-Unloading Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12087)
*Keramat Hasani,Frank Werner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一个带单一公共服务器（同时负责装载与卸载操作）的并行机调度问题，目标是最小化制造跨度（makespan）。证明了当机器数为输入的一部分时该决策问题为强NP完全；分析了List Scheduling（LS）和Longest Processing Time（LPT）两种启发式算法的最坏情况性能比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公共服务器调度模型通常只考虑装载操作，而实际场景（如自动化制造、机器人上下料）中服务器还需在工件加工结束后立即执行卸载操作。本文旨在填补这一更贴近实际需求的模型在计算复杂度与启发式算法性能分析方面的空白。

Method: 通过归约证明强NP完全性；采用最坏情况分析（worst-case analysis）对LS和LPT算法建立近似比上界。具体对三台机器给出LS的5/2近似比，对任意固定m分析LS的渐近界4-3/m，并给出LPT的有限实例近似比精确表达式。

Result: 决策问题在机器数为输入一部分时是强NP完全的；LS在三台机器上近似比至多为5/2；对任意固定m≥3，LS的一般界随工件数增长趋近4-3/m；LPT的有限实例近似比为3-2/m+(m-1)(m-2)/(mn)，随工件数增长趋近3-2/m。

Conclusion: 该带加载与卸载双重操作的公共服务器并行机调度问题在复杂性上比加载-only模型更困难（强NP完全），且两种经典启发式算法均具有常数近似比，其中LPT在渐近意义上优于LS。

Abstract: This paper investigates a parallel machine scheduling problem featuring a single common server responsible for both loading and unloading operations. Each job consists of a unit-time loading operation, non-preemptive processing on one of \(m\) identical machines, and a unit-time unloading operation executed by the same server. The objective is to minimize the makespan. Unlike classical loading-only common-server models, our setting requires the server to handle an unloading operation immediately after a job's processing phase concludes. We prove that the corresponding decision problem is strongly NP-complete when the number of machines is given as part of the input. Furthermore, we analyse the worst-case performance of the List Scheduling (LS) and Longest Processing Time (LPT) heuristics. For three machines, we establish that Algorithm LS achieves an approximation ratio of at most \(5/2\). For an arbitrary fixed \(m \ge 3\), we show that the general LS bound approaches \(4-3/m\) as the number of jobs grows, whereas for Algorithm LPT, we prove the finite-instance ratio $3-\frac{2}{m}+\frac{(m-1)(m-2)}{mn}.$ Thus, for each fixed \(m\), the LPT bound approaches \(3-2/m\) as the number of jobs grows.

</details>


### [134] [On the Convergence Rate Lower Bound of Biochemical Computational Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12109)
*Yuzhen Fan,Chuanhou Gao,Shibo He,Jiming Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简洁的收敛速率特征化方法，用于描述具有多个输出物种的生化计算模块，并严格证明了该收敛速率由雅可比矩阵实部最大（最不负）的特征值所界定。通过两个数值例子验证了理论下界对收敛速率范围的影响及其对反应速率常数的依赖关系，为生化计算模块的速度评估与精度控制设计提供了实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生化反应网络是实现分子计算的核心理论框架，但实际实现中计算输出编码于限制稳态（LSSs）的物种浓度，而实际运行时间有限，因此有限时间内的计算精度成为关键挑战。现有研究缺乏对多输出物种生化计算模块收敛速率的系统性表征，难以指导高速、高精度模块的设计。

Method: 提出一种针对多输出物种生化计算模块收敛速率的简洁特征化方法，从理论上严格证明该收敛速率由雅可比矩阵最大实部（最不负）特征值所约束，并通过两个数值例子展示理论下界如何塑造收敛速率范围及其对反应速率常数的依赖关系。

Result: 通过严格的数学证明确立了收敛速率与雅可比矩阵特征值（实部最大者）之间的理论界限关系；两个数值实例验证了该理论下界对收敛速率范围的约束作用，并揭示了反应速率常数对收敛速率的依赖规律。

Conclusion: 该研究为系统性评估生化计算速度提供了理论框架，并为构建高精度、误差可控的生化计算模块提供了实用的设计度量标准。

Abstract: Biochemical reaction networks have become a central theoretical framework for implementing molecular computation. A key challenge is finite time computational accuracy, as computation outputs are encoded in limiting steady states (LSSs) of species concentrations while practical implementations operate for only finite time. This work proposes a concise characterization of convergence rate for biochemical computational modules with multiple output species, and rigorously establishes it as being bounded by the eigenvalue with largest (least negative) real part of the Jacobian matrix. Two numerical examples illustrate how the theoretical lower bound shapes the convergence rate range and reveals its dependence on reaction rate constants. This formulation enables systematic evaluation of biochemical computation speed and provides a practical design measure for constructing high-accuracy and error-controlled biochemical computational modules.

</details>


### [135] [The Advective Fisher-Rao Geometry of Deterministic Measure Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12111)
*Benjamin Gess,Johannes Müller*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于概率测度路径（由连续性方程支配）优化任务的新型平流Fisher-Rao度量，该度量可产生最优下降方向，并从三个不同视角自然导出，辅以计算实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为概率测度路径上的优化任务寻找一种适当的度量，使得优化过程能够获得最优的下降方向，从而更有效地拟合概率密度。

Method: 引入平流Fisher-Rao度量，并证明其从三个视角自然涌现：(1) 路径测度上Fisher-Rao度量的重标度零噪声极限；(2) Freidlin-Wentzell大偏差速率泛函二阶变分的期望值；(3) 动态最优传输中Benamou-Brenier作用泛函的Hessian。随后通过计算实验验证该度量的效果，并与Gauss-Newton方法进行对比。

Result: 实验表明，平流Fisher-Rao度量在概率密度拟合任务中产生最优拟合效果，而Gauss-Newton方法则在速度场拟合中表现最优，两者分别适用于不同类型的状态变量拟合。

Conclusion: 平流Fisher-Rao度量是概率测度路径上优化任务的正确几何结构，能够提供最优下降方向，其理论来源多样且坚实，计算上也有良好的拟合能力，与Gauss-Newton方法形成互补。

Abstract: A novel advective Fisher-Rao metric is introduced for optimization tasks on paths of probability measures governed by the continuity equation. This metric is shown to lead to optimal descent directions.
  It is then shown that this metric arises naturally from three different perspectives: As the rescaled zero-noise limit of the Fisher-Rao metric on path measures, as the expected value of the second variation of the Freidlin--Wentzell large deviation rate functional, and as the Hessian of the Benamou--Brenier action functional from dynamic optimal transport.
  We supplement this geometric construction with computational experiments. Here, we demonstrate empirically that the advective Fisher-Rao metric yields the desired optimal fitting of probability densities, whereas the Gauss--Newton method yields optimal fitting of velocity fields.

</details>


### [136] [A Sharp Four-Layer Theorem for Integer-Occupied Slices of a Planar Disk-Slab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12204)
*Xinyue Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有理正定二次子水平集与全秩仿射格陪集及闭片（slab）相交时的格点结构问题，提出一种确定性的二维delta=3/4约简方法，证明在特定分支下格点至多分布在所选泛函的四个整数层上，并通过显式有理实例证明该界是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决有理正定二次子水平集与闭片相交情形下格点分布的结构性问题，给出一个紧的层数上界（四个整数层）并证明其最优性。

Method: 采用确定性二维delta=3/4约简选取原始对偶整数泛函与内椭圆；通过直径估计将反例归约为三块连续五层；利用最近整数不等式排除中间块；借助对称性与胞元局部化将两侧块化为八种模式，其中四种由共享容量不等式导出，四种由包含17,640个正系数（最小为625/2048）的精确四变量Bernstein证书验证。

Result: 在Babai点位于椭圆外的分支中，盘-片内格点至多占据所选泛函的四个整数层；显式有理实例达到四层，证明上界是紧的；Bernstein证书含17,640个正系数，最小系数为625/2048。

Conclusion: 该四层上界是严格且不可改进的，通过组合多种不等式技术与计算辅助验证（Bernstein证书）完整证明了该结果，为格点计数与整数规划的理论提供了新的紧性结论。

Abstract: Let a rational positive-definite quadratic sublevel set on a full-rank affine lattice coset be intersected with a closed slab. A deterministic two-dimensional delta=3/4 reduction selects a primitive-dual integer functional and an inner ellipse. In the branch where the corresponding Babai point lies outside that ellipse, the lattice points in the disk-slab occupy at most four integer levels of the selected functional. An explicit rational instance attains four, so the bound is sharp. A strict diameter estimate reduces any counterexample to three blocks of five consecutive levels, and a nearest-integer inequality excludes the central block. Symmetry and cell localization reduce the two side blocks to eight modes: four follow from a shared-cap inequality, and four from an exact four-variable Bernstein certificate. The certificate contains 17,640 positive coefficients, with minimum 625/2048.

</details>

### [137] [A Recursive Representation of Compatibility Matrices for Transported Differential Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11365)
*Gerardo Hernandez-del-Valle*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个递归代数框架，用于分析热方程移动边界问题中出现的相容矩阵。该框架将每个相容矩阵分解为一个普适参考矩阵和一系列传输扰动，揭示了只有传输行在每个递归级别改变，从而将行列式理解为参考矩阵行列式加上连续低秩扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动边界问题中的边界迹算子（如Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射和布朗运动的首达问题）分析中自然出现相容矩阵，但缺乏系统性的代数组织框架。作者旨在建立一个递归代数基础，以支持未来对递归行列式公式和边界迹算子的研究。

Method: 构建两个递归层级：(1) 内部传输微分算子；(2) 通过边界条件的逐次微分生成的边界算子。两者的相互作用产生一个递归相容矩阵族，其规模随传输算子的阶数线性增长。关键贡献是将每个相容矩阵分解为普适参考矩阵加一系列传输扰动，从而隔离边界几何与递归传输修正。

Result: 每个相容矩阵可被分解为参考矩阵和传输扰动之和，且仅传输行在每个递归级别发生变化。相容矩阵的行列式可解释为参考矩阵行列式加上连续低秩扰动的结果。显式示例展示了该递归构造，并暗示参考行列式中存在额外的代数结构。

Conclusion: 该递归表示提供了相容矩阵的系统化组织，并为未来研究递归行列式公式、边界迹算子以及移动边界问题的Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射建立了代数基础。

Abstract: We develop a recursive algebraic framework for the compatibility matrices arising in moving-boundary problems for the heat equation. Such matrices naturally appear in the analysis of boundary trace operators, including Dirichlet-to-Neumann maps and first-passage problems for Brownian motion. The construction is based on two recursive hierarchies: transported differential operators in the interior and boundary operators generated by successive differentiation of the boundary condition. Their interaction produces a recursive family of compatibility matrices whose size grows linearly with the order of the transported operator.
  The main contribution of the paper is a decomposition of every compatibility matrix into a universal reference matrix and a sequence of transport perturbations. This representation isolates the boundary geometry from the recursive transport corrections and shows that only the transport rows change at each recursive level. Consequently, the determinant of the compatibility matrix may be interpreted as the determinant of a reference matrix together with successive low-rank perturbations.
  The recursive construction is illustrated through explicit examples, which suggest additional algebraic structure in the reference determinants. The resulting recursive representation provides a systematic organization of compatibility matrices and establishes an algebraic foundation for future investigations of recursive determinant formulas, boundary trace operators, and Dirichlet-to-Neumann maps for moving-boundary problems.

</details>


### [138] [Optimal Convergence Rate for Periodic Homogenization of Rearrangement-Invariant Convex Hamilton--Jacobi Equations in Infinite Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11449)
*Seho Park*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了环面上不可区分粒子构成的无限系统所对应的凸哈密顿-雅可比方程周期均匀化的最优收敛速率 O(ε)，在初始数据仅依赖于平均构型的假设下成立。通过在无限维希尔伯特空间中切割有限维平均时间投影并利用周期性和重排不变性诱导的紧商进行粘合，克服了无限维空间中缺乏局部紧性和有限维拓扑的困难，并给出示例证明该速率是尖锐的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有限维结果[1]（基于拉格朗日作用量度量的长时间行为和曲线手术论证）推广到由无限不可区分粒子系统产生的哈密顿-雅可比方程。核心挑战在于无限维希尔伯特空间中的极小化曲线缺乏局部紧性和有限维拓扑结构，使得原有工具无法直接应用。

Method: 通过切割极小化曲线的有限维平均时间投影，并利用周期性和重排不变性所诱导出的紧商空间，在无限维希尔伯特空间中粘合被提升的曲线片段，从而绕过无限维空间中局部紧性缺失的困难，最终证明最优收敛速率 O(ε)。

Result: 证明了无限粒子系统凸哈密顿-雅可比方程周期均匀化的最优收敛速率 O(ε)，并通过一个具体示例验证该收敛速率是尖锐的（不可改进）。

Conclusion: 本文成功将有限维均匀化结果扩展到无限维粒子系统场景，所提出的曲线切割-粘合方法有效解决了无限维希尔伯特空间中缺乏局部紧性的根本困难，且最优速率 O(ε) 已被证明是尖锐的。

Abstract: We prove the optimal convergence rate $O(\varepsilon)$ for periodic homogenization of convex Hamilton-Jacobi equations arising from infinite systems of indistinguishable particles on the torus, under the assumption that the initial data depend only on the mean configuration. This extends the finite-dimensional result [1], which is based on the large-time behavior of the Lagrangian action metric and a curve-surgery argument. Here, these tools cannot be applied directly because minimizing curves live in an infinite-dimensional Hilbert space, where local compactness and finite-dimensional topology are unavailable. We overcome this difficulty by cutting the finite-dimensional mean-time projection of a minimizing curve and gluing the lifted pieces in the Hilbert space using the compact quotient induced by periodicity and rearrangement invariance. We conclude with an example showing that this rate is sharp.

</details>


### [139] [Quantitative Unique Continuation on Simplex and $\mathbb{Z}^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11602)
*Shihe Liu,Yunfeng Shi,Zhifei Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在n维单纯形格Δ_N^(n)和整数格Z^n上建立了任意n≥3的定量唯一延拓性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在更高维度的格点上建立定量唯一延拓，将已有的结果推广到任意n≥3的情形。

Method: 分别在n维单纯形格Δ_N^(n)和Z^n上构造定量唯一延拓的证明框架，覆盖任意维度n≥3。

Result: 成功在两类重要的格点结构（单纯形格与整数格）上建立了任意维度的定量唯一延拓结果。

Conclusion: 该工作将定量唯一延拓的理论推广至任意高维格点情形，为相关偏微分方程和谱理论领域提供基础工具。

Abstract: In this work, we establish the quantitative unique continuation on both $n$-dimensional simplex lattice $Δ^{(n)}_{N}$ and $\mathbb{Z}^n$ for arbitrary $n\geq 3$.

</details>


### [140] [Admissible Invariant-Torus Foliations for Steady Euler Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11547)
*Naoki Sato,Ken Abe*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维稳定欧拉流中叶状结构（foliation structure）的一般分类问题。在环面域中，以通量函数Ψ的水平集为叶，证明了满足ι_u dΨ=0和p=p(Ψ)假设的C¹稳定欧拉流可以表示为切向流形式u=c1(Ψ)ξ¹+c2(Ψ)ξ²，其中ξ¹和ξ²是环面叶上加权调和一次型自然基对应的无散矢量场。通量函数满足一个标量方程（法向通量方程）。该刻画揭示了稳定欧拉流的一般叶状结构，轴对称情形可还原出Clebsch表示和Grad-Shafranov方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借鉴Arnold于1965年建立的结构定理（保证非恒压的一般三维稳定欧拉流存在不变曲面叶状结构），作者希望进一步刻画这些叶状结构的具体形式，回答"稳定欧拉流中究竟能出现哪些叶状结构"这一根本性问题。

Method: 在环面域中，以通量函数Ψ的水平集为叶子，在假设ι_u dΨ=0（流场沿叶切向）和p=p(Ψ)（压力为通量函数的函数）下，利用加权调和一次型的自然基构造提升的无散矢量场ξ¹和ξ²，证明稳定欧拉流可表示为切向流表示u=c1(Ψ)ξ¹+c2(Ψ)ξ²，并推导出通量函数所满足的法向通量方程（单一标量方程）。轴对称情形作为特例被恢复。

Result: 证明了满足假设条件的C¹稳定欧拉流具有上述切向流表示，且通量函数满足法向通量方程。该刻画统一了一般三维情形与轴对称情形，轴对称情况下可恢复Clebsch表示和Grad-Shafranov方程作为特例。

Conclusion: 该研究完整刻画了稳定欧拉流的一般叶状结构，确立了通量函数与法向通量方程在描述此类流动中的核心地位，并将经典轴对称结果统一纳入更一般的三维框架之中。

Abstract: In 1965, V. I. Arnold established a structure theorem guaranteeing the existence of a foliation by invariant surfaces for general three-dimensional steady Euler flows with non-constant pressure. In this paper, we investigate what foliation structures can arise in steady Euler flows. We consider a toroidal domain foliated by the level sets of a flux function $Ψ$, and prove that every $C^{1}$ steady Euler flow $(\boldsymbol{u},p)$ satisfying the assumptions $ι_{\boldsymbol{u}}dΨ=0$ and $p=p(Ψ)$ admits the tangential flow representation \[\boldsymbol{u}=c_1(Ψ)\boldsymbolξ^{1}+c_2(Ψ)\boldsymbolξ^{2},\] for some lifted solenoidal vector fields $\boldsymbolξ^{1}$ and $\boldsymbolξ^{2}$ associated with a natural basis of weighted harmonic one-forms on the toroidal leaves. Moreover, the flux function $Ψ$ satisfies a single scalar equation, referred to as the normal flux equation. These characterizations reveal the general foliation structure of steady Euler flows, with the Clebsch representation and the Grad--Shafranov equation recovered as the axisymmetric special case.

</details>


### [141] [Pure-swirl loss of boundedness under $L^1_tL^2_x$ forcing: exact mixed-norm ranges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11553)
*Hugo Beirão da Veiga,Jiaqi Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 论文构造了三维强迫Navier–Stokes方程组在圆柱域中的显式纯涡旋解。对于任意T>0及满足1/p+1/q>1的1≤p,q<∞，外力属于L^q(0,T;L^p(D))且在t<T时光滑。该解在[0,T)上经典，在T时刻强L^2延拓为唯一的Leray–Hopf解，满足能量等式，但解的L^∞范数在t→T时发散至无穷。构造通过环形抵消技术精化了Zhang的剖面，保留了对称轴处的光滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Navier–Stokes方程解的正则性边界问题，特别是外力在临界混合范数空间中如何影响解的爆破。通过构造一个在有限时间内L^∞范数发散的显式解，考察外力Lebesgue空间指标满足1/p+1/q>1这一临界条件的端点行为，并澄清此前构造中k=1情形的端点讨论。

Method: 采用显式构造方法，在圆柱域中构建纯涡旋（swirl-free）解。核心技巧是引入了"环形抵消"（annular cancellation）机制来精化Zhang的profile，确保对称轴处的光滑性。这种构造是对作者此前加权构造在k=1情形的直接自包含锐化，其中对流项被压力吸收，因此同样适用于强迫Stokes系统。

Result: 确定了外力和速度的精确混合范数范围，获得了外力、速度上确界范数以及涡量场（enstrophy）的双边速率估计。解在[0,T)上经典，在T时刻延拓为唯一的Leray–Hopf解且满足能量等式，同时‖v(t)‖_{L^∞}→ ∞当t→T。此外，外力还满足额外的能量类性质f∈L^1(0,T;L^2(D))。

Conclusion: 该构造在临界指标处提供了Navier–Stokes方程外力和解空间的精确匹配结果，明确了L^q(0,T;L^p(D))中1/p+1/q>1这一条件下解的最高奇异行为。由于对流项被压力完全吸收，该例子同时适用于强迫Stokes系统，展示了两个系统在这种外力条件下具有一致的爆破行为。

Abstract: We construct an explicit pure-swirl solution of the forced three-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations in a circular cylinder. For every $T>0$ and $1\le p,q<\infty$ with $1/p+1/q>1$, the force belongs to $L^q(0,T;L^p(D))$ and is smooth for $t<T$. Moreover, the same construction always yields the additional energy-class property $f\in L^1(0,T;L^2(D))$. The solution is classical on $[0,T)$, extends strongly in $L^2(D)$ to the unique Leray--Hopf solution at time $T$, and satisfies the energy equality, while $\|v(t)\|_{L^\infty(D)}\to\infty$ as $t\uparrow T$. We determine the exact mixed-norm ranges of both the force and the velocity and obtain two-sided rates for the force, the velocity supremum norm and the enstrophy. The construction refines Zhang's profile by an annular cancellation that preserves smoothness at the symmetry axis. It is a direct, self-contained sharpening of the $k=1$ part of our previous weighted construction and supersedes its endpoint discussion. Since the convection term is absorbed by the pressure, the same example applies to the forced Stokes system.

</details>


### [142] [Higher Regularity of Homogeneous Gradient Compositions for $p$-Laplace-Type Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11586)
*Quoc Hung Nguyen,Le Xuan Truong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究非齐次p-Laplace方程div(|Du|^{p-2}Du)=f的解梯度函数的更高正则性。在梯度Du∈C^{0,α}且α≤1/(p-1)的假设下，若Φ在原点外光滑且为m次正齐次函数，则当m>k/α时，Φ(Du)属于C^k_loc，且其至多k阶导数在临界集上为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: p-Laplace方程的解在临界集（即梯度为零的点集）上通常不满足C²正则性，但梯度本身是局部Hölder连续的。作者希望理解梯度函数（特别是梯度的高次幂）在这种临界点附近的更高阶正则性，这对理解退化椭圆方程解的精细结构具有重要意义。

Method: 证明主要使用三个关键工具：(1) 引入内蕴尺度r≃|Du|^{1/α}来放大临界集附近的几何；(2) 利用归一化一致椭圆方程的Schauder估计；(3) 建立跨越临界集的扩张引理。该方法还可推广到各向异性方程、椭圆和抛物型p-Laplace方程组，以及多孔介质方程。

Result: 证明了当m>k/α时Φ(Du)∈C^k_loc，且所有至多k阶导数在临界集上消失。该结果对自治各向异性方程、椭圆与抛物型p-Laplace方程组同样成立（在适当的梯度Hölder假设下），并给出了多孔介质方程非负解的高次幂的C^k正则性判据。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了p-Laplace型方程解在临界集附近的正则性结构：尽管解本身不是C²的，但其梯度函数的适当高次齐次组合可以拥有任意高阶的正则性。这为理解退化/奇异椭圆方程的正则性理论提供了统一框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we study higher regularity of homogeneous functions of the gradient of solutions to the inhomogeneous $p$-Laplace equation $\operatorname{div}(|Du|^{p-2}Du)=f$. Although a solution need not be of class $C^2$ across its critical set, its gradient is locally Hölder continuous. Suppose that $Du\in C^{0,α}_{\rm loc}$ with $α\le 1/(p-1)$, and let $Φ$ be smooth away from the origin and positively homogeneous of degree $m$. We prove that $Φ(Du)\in C^k_{\rm loc}$ whenever $m>k/α$. Moreover, all its derivatives of order at most $k$ vanish on the critical set. The proof uses the intrinsic scale $r\simeq |Du|^{1/α}$, Schauder estimates for a normalized uniformly elliptic equation, and an extension lemma across the critical set. We also obtain corresponding results for autonomous anisotropic equations and for elliptic and parabolic $p$-Laplace systems, under the appropriate Hölder assumption on the gradient. Finally, the same argument gives $C^k$ regularity criteria for high powers of nonnegative solutions to the porous medium equation.

</details>


### [143] [Sharp instability of planar isotropic--nematic interfaces in the Landau--de Gennes model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12131)
*Wei Wang,Qin Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了Landau–de Gennes模型中一维平面各向同性—向列相界面的稳定性问题，在仅含各向异性弹性常数L的设定下，去除了此前L<0时证明不稳定性所需的额外条件，证明了在整个-3/2<L<0范围内任何约化能量对角平面类的非负极小元在一维一般扰动下都是不稳定的。L=0被确认为从负L侧看的不稳定区间的尖锐端点。此外还定义了最优稳定性指标，并修正了此前界面剖面中一个因子为2的归一化不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于Landau–de Gennes模型中L<0时一维平面各向同性—向列相界面不稳定性的证明仅在一个额外条件下成立，本文旨在移除该限制条件，完整刻画负L范围内所有可能极小元的不稳定性，从而确立L=0作为不稳定区间的尖锐端点，并解决稳定性指标的定义与界面剖面归一的规范性问题。

Method: 通过移除先前工作所需额外条件，对约化能量对角平面类中任意非负极小元，在一般一维扰动下进行分析，构造并检验临界算子的符号性质，证明其在-3/2<L<0全范围内非负性破坏（即不稳定），并验证L=0处临界算子非负以确立尖锐端点；同时重新定义最优稳定性指标并修正界面剖面的因子二归一化不一致。

Result: 在-3/2<L<0的整个区间内，任何非负的约化能量对角平面类极小元在一般一维扰动下均不稳定；L=0处临界算子非负，确认其为不稳定区间的尖锐端点；给出了最优稳定性指标的定义，并修正了先前界面剖面的因子2归一化误差。

Conclusion: 该结果完整确立了负L侧界面不稳定性成立的充要范围，L=0是负L侧不稳定区间的尖锐端点，从而填补了此前证明中的空白，推动了Landau–de Gennes模型中界面稳定性理论的完善。

Abstract: We study the stability of one-dimensional planar isotropic--nematic interfaces in the Landau--de Gennes model with anisotropic elastic constant $L$. Earlier work proved the instability for $L<0$ only under an extra condition. We remove this condition and prove that any non-negative minimizer of the reduced energy within the diagonal planar class is unstable under general one-dimensional perturbations throughout $-\frac{3}{2}<L<0$. This result shows that $L=0$ is the sharp endpoint of the instability range from the negative-$L$ side: the critical operator is non-negative at $L=0$, while instability holds over the full negative-$L$ range of the reduced problem. We also define the optimal stability index and correct a factor-of-two normalization inconsistency in a previously stated interface profile.

</details>


### [144] [Superharmonicity of the fractional ground state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11686)
*Nicola De Nitti,Xavier Fernández-Real*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于分数阶拉普拉斯算子(-Δ)^s在任意开集Ω上的正基态u，若存在，则满足不等式-Δu>λ_s(Ω)^{1/s}u；在球B_R中，还建立了定量对数凹估计D²log u(x)≤D²log u(0)<-λ_s(B_R)^{1/s}/n·Id。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在证明分数阶拉普拉斯算子正基态满足经典拉普拉斯意义下的严格不等式-Δu>λ_s^{1/s}u，并建立对数凹估计，这是对分数阶问题中基态性质的深入刻画，为理解这类非局部算子基态的几何与分析性质提供基础。

Method: 利用分数阶拉普拉斯算子的谱性质与第一特征值λ_s(Ω)的定义，结合基态u的正性与极值原理推导严格不等式；在球域情形中，通过对数凹性技术（拼接函数、比较原理等）获得定量的对数凹估计。

Result: 在任意开集Ω上证明了正基态满足-Δu>λ_s(Ω)^{1/s}u；在球体B_R中获得了定量的对数凹性估计D²log u(x)≤D²log u(0)<-λ_s(B_R)^{1/s}/n·Id，表明基态具有严格且定量的对数凹性。

Conclusion: 本文为分数阶拉普拉斯算子正基态建立了新的严格不等式和对数凹性定量估计，推广了经典拉普拉斯情形的相关结果，深化了对分数阶椭圆问题中基态结构性质的认识。

Abstract: Let $s\in(0,1)$. We prove that the positive ground state $u$ of the fractional Laplacian $(-Δ)^s$ on an arbitrary open set $Ω$, whenever it exists, satisfies $-Δu>λ_s(Ω)^{1/s}u$ in $Ω$, where $λ_s(Ω)$ denotes the corresponding first eigenvalue. In a ball $B_R$, we also obtain the quantitative log-concavity estimate $D^2\log u(x)\le D^2\log u(0) < -\frac{λ_s(B_R)^{1/s}}{n}\,\mathrm{Id}$.

</details>


### [145] [The critical exponent for semilinear wave equations with damped oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11722)
*Marcello D'Abbicco*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类带幂非线性项的演化方程在具有特定耗散效应（低频抑制振荡、高频产生过阻尼）下全局小数据解的存在性指数，并指出在n=3维空间粘弹性耗散波动方程情形下，Wenhui Chen最近证明的爆破结果与该存在性指数的最优性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定具有特殊耗散结构（低频抑制振荡、高频过阻尼）的演化方程全局小数据解的存在性指数，并验证该指数是否最优（即是否与爆破临界指数相匹配）。

Method: 通过分析耗散项在不同频率区域（低频和高频）的不同作用机制，研究带幂非线性项的演化方程，确定全局小数据解的临界存在性指数。研究涵盖多种耗散型波动方程（如粘弹性耗散波动方程），并与已有的爆破结果进行对比。

Result: 确定了该类演化方程全局小数据解的存在性指数。在n=3维情形下，粘弹性耗散波动方程的爆破结果（由Wenhui Chen证明）表明该存在性指数是最优的。

Conclusion: 该文确定了带幂非线性且具有特殊耗散结构演化方程的存在性指数，并通过与粘弹性耗散波动方程在n=3维的爆破结果对照，验证了该指数的最优性。

Abstract: We determine the existence exponent for the global small data solutions for a class of evolution equations with power nonlinearity under the effect of a dissipation that dampen the oscillations at low frequencies and produce overdamping at high frequencies. In the case of the wave equation with viscoelastic dissipation, the counterpart of blow-up in space dimension $n=3$ has been very recently proved by Wenhui Chen, showing that the existence exponent is optimal.

</details>


### [146] [Scattering Theory For 3D Cubic Damped Magnetic Schrödinger Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11834)
*Luca Fanelli,Haruya Mizutani,Yilin Song,Ying Wang,Jiqiang Zheng*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究三维变系数、含磁势和局部阻尼项的三次散焦非线性薛定谔方程，在不要求度量G满足非陷阱条件的情况下，仅假设变系数区域包含于有效阻尼区域，并在切向磁场满足单中心条件时证明了H^{1+ε}初值的整体适定性、一致质量与能量界及局部能量衰减；进一步施加阻尼区域内全磁场支撑条件后可获得散射结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性薛定谔方程在变系数介质、磁场和阻尼作用下的长时间行为研究是数学物理中的重要问题。以往结果通常需要非陷阱条件，本文旨在放宽该条件，用阻尼效应替代非陷阱假设，从而在更一般的几何情形下建立整体适定性与散射理论。

Method: 采用变系数框架下的Mourre估计/共形能量方法，结合局部阻尼效应。关键思想是利用有效阻尼区域覆盖变系数区域，使得能量耗散足以克服几何陷阱效应；磁场的单中心条件用于控制切向磁场带来的额外项；散射部分通过对全磁场附加支撑条件，将阻尼区域内的磁场约束与自由薛定谔演化联系起来。

Result: 在弱假设下（变系数区域包含于阻尼区域 + 切向磁场单中心条件），得到H^{1+ε}初值的整体适定性、一致质量与能量界以及局部能量衰减；在强假设下（阻尼区域内全磁场支撑条件），对任意0≤s<1，解在H^s中散射到自由薛定谔演化。附录还给出抽象Hamiltonian框架下的常数阻尼设定。

Conclusion: 本文通过阻尼效应替代传统非陷阱条件，成功建立了更一般几何情形下三维散焦三次NLS的整体适定性与散射理论，为变系数介质中非线性波的长时间行为研究提供了新途径。

Abstract: We consider the three-dimensional defocusing cubic nonlinear Schrödinger equation with variable coefficients, a magnetic potential, and a non-negative localized damping term, \[ i\partial_tu+(\nabla-iA)\cdot G(\nabla-iA)u+ia(x)u=|u|^2u, \qquad t>0,\quad x\in\mathbb R^3. \] No non-trapping condition is imposed on the metric $G$. Instead, the variable-coefficient region is assumed to be contained in the effective damping region. Under a one-centre condition on the tangential magnetic field, we prove global well-posedness for initial data in $H^{1+\varepsilon}$, uniform mass and energy bounds, and show the local energy decay. To obtain scattering, we impose a support condition on the full magnetic field inside the damping region. Under these stronger assumptions, the solution scatters to a free Schrödinger evolution in $H^s$ for every $0\le s<1$. The appendix discusses a separate constant-damping framework for abstract Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [147] [Existence and non-existence results for a fractional Lane-Emden equation with nonlocal Neumann conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11842)
*Eleonora Cinti,Matteo Talluri,Tobias Weth*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究半空间中带非局部Neumann条件的分数阶Lane-Emden方程，证明了临界问题在任何维度下都存在非常数解，同时证明了在维度n=1时次临界问题仅有平凡解，后者通过新建立的Pohozaev型恒等式及解的衰减估计得出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索分数阶Lane-Emden方程在半空间中带非局部Neumann条件下的解的存在性与唯一性问题，尤其是临界问题和次临界问题在不同维度下的行为差异。

Method: 建立一个新的Pohozaev型恒等式，结合对解的适当的衰减估计，来证明次临界问题在n=1时的平凡解唯一性；通过其他分析方法建立临界问题在所有维度下非常数解的存在性。

Result: (1) 临界问题在任何维度下都存在非常数解；(2) 在维度n=1时，次临界问题仅有平凡解。

Conclusion: 临界问题与次临界问题在解的存在性上表现出不同的维度依赖性：临界问题在所有维度中均有非常数解，而一维亚临界问题则被限制为只有平凡解，这一结论依赖于新推导的Pohozaev型恒等式。

Abstract: We consider the fractional Lane-Emden equation with a nonlocal Neumann condition in a half-space. We establish the existence of non-constant solutions for the critical problem in any dimension. On the other hand, we show that, in dimension $n=1$, the subcritical problem admits only the trivial solution. This result follows from a new Pohozaev-type identity, which we obtain by using suitable decay estimates for the solutions.

</details>


### [148] [Sharp logarithmic corrections for a strong-weak Lotka-Volterra competition system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11956)
*Hongjun Guo,Dongyuan Xiao,Maolin Zhou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维强-弱Lotka-Volterra竞争-扩散系统中强竞争者u的波前渐近行为，在参数条件0<a<1<b下，通过将竞争系统转化为合作抛物系统并结合移动域比较原理和热核估计，推导出水平集的精细对数渐近展开，并依据三个特征速度的大小将长时间动力学划分为五种机制（拉拽、非局部拉拽、推进、临界过渡），给出了显式的对数及双对数相位修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献仅给出了该竞争-扩散系统的波前传播速度的主导阶渐近结果，尚未在全参数空间内给出波前精细的对数修正项。这些对数修正对于刻画波前剖面的长时间相位偏移至关重要，因此需要发展新的理论工具来获得更加精确的渐近展开。

Method: 首先将竞争系统通过变换转化为等价的合作抛物系统；在此基础上建立移动域比较原理；利用热核估计技术，推导强竞争者u最右端水平集的精细对数渐近展开式。通过比较三个特征速度的大小对长时间动力学机制进行分类。

Result: 依据三个特征速度的数量级，将长时间动力学划分为五种机制：拉拽（pulled）、非局部拉拽（nonlocally pulled）、推进（pushed）和临界过渡（critical transition）前锋，并给出了每种机制下显式的对数与双对数相位修正项。

Conclusion: 本文的结果捕捉到了以往主导阶理论忽略的波前剖面精细长时间相位偏移，为竞争-扩散系统中波前传播的精细渐近分析提供了完整且统一的框架。

Abstract: We study the one-dimensional strong-weak Lotka-Volterra competition-diffusion system \[ u_t=u_{xx}+u(1-u-av),\qquad v_t=dv_{xx}+rv(1-v-bu), \] with compactly supported initial data under the parameter condition $0<a<1<b$. Existing literature only gives leading-order spreading speed asymptotics without refined logarithmic corrections for wave fronts over the full parameter space. We convert the competitive system into an equivalent cooperative parabolic system and establish moving-domain comparison principles. Based on heat-kernel estimates, we derive sharp logarithmic asymptotic expansions for the rightmost level set of the stronger species $u$. According to the magnitudes of three characteristic speeds, five long-time dynamical regimes are classified, including pulled, nonlocally pulled, pushed and critical transition fronts, with explicit logarithmic and double-logarithmic phase corrections. Our results capture delicate long-time phase offsets of wave profiles neglected in previous leading-order theories.

</details>


### [149] [Improved weak-strong uniqueness for general cross-diffusion systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11982)
*Noah Geltner,Maria Heitzinger,Ansgar Jüngel*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有广泛适用性的交叉扩散系统的有界解的弱-强唯一性，在有无体积填充效应、有无完全强制性等条件下均建立了唯一性结果，大幅放宽了现有文献中对强解的正则性和正性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交叉扩散系统在生物数学、物理等领域有广泛应用，但弱-强唯一性这一基本问题在现有研究中受到强解正则性和正性假设的严格限制。传统的证明方法依赖于正性假设来构造熵函数，且需要较强的正则性条件，这限制了理论的适用范围，尤其对于非强制性系统缺乏有效工具。

Method: 证明基于两个核心思想：(1) 通过构造"粘合熵"(glued entropy)来消除对正性条件的需求，使得无需强解严格为正即可建立唯一性；(2) 利用梯度估计(Gradient estimate)和Gagliardo-Nirenberg不等式来放宽对强解的正则性要求。对于非强制性系统，体积填充效应(volume-filling constraint)被用来弥补强制性的缺失。

Result: 在广泛的交叉扩散系统类别中建立了有界解的弱-强唯一性定理，包括有/无体积填充效应、有/无完全强制性的情况。与现有结果相比，对强解的正则性和正性假设得到了显著放松，并将理论推广到了非强制性系统。

Conclusion: 通过引入粘合熵和结合梯度估计与Gagliardo-Nirenberg不等式的技巧，成功地在更弱的假设下建立了交叉扩散系统的弱-强唯一性，为后续研究提供了新的方法论工具，尤其为非强制性和无正性假设的情形开辟了新的分析途径。

Abstract: The weak-strong uniqueness of bounded solutions is established for a broad class of cross-diffusion systems, with or without volume-filling effects, and with or without full coercivity. Compared to existing results, the regularity and positivity assumptions on the strong solution are significantly relaxed. The analysis also extends to non-coercive systems, with the volume-filling constraint compensating for the lack of coercivity. The proof is based on two key ideas: The positivity condition is eliminated through the construction of a glued entropy, while the regularity requirement can be relaxed by using the gradient estimate and the Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality.

</details>


### [150] [Variational Principles and Rearrangement Inequalities for asymmetric Operators on Periodic Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11986)
*Xing Liang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对周期格上的非对称二阶差分算子的主特征值，建立了变分公式与重排不等式。对于一般不可约最近邻算子，正右、左特征向量提供显式鞍点，从而得到关于正轮廓和周期对数校正子的等值最小-最大与最大-最小公式。校正子唯一且满足非线性离散通量守恒律。对于指数倾斜对称扩散，公式将离散Dirichlet项与势项从精确的非线性周期校正中分离。在一维情形，通量为常数，基本标量通量表示产生了对每个倾斜最大化倾斜主特征值的钟形循环重排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究周期格上非对称二阶差分算子主特征值的变分表征与优化问题，为理解离散椭圆算子在周期介质中的谱性质提供理论工具。

Method: 利用正左右特征向量构造显式鞍点，建立最小-最大与最大-最小变分公式；通过非线性离散通量守恒律刻画唯一校正子；在一维情形引入标量通量表示并构造钟形循环重排。

Result: 获得了主特征值的精确变分公式，证明校正子唯一性及通量守恒性质；在指数倾斜扩散情形实现了离散项与非线性周期校正的分离；一维情形下证明了钟形循环重排可最大化倾斜主特征值。

Conclusion: 该工作为周期格上非对称差分算子主特征值的变分刻画与极值问题提供了完整理论框架，特别在一维情形得到了清晰的重排不等式结果。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish variational formulas and a rearrangement inequality for principal eigenvalues of asymmetric second-order difference operators on periodic lattices. For a general irreducible nearest-neighbor operator, positive right and left eigenvectors give an explicit saddle point and hence equal minimum--maximum and maximum--minimum formulas over positive profiles and periodic logarithmic correctors. The corrector is unique and satisfies a nonlinear discrete flux-conservation law. For exponentially tilted symmetric diffusion, the formula separates the discrete Dirichlet and potential terms from an exact nonlinear periodic correction. In one dimension the flux is constant, and an elementary scalar-flux representation yields a bell-shaped cyclic rearrangement that maximizes the tilted principal eigenvalue for every tilt.

</details>


### [151] [Variable Exponent Regularity via Muckenhoupt Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12095)
*Daviti Adamadze,Anna Kh. Balci,Lars Diening*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文首次在不需要log-Hölder连续性的前提下，建立了$p(x)$-Laplacian弱解的梯度更高可积性和局部$L^\infty$估计，通过将其推广到广义Muckenhoupt条件，允许指数是不连续的BMO点态乘子，架起了经典$p(x)$正则性与加权Muckenhoupt理论之间的桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的$p(x)$-Laplacian正则性理论通常依赖于指数$p$满足log-Hölder连续性条件，这限制了可处理的指数类。作者希望放宽这一限制，使得指数可以是非连续的，从而覆盖更广泛的非标准增长问题。

Method: 将$p(x)$-Laplacian的正则性结果建立在满足广义Muckenhoupt条件的指数上，取代传统的log-Hölder连续性假设。该框架允许指数作为BMO类的点态乘子，从而实现从经典加权理论到非标准增长方程正则性的推广。

Result: 在广义Muckenhoupt条件下，首次获得了$p(x)$-Laplacian弱解的梯度更高可积性以及局部$L^\infty$估计，扩展了可处理指数的范围至非连续情形。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了经典$p(x)$正则性理论与加权Muckenhoupt理论之间的空白，为具有高度不规则非标准增长的微分方程分析提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: For the first time, we establish higher integrability of the gradient and local $L^\infty$ estimates of weak solutions to the $p(x)$-Laplacian without assuming $\log$-Hölder continuity of $p$. Instead, we establish these results for exponents satisfying a generalized Muckenhoupt condition. This admits discontinuous exponents acting as pointwise multipliers of the BMO class. Our framework bridges the gap between classical $p(x)$-regularity and weighted Muckenhoupt theory, providing a new foundation for the analysis of differential equations with highly irregular non-standard growth.

</details>


### [152] [Well-posedness and passivity for a class of bilinear control systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12112)
*Abdelhakim Dahmani,Hannes Gernandt,René Hosfeld,Timo Reis,Tolgahan Tasci*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类抽象双线性无穷维系统（涵盖区域供热系统和量子控制等应用），首先在Banach空间中分析了具有容许输入算子的抽象双线性系统的温和解和经典解的存在性及对数据的连续依赖性；在Hilbert空间中进一步利用这些结果证明了适当定义的共位输出的无源性。研究成果应用于双线性薛定谔方程、Fokker--Planck方程以及区域供热/供冷管道系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双线性无穷维系统在区域供热和量子控制等多个实际应用中广泛出现，然而其解的适定性（存在性、唯一性、连续依赖性）以及无源性等关键性质尚未得到系统性的理论分析，需要建立统一的抽象框架来研究这类系统。

Method: 采用算子半群理论，在Banach空间中利用容许输入算子的框架分析温和解和经典解的存在性与连续依赖性；在Hilbert空间中引入适当定义的共位输出，利用对偶关系证明系统的无源性。

Result: 证明了抽象双线性系统在Banach空间中温和解和经典解的存在性定理及其对初始数据和输入/参数的连续依赖性；在Hilbert空间中建立了共位输出意义下的无源性结论，并将一般理论成功应用于双线性薛定谔方程、Fokker--Planck方程和区域供热/供冷管道系统。

Conclusion: 本文为抽象双线性无穷维系统提供了统一的适定性与无源性分析框架，并通过多个具有代表性的应用实例验证了理论的有效性和广泛适用性，为后续的控制设计和稳定性分析奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We study an abstract class of bilinear infinite-dimensional systems that arises in various applications, including district heating systems and quantum control. First, we analyze the existence of mild and classical solutions for abstract bilinear systems with admissible input operators in Banach spaces and study continuous dependence on the data. When the underlying space is a Hilbert space, these results are used to show passivity for a suitably defined co-located output and for mild solutions. The results are applied to the bilinear Schrödinger equation, the Fokker--Planck equation, and a district heating or cooling pipe.

</details>


### [153] [Reconstruction of shear modulus inclusions in elastostatics via divergence-free localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12141)
*Henrik Garde,Nuutti Hyvönen,Valter Pohjola*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文改进了线性弹性静力学逆问题中的单调性形状重建方法，使其能够独立于第一Lamé参数的不均匀性，直接重建第二Lamé参数（剪切模量）中的夹杂物，超越了以往只能恢复体积模量夹杂物的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单调性方法在弹性静力学形状重建中存在局限，只能在特定Lamé参数局部相互作用假设下恢复体积模量中的夹杂物，无法独立重建剪切模量中的夹杂物。需要改进方法以克服这一限制。

Method: 通过对能量进行线性化处理，利用调和向量场实现散度无关的局域化，从而将单调性方法推广到剪切模量的重建场景，使其对第一Lamé参数的不均匀性不敏感。

Result: 该方法能够独立于第一Lamé参数中的不均匀性，成功重建第二Lamé参数（剪切模量）中的夹杂物，弥补了以往方法的不足。

Conclusion: 改进的单调性方法为线性弹性静力学中的剪切模量夹杂物重建提供了理论保障，扩展了单调性方法在弹性逆问题中的应用范围。

Abstract: We formulate an improved version of the monotonicity method for shape reconstruction in the inverse problem of linear elastostatics. We show that this method can reconstruct inclusions in the second Lamé parameter, i.e., the shear modulus, independently of inhomogeneities in the first Lamé parameter. This improves on earlier methods that only recover inclusions in the bulk modulus under certain assumptions on the local interplay between the Lamé parameters. Our proofs are based on linearization and divergence-free localization of energy using harmonic vector fields.

</details>


### [154] [The traveling wave solutions of the 1D hyperbolic Keller-Segel equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12226)
*Xin Liu,William Kyle Barker*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维双曲Keller-Segel方程在群体感应条件下具有单侧远场条件的行波解。这些行波解在移动坐标下是分段光滑的，并且在间断处满足熵不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双曲Keller-Segel方程在群体感应机制下行波解的存在性和性质，特别是具有单侧远场条件的行波解。

Method: 通过分析一维双曲Keller-Segel方程在移动坐标系中的结构，构造分段光滑的行波解，并利用熵不等式条件来处理解的间断（激波）部分。

Result: 得到了具有单侧远场条件的行波解，这些解在移动坐标系中是分段光滑的，并且在间断处满足熵不等式。

Conclusion: 双曲Keller-Segel方程在群体感应条件下存在满足熵不等式的分段光滑行波解。

Abstract: The goal of this paper is to investigate the traveling wave solutions with one-sided far field conditions for the one-dimensional hyperbolic Keller-Segel equations with quorum sensitivity. The traveling wave solutions are piece-wise smooth in the moving coordinate, and satisfy the entropy inequality at the discontinuity.

</details>


### [155] [An $L^p$-Theory for Time-Periodic Mixed-Order Partial Differential Equations under General Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12250)
*Guillaume Neuttiens,Jonas Sauer*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为混合阶偏微分方程和系统的周期时间边值问题建立了L^p理论，基于Newton多边形和阶函数描述的各向异性函数空间，发展了半空间上的迹理论，并通过补余条件刻画一般边界条件下的适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的L^p边值问题理论主要针对一致阶的椭圆或抛物算子，而许多实际系统中的PDE混合阶算子缺乏系统性理论。此外，带时间导数的边界条件以及动态边界条件需要更一般的框架。

Method: 利用Newton多边形和阶函数构建各向异性函数空间，发展半空间上的迹理论并通过实插值刻画迹空间；建立抽象框架，用解的迹的补余条件刻画适定性；对混合阶微分算子，将补余条件简化为一个补余边界矩阵的可逆性，从而给出L^p空间适定性的显式判据。

Result: 得到的理论适用于一般的Newton多边形结构，并允许边界算子包含时间导数。作为应用，建立了Cahn–Hilliard–Gurtin系统和带动态边界条件的抛物问题的时间周期L^p适定性。

Conclusion: 该工作为混合阶PDE系统的周期时间边值问题提供了一个统一且显式的L^p适定性理论，兼容性条件从抽象框架中自然涌现，并成功应用于涉及动态边界条件的实际系统。

Abstract: We develop an $L^p$-theory for time-periodic boundary value problems associated with partial differential equations and systems of mixed order. Our approach is based on anisotropic function spaces described by order functions and their associated Newton polygons. We develop a trace theory on the half-space, including a characterization of the trace spaces by real interpolation.
  For general boundary conditions, we introduce an abstract framework in which well-posed\-ness is characterized by a complementing condition formulated in terms of the traces of solutions. In particular, the admissible data space, including the compatibility conditions induced by the boundary operators, emerges naturally from the abstract framework. For mixed-order differential operators, the complementing condition is reduced to the invertibility of a complemented boundary matrix, yielding an explicit criterion for well-posedness in $L^p$-based spaces. The resulting theory applies to general Newton polygon structures and allows for boundary operators involving time derivatives. As applications, we establish time-periodic $L^p$-well-posedness for the Cahn--Hilliard--Gurtin system and for parabolic problems with dynamic boundary conditions.

</details>


### [156] [Morrey's problem in $\mathbb{R}^{2 \times 4}$ and $\mathbb{R}^{3 \times 3}_\mathrm{sym}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12298)
*Andrea Agazzi,Giuseppe Bruno,André Guerra,Federico Pasqualotto*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在$\mathbb{R}^{2\times 4}$中构造了一个显式的秩一凸但非拟凸的被积函数，通过一个具有12个非零Fourier模的映射$\mathbb{T}^4\to\mathbb{R}^2$来证伪拟凸性不等式。该映射由标量势导出，从而也在$\mathbb{R}^{4\times 4}_{\text{sym}}$中得到秩一凸非拟凸的例子。这些例子通过对Grabovsky在$\mathbb{R}^{8\times 2}$中例子的转置和限制获得；此外还修改了Šverák的例子，在$\mathbb{R}^{3\times 3}_{\text{sym}}$中构造了秩一凸非拟凸被积函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟凸性是变分法中保证泛函弱下半连续性的核心概念，而秩一凸性是拟凸性的必要条件。长期以来，在低维矩阵空间中寻找秩一凸但非拟凸的被积函数是微弹性与变分法领域的经典公开问题。此前已知的最小维度反例在$\mathbb{R}^{8\times 2}$（Grabovsky）和对称空间等领域仍有空缺，本文旨在将维度降到更低的$\mathbb{R}^{2\times 4}$以及对称矩阵空间，以深化对两个凸性概念之间差距的理解。

Method: 本文采用代数和Fourier分析方法。首先对Grabovsky在$\mathbb{R}^{8\times 2}$中的反例进行转置（transposition）和限制（restriction）操作，降维得到$\mathbb{R}^{2\times 4}$中的例子。通过构造一个$\mathbb{T}^4\to\mathbb{R}^2$的映射（含12个非零Fourier模）来验证非拟凸性。由于该映射可由标量势导出，进一步得到$\mathbb{R}^{4\times 4}_{\text{sym}}$中的秩一凸非拟凸例子。同时通过对Šverák经典例子进行修改，在$\mathbb{R}^{3\times 3}_{\text{sym}}$中构造新的反例。

Result: 成功构造了$\mathbb{R}^{2\times 4}$中显式的秩一凸非拟凸被积函数，并通过标量势将其推广到$\mathbb{R}^{4\times 4}_{\text{sym}}$；同时通过修改Šverák的例子得到$\mathbb{R}^{3\times 3}_{\text{sym}}$中的秩一凸非拟凸被积函数。这分别将非对称和对称矩阵空间中该类反例的维度显著降低。

Conclusion: 本文的结果表明，即便在$\mathbb{R}^{2\times 4}$这样的相对低维空间以及对称矩阵空间$\mathbb{R}^{4\times 4}_{\text{sym}}$和$\mathbb{R}^{3\times 3}_{\text{sym}}$中，秩一凸性仍然不能蕴含拟凸性。通过系统性地利用转置、限制和标量势技巧，该工作不仅丰富了已知反例库，也为最终确定两类凸性等价的最小临界维度提供了重要进展。

Abstract: We find an explicit rank-one convex non-quasiconvex integrand in $\mathbb{R}^{2\times 4}$: to falsify the quasiconvexity inequality, we exhibit a map $\mathbb{T}^4\to \mathbb{R}^2$ with $12$ non-zero Fourier modes. In fact, this map is obtained from a scalar potential, so we also find a rank one convex integrand in $\mathbb{R}^{4\times 4}_\text{sym}$ which is not quasiconvex. These examples are obtained by transpositions and restrictions of Grabovsky's example of a rank-one convex, non-quasiconvex integrand in $\mathbb{R}^{8 \times 2}.$ We also modify Šverák's example to construct a rank-one convex non-quasiconvex integrand in $\mathbb{R}^{3\times 3}_\text{sym}$.

</details>
