### [157] [Anisotropic Thermalization in Far-from-Equilibrium Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11445)
*Arnab Debnath,Timothy Breitzman,Kaushik Dayal*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需矩闭合近似的确定性间断伽辽金（DG）有限元方法来求解玻尔兹曼方程，针对一类远离平衡态的仿射流动（简单剪切、压力剪切、双向剪切和涡流）。研究发现速度分布在演化过程中始终近似为各向异性高斯分布，且无碰撞自由流极限下高斯分布的协方差张量等于右柯西-格林张量的逆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的玻尔兹曼方程求解方法通常依赖矩闭合近似或随机方法（如DSMC），前者在远离平衡态条件下精度不足，后者存在统计噪声。作者旨在发展一种确定性的数值方法，能够在不做矩闭合近似的前提下，直接求解远离平衡态的玻尔兹曼方程，并揭示此类流动中速度分布的内在结构。

Method: 利用仿射流动的特殊性，将玻尔兹曼方程约化为仅依赖速度空间的约化分布函数方程，从而降低求解维度。采用张量积拉格朗日 DG 离散方法对约化方程进行数值求解，并针对四种代表性流动（简单剪切、压力剪切、双向剪切和涡流）进行验证。同时通过理论推导，将自由流极限下高斯分布协方差张量与右柯西-格林张量建立联系。

Result: 数值结果表明，尽管系统远离平衡态，速度分布在整个演化过程中仍能由各向异性高斯分布很好地近似。在无碰撞自由流极限下，高斯分布的协方差张量等于右柯西-格林张量的逆，该理论预测在短时尺度上与数值解吻合良好；在长时间尺度上，由于碰撞效应的影响，两者逐渐偏离。

Conclusion: 该研究为远离平衡态的玻尔兹曼方程提供了一种确定性的、无需矩闭合近似的求解框架，并揭示了仿射流动中速度分布的各向异性高斯结构。这一发现为理解非平衡态动力学提供了新的理论视角，同时数值方法本身也为更复杂的非平衡流动模拟奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a deterministic discontinuous Galerkin (DG) finite-element solution of the Boltzmann equation, without moment-closure approximations, under a class of far-from-equilibrium deformations. Specifically, we consider affine flows which reduce the Boltzmann equation to a purely velocity-space problem for the reduced distribution function in a reduced velocity field. We solve the reduced equation using a tensor-product Lagrange DG discretization for four representative flows: simple shear, pressure shear, bi-directional shear, and a vortex flow. Our principal finding is that the velocity distribution is well-approximated by an anisotropic Gaussian throughout the evolution, despite the non-equilibrium conditions. Further, we show the evolution of the covariance tensor of the Gaussian distribution is equal to the inverse of the right Cauchy-Green tensor in the free-streaming limit without collisions. This prediction compares very well with the numerical solution at short times; at longer times, they grow apart, reflecting the influence of particle collisions.

</details>


### [158] [Influence of Flow on Discharge Behaviors and CO2 Conversion in Gliding Arc Discharges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11463)
*Alexander Davis,Charles Burton,Stephanie Pecaut,Matthew Hershey,Michelle M. Driscoll,Linsey C. Seitz,Dayne F. Swearer*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出将"平均放电时间"作为滑移电弧等离子体反应器的特征描述符，直接从电压和电流波形中提取，用于量化电弧动力学与流动稳定性之间的关系，为传统反应器表征方法无法捕获的瞬态行为提供操作条件下的定量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子反应器是电气化化学生产的独特手段，尤其在将CO2等温室气体转化回化学原料方面具有潜力。暖等离子体（如滑移电弧放电）作为一类无催化剂等离子体反应器，具有高能量效率和高可扩展性，但传统的反应器表征方法未能充分考虑电弧行为与流体动力学之间的联系，缺乏量化瞬态电弧动力学和稳定性的操作型描述符。

Method: 作者引入"平均放电时间"作为描述滑移电弧动力学特征的新参数，直接从电压和电流波形中提取该指标。利用高速摄影技术建立不同等离子体行为（模式）的电学特征签名，并评估放电时间分布与雷诺数之间的函数关系。

Result: 研究发现平均放电时间随雷诺数呈非线性下降趋势，建立了电弧行为与底层流体动力学之间的直接联系。该指标为滑移电弧的瞬态动力学和稳定性提供了定量、操作条件下的描述符，能够捕获传统表征方法所忽略的放电行为信息。

Conclusion: 平均放电时间是一种有效的操作型定量描述符，能够揭示滑移电弧放电的瞬态动力学与稳定性，弥补了传统反应器表征在电弧-流体耦合效应方面的不足，为暖等离子体反应器的性能评估和优化提供了新工具。

Abstract: Plasma reactors present themselves as a unique means of electrified chemical production, particularly in the conversion of greenhouse gases (e.g., CO2) back into chemical feedstocks. Warm plasmas, such as gliding arc discharges, represent a growing class of catalyst-free plasma reactors that demonstrate high energy efficiency and scalability. Here, we introduce mean discharge time as a characteristic descriptor of gliding arc dynamics, extracted directly from voltage and current waveforms. Electrical signatures for distinct plasma behaviors (modes) were established using high-speed photography, and discharge time distributions were evaluated as a function of Reynolds number. Mean discharge time is shown to decrease non-linearly with Reynolds number, providing a link between arc behavior and underlying fluid dynamics, which is otherwise neglected in traditional reactor characterization. Mean discharge time thereby provides a quantitative, operando descriptor of transient gliding arc dynamics and stability, offering insight into discharge behavior that traditional characterization approaches do not capture.

</details>


### [159] [Thermochemical non-equilibrium effects on turbulent boundary layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11717)
*Jun-Yang Li,Ming Yu,Dong Sun,Hong-Min Su,Peng-Xin Liu,Xian-Xu Yuan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过直接数值模拟研究了高马赫数湍流边界层在高焓条件下的流动，对比了低焓完全气体、化学非平衡高温混合气体和完全热化学非平衡高温混合气体三种工况，发现高焓效应几乎不改变速度统计量但显著改变近壁温度场，导致平动-转动温度与振动温度出现显著偏差，传统广义雷诺类比失效，为此提出一种新的复合广义雷诺类比；同时揭示热非平衡会抑制O2离解并促进NO生成，并通过谱分析评估了大涡模拟的亚格子闭合项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高马赫数湍流边界层在高焓条件下涉及湍流、化学反应与振动能量弛豫之间的复杂耦合，现有广义雷诺类比（GRA）无法准确描述振动温度行为，同时两温度模型对湍流统计量及近壁化学反应的影响尚不清楚，亟需通过高保真DNS数据进行系统研究并发展改进的模型。

Method: 采用直接数值模拟（DNS）方法，对三种流动条件（低焓量热完全气体、化学非平衡高温混合气体、完全热化学非平衡高温混合气体）的高马赫数湍流边界层进行模拟，通过对比分析两温度模型对湍流统计量的影响，考察湍流-化学-振动能耦合机制；提出基于振动温度的复合广义雷诺类比（composite GRA）对标准公式进行修正；并对湍流-化学反应及湍流-振动弛豫相互作用项进行谱分析，进而通过对谱函数积分评估大涡模拟的亚格子闭合项。

Result: 研究发现：高焓效应对速度统计量几乎无影响，但显著改变近壁温度场；近壁区域平动-转动温度与振动温度之间存在显著差异，传统GRA对振动温度失效，而提出的复合GRA与DNS数据吻合良好；热非平衡效应抑制O2离解并促进NO生成，导致O和NO的平均浓度分别降低和升高；谱分析表明温度脉动主导能量包含尺度上的湍流-化学及湍流-振动弛豫相互作用量；在小滤波尺度下交叉相关项量级可与温度脉动项相当甚至更大，而在较大滤波尺度下温度脉动项占主导。

Conclusion: 本文通过DNS系统揭示了高焓高马赫数湍流边界层中热化学非平衡效应对湍流统计量、近壁温度分布及化学反应的关键影响，提出的复合GRA有效修正了传统公式对振动温度描述的不足，并为大涡模拟中亚格子闭合项的建模提供了基于谱分析的定量依据，对高超声速飞行器热防护和气动热预测具有重要意义。

Abstract: This investigation employs direct numerical simulations (DNS) of high-Mach-number turbulent boundary layers under three flow conditions: a low-enthalpy calorically perfect gas, and two high-temperature gas mixtures, one in chemical non-equilibrium state and the other in full thermochemical non-equilibrium state. The influences of the two-temperature model on turbulent statistics and the coupling among turbulence, chemistry, and vibrational energy are examined. It is found that while high-enthalpy effects leave the velocity statistics virtually unchanged, they dramatically modify the near-wall temperature field. A pronounced disparity between the translational-rotational temperature and the vibrational temperature arises in the near-wall region, rendering the conventional generalized Reynolds analogy (GRA) inaccurate for vibrational temperature. To remedy this, a novel composite GRA is proposed that blends a vibrational-temperature-based relation with the standard formulation, and it demonstrates excellent agreement with the DNS data. Thermal non-equilibrium effects also substantially alter near-wall chemical reactions: it suppresses O2 dissociation while promoting NO formation, leading to a corresponding decrease and increase in the mean concentrations of O and NO, respectively. Spectral analyses of the turbulence-chemistry and turbulence-vibrational relaxation interaction terms reveal that temperature fluctuations dominate these flow quantities at energy-containing scales. Integrating the resulting spectral functions, we evaluate subgrid-scale closure terms for large-eddy simulation. At small filtering scales, the magnitude of the cross-correlation term rivals or exceeds that of the temperature fluctuation term, whereas the temperature fluctuation term becomes dominant at larger filter scales.

</details>


### [160] [A transport geometry of acoustic analogies:exact holonomy of source re-attribution and its observable consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12031)
*Sparsh Sharma*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文首次为声学类比（acoustic analogy）的非唯一性建立了定量几何结构。通过将类比组织为有效介质空间上的纤维族，并用频率保持、无旋转的线性时空映射定义类比间的输运，作者得到了三个精确结果：对流类比族在输运下不封闭（可生成Goldstein广义介质）；类比输运的离散和乐（holonomy）以闭式给出，包括精确谱膨胀和秩四阶消失的旋转；连续极限下升压扇区平坦而曲率集中于各向异性方向。所有类比给出相同远场，但近似源模型在类比间输运时会产生精确、无参数的谱膨胀与指向性旋转偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声学类比源项的非唯一性（即Navier-Stokes方程的不同重排将相同的辐射声归因于不同的表观源）早已被认识，但从未获得定量结构。作者旨在填补这一空白，为类比之间的变换建立严谨的数学几何框架。

Method: 将声学类比组织为有效介质空间上的纤维族，并通过唯一的、频率保持的、无旋转的线性时空映射定义类比间的输运。分析了对流类比的闭包性、离散和乐结构（谱膨胀加上四阶消失旋转）、以及连续极限下的曲率（升压扇区平坦、各向异性方向由声速张量增量的交换子给出）。所有恒等式均进行符号与数值验证。

Result: (1) 经典对流类比族在输运下不封闭，会生成具有各向异性声速张量的广义介质（即Goldstein介质）；(2) 离散输运和乐由精确谱膨胀和四阶消失旋转给出，且在声学视界上输运奇异；(3) 连续极限下升压扇区平坦，曲率仅存在于各向异性方向。物理远场不变，但近似源模型输运产生精确无参数的谱膨胀与指向性旋转偏差；各向异性介质的精确远场律扩展至开放输运路径。

Conclusion: 声学类比的几何结构具有操作意义而非物理意义：所有精确类比产生相同远场。其主要价值在于，当混合预测方法在类比间输运近似源模型时，可精确量化由此产生的谱膨胀与指向性旋转偏差，为各向异性介质提供精确远场定律，且所有结论经符号和数值验证。

Abstract: The source term of an acoustic analogy is not unique: different rearrangements of the Navier-Stokes equations attribute the same radiated sound to different apparent sources. Although this non-uniqueness has long been recognised, it has never been given a quantitative structure. We provide one by organising acoustic analogies into a fibre-like family over the space of effective media and defining transport between analogies through unique frequency-preserving, rotation-free linear space-time maps. Three exact results follow. First, the classical family of convected analogies is not closed under transport: successive uniform-flow regaugings generate effective media with anisotropic sound-speed tensors, recovering the generalised media introduced by Goldstein. Second, the discrete holonomy of analogy transport is obtained in closed form. It consists of an exact spectral dilation and a rotation that vanishes to fourth order in Mach number; transport becomes singular on a sonic horizon in analogy space. Third, in the continuum limit the boost sector is flat, while curvature is confined to anisotropy directions and is given by the commutator of sound-speed-tensor increments. The geometry does not imply any change in the physical sound field: all exact analogies yield the same far field. Its significance is operational. When an approximate source model is transported between analogies, as commonly occurs in hybrid prediction methods, the resulting far-field predictions acquire an exact, parameter-free bias consisting of a rigid spectral dilation and directivity rotation. An exact far-field law for anisotropic media extends these results to open transport paths. All identities are verified symbolically and numerically.

</details>


### [161] [Strain-coupled one-dimensional turbulence for rapid distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12048)
*Sparsh Sharma*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于一维湍流（ODT）的应变耦合公式，以低计算成本演化受平均应变扭曲的湍流，该公式结合了连续强迫、能量守恒的快速压力-应变再分配算子和域膨胀缩放，能够再现快速畸变理论并在有限应变下捕获非线性弛豫及宽带畸变谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均应变对湍流的扭曲（分量放大、压力介导再分配、谱缩放）对风洞收缩、升力面近滞止区等流动至关重要，但现有方法各有缺陷：尺度分辨模拟昂贵、线性快速畸变理论忽略有限应变下的非线性弛豫、二阶矩封闭丢弃谱信息。因此需要一种既能保留谱信息又经济高效的方法。

Method: 将平均应变产生项作为线速度的连续强迫施加；引入与均匀快速畸变理论一致的能量守恒再分配算子来表示快速压力-应变贡献；通过ODT域的膨胀（dilatation）表征尺度压缩。在未扭曲湍流关于ODT线轴对称的情况下，非局域三维快速压力-应变积分可约化为闭合的单线泛函。

Result: 模型预测的宽频畸变谱在应变-湍流比约0.8处偏离线性理论的刚性谱平移，该区域快速畸变与非线性涡动力学同时活跃。公式在启动阶段再现快速畸变理论，并定量捕获Lee和Reynolds（1985）的雷诺应力各向异性趋势。

Conclusion: 该应变耦合ODT公式提供了一种经济且具有谱分辨能力的湍流应变成像框架，能够桥接线性快速畸变理论与非线性湍流演化之间的间隙，适用于工程相关的受应变湍流流动。

Abstract: The distortion of turbulence by mean strain - through component amplification, pressure-mediated redistribution and spectral rescaling - is central to flows ranging from wind-tunnel contractions to stagnation regions near lifting surfaces. Capturing this process economically remains difficult: scale-resolving simulation is expensive, linear rapid-distortion theory omits nonlinear relaxation over finite strain, and second-moment closures discard spectral information. We present a strain-coupled formulation of one-dimensional turbulence (ODT) that evolves strained turbulence with one-dimensional scale resolution at low computational cost. Mean-strain production is imposed as a continuous forcing of the line velocity, the rapid pressure-strain contribution is introduced as an energy-conserving redistribution operator consistent with homogeneous rapid-distortion theory, and scale compression is represented by dilatation of the ODT domain. The model predicts a broadband distorted spectrum that departs from the rigid spectral translation of linear theory at a strain-to-turbulence ratio of approximately 0.8, where rapid distortion and nonlinear eddy dynamics are both active. Under axisymmetry of the undistorted turbulence about the ODT line, the nonlocal three-dimensional rapid pressure-strain integral reduces to a closed single-line functional. The formulation reproduces rapid-distortion theory at onset and quantitatively captures the Reynolds-stress anisotropy trends of Lee and Reynolds (1985).

</details>


### [162] [Effects of Soret Diffusion and Radiative Heat Loss on the Evolution of Buoyant Flame Kernels in Ultra-Lean Hydrogen-Air Mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12223)
*Ivan S. Yakovenko,Alexey D. Kiverin*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过详细化学机理和低马赫数数值模拟，研究了静止6vol.%氢气-空气混合物中超贫氢火焰在重力作用下的燃烧行为，定量分析了Soret扩散和辐射热损失对火焰核生长、浮力上升、变形及破碎的独立与耦合影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超贫氢火焰在地面重力下受优先扩散、热扩散、热损失与自诱导对流耦合作用控制，其火焰核生长与破碎机制尚未被充分阐明，尤其是Soret扩散与辐射热损失各自和联合的作用需系统定量研究。

Method: 采用详细化学机理与低马赫数公式，对6vol.% H2-空气混合物进行数值模拟。构建完整计算组合：独立启用/禁用Soret扩散与光学薄辐射热损失。用一维球对称计算隔离点火后初始火焰核生长，用二维平面与轴对称模拟描述火焰的浮力上升、变形与破碎。

Result: 火焰前锋半径遵循Rf^2≈Kt而非匀速扩展；Soret扩散增大有效增长系数K，辐射减小K。轴对称计算比平面计算更好地再现实验中的前导点轨迹。Soret扩散产生更大更快上升且上帽更接近圆形的火焰核，辐射对轨迹影响较弱但增加横向扁平化。环形涡拉伸火焰段并导致局部熄火和碎片化；Soret扩散延迟破碎而辐射提前破碎，二者对破碎时间的影响近乎相互抵消。

Conclusion: Soret输运与辐射主要改变火焰核的生长速率及对涡诱导熄火的抵抗能力，而定性破碎路径仍由流体动力学控制，即破碎的基本机制不受Soret与辐射的定性改变。

Abstract: Ultra-lean hydrogen flames under terrestrial gravity are governed by a coupled interaction among preferential diffusion, thermal diffusion, heat loss, and self-induced convection. This study numerically examines combustion in a quiescent 6~vol.\% H$_2$--air mixture using detailed chemistry and a low-Mach-number formulation. A complete calculations set was considered, with Soret diffusion and optically thin radiative heat loss independently enabled and disabled. One-dimensional spherical calculations were used to isolate the initial post-ignition flame kernel growth, while two-dimensional planar and axisymmetric simulations described its subsequent buoyant rise, deformation, and breakup. Over the analyzed interval, the spherical flame-front radius followed $R_f^2\approx Kt$ rather than constant-speed expansion. Soret diffusion increased the effective growth coefficient $K$, whereas radiation reduced it. The axisymmetric calculations reproduced the experimentally measured leading-point trajectory substantially better than the planar formulation. Soret diffusion produced larger, faster-rising kernels and maintained a more nearly circular upper cap, whereas radiation had a weaker effect on trajectory but increased relative lateral flattening. In all cases, a toroidal vortex stretched the flame segment and caused local extinction and fragmentation. Soret diffusion delayed breakup, while radiation advanced it; their combined effect on breakup time was nearly compensating. The results show that Soret transport and radiation primarily alter kernel growth and resistance to vortex-induced extinction, while the qualitative breakup pathway remains hydrodynamically controlled.

</details>

### [163] [Heavy Ball GMRESR method for Nonsymmetric Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11270)
*Mei Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出HBGMRESR方法，将HBGMRES作为内循环替代GMRESR中的GMRES内方法，在保留GMRESR全局最小化优势的同时恢复收敛速度，数值实验表明其优于GMRESR和HBGMRES。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GMRESR虽然比HBGMRES性能更好，但仍存在与GMRES及其重启版本类似的收敛缓慢问题。作者希望结合HBGMRES的优势（弥补重启GMRES收敛慢）与GMRESR的优势（在特定Krylov子空间部分进行全局最小化），构造一种新方法。

Method: 提出HBGMRESR方法：将GMRESR中的内循环GMRES替换为HBGMRES，以此在保留GMRESR全局最小化特性（即对外算法GCR产生的新搜索向量及所有先前搜索向量进行近似最优求解）的同时，利用HBGMRES弥补收敛速度的损失。

Result: 在真实数据上的数值测试表明，HBGMRESR在收敛性能上优于GMRESR和HBGMRES，验证了新方法的优越性。

Conclusion: HBGMRESR成功综合了HBGMRES与GMRESR各自的优点，既保持了GMRESR在Krylov子空间特定部分全局最小化的好处，又恢复了收敛速度，是求解线性系统的有效Krylov子空间方法。

Abstract: The heavy ball GMRES (HBGMRES) method is one of Krylov subspace methods for linear systems combined with the restarted GMRES and the heavy ball method which is applied in optimization. HBGMRES not only keeps benefit of the restarted GMRES in limiting memory usage and controlling orthogonalization cost, but also is able to cover up the slow convergence problem in the restarted GMRES. Another type of Krylov subspace methods is GMRESR which consists of GCR as the outer algorithm and certain steps of GMRES as an inner method. Compared with HBGMRES, GMRESR gives the approximately optimal solution over the new search vector gained from GMRES and all previously kept search vectors in GCR. Even though GMRESR performs better than HBGMRES, it has slow convergence which is similar to GMRES and the restarted GMRES. Inspired by HBGMRES, we present the heavy ball GMRESR method (HBGMRESR) by using HBGMRES as the inner loop instead of using GMRES to salvage the lost convergence speed while still keeping the benefit of GMRESR that in the sense of a global minimization over some specific part of the Krylov subspace is done. Numerical tests on real data are presented to demonstratee the superiority of the new methods over GMRESR and HBGMRES.

</details>


### [164] [Stability of Finite-Batch Particle Mean-Field Variational Inference Beyond Strong Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11486)
*Vinh Nguyen,Truong Vu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了均值场变分推断（MFVI）的有限批量粒子算法，将其视为投影Wasserstein动力学的全离散随机逼近。在目标势能全局光滑但非强凸的条件下，通过曲率缺陷量量化与收缩性的偏离，证明了非渐近Wasserstein稳定性界，该界将初始化、乘积经验近似、有限批量漂移误差、时间离散化以及沿耦合轨迹累积的缺陷分开。在均匀缺陷界下，粒子迭代保持在MFVI最小值的O(√(β/α))范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有粒子算法分析通常依赖强凸假设（收缩性），而实际应用中的目标势能往往只是全局光滑而非强凸。作者希望量化非强凸情形下与收缩性的偏离，并建立可实现的有限批量粒子算法的非渐近稳定性保证。

Method: 将有限批量粒子算法形式化为投影Wasserstein动力学的全离散随机逼近；引入曲率缺陷量d_α(x,y)刻画一步Euler收缩估计的加性损失；构造平稳比较阵列（总体律为MFVI最小化器、粒子级律为随机乘积经验测度），显式控制投影漂移差异；给出逐坐标缺陷估计和维度无关投影漂移敏感性的结构条件；构造任意维光滑非凸基准函数（具有闭式MFVI解）说明方法的有效性；讨论多项式增长漂移需要修改未驯服的显式格式的原因。

Result: 在均匀缺陷界d_α≤β下，粒子迭代与任意MFVI最小化器的距离保持在O(√(β/α))以内，误差项对粒子数、批量大小和步长具有显式依赖。提供了维度无关性的结构条件，以及一个任意维非凸但具有闭式MFVI解的基准实例。

Conclusion: 该工作将MFVI粒子算法的理论从强凸情形扩展到非强凸光滑情形，通过曲率缺陷的概念建立了清晰的稳定性保证，并指出多项式增长漂移下需要改进显式格式。

Abstract: We study the implementable finite-batch particle algorithm for mean-field variational inference as a fully discrete stochastic approximation of the projected Wasserstein dynamics. The target potential is globally smooth but need not be strongly convex. The departure from contractivity is quantified by the curvature defect \[ \mathfrak d_α(x,y) =
\bigl[α\|x-y\|^2- \langle\nabla V(x)-\nabla V(y),x-y\rangle\bigr]_+, \] which is the additive loss in the one-step Euler contraction estimate. We prove a non-asymptotic Wasserstein stability bound that separates initialization, product-empirical approximation, finite-batch drift error, time discretization, and the defects accumulated along the coupled trajectories. Under the uniform bound $\mathfrak d_α\leqβ$, the particle iterates remain within $O(\sqrt{β/α})$ of any MFVI minimizer, up to explicit errors in the particle number, batch size, and step size. The proof uses a stationary comparison array whose population law is an MFVI minimizer but whose particle-level law is a random product empirical measure, and it controls the resulting projected-drift discrepancy explicitly. We also give coordinatewise defect estimates and structural conditions for dimension-independent projected-drift sensitivity, construct an arbitrary-dimensional smooth nonconvex benchmark with a closed-form MFVI minimizer, and explain why polynomially growing drifts require a modification of the untamed explicit scheme.

</details>


### [165] [Exact characterisation of maximum-angle conditions for spherical finite element meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11353)
*Hiroki Ishizaka*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对球面三角剖分，建立了内在球面最大角条件的代数刻画，通过三个顶点向量构成的无量纲量给出统一正下界判定，并推导出球面半正则参数与弦半正则参数之间具有锐利常数2/√3的局部无关比较，从而无需最小角或形状正则假设即可为各向异性有限元分析提供几何控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大角条件是标准有限元网格假设，允许各向异性三角形；然而精确球面三角形上，弦仿射核心角度与内在球面角度不一致，径向几何还引入曲率尺度畸变。现有最小角或形状正则假设过于严格，无法适用于球面网格的各向异性分析，因此需要建立球面内在最大角条件与有限元分析所需几何控制之间的联系。

Method: 给出三个顶点向量构造的无量纲量，其统一正下界与球面最大角条件等价，无需评估球面角或球面面积。借助支撑平面几何将弦外接半径与径向畸变联系起来，推导内在球面与弦半正则参数之间的锐利比较（常数2/√3，并刻画等号情形）；证明基于面积的参数小于球面半正则参数，局部平坦极限下锐利常数为1。对球面有限元网格，顶点判据蕴含具有显式常数的统一弦半正则性，相对细化使径向畸变因子一致收敛到1。

Result: 建立了球面最大角条件的代数等价判据；获得了球面与弦半正则参数间常数2/√3的锐利局部无关比较及其等号刻画；证明了面积参数在局部平坦极限下以锐利常数1小于球面半正则参数；球面网格的顶点判据给出显式常数的弦半正则性，相对细化保证径向畸变因子一致收敛到1。

Conclusion: 内在球面最大角判据结合相对细化，提供了各向异性有限元分析所需的几何控制，且无需施加最小角或形状正则条件，适用于球面三角剖分。

Abstract: Maximum-angle conditions are standard finite-element mesh hypotheses that permit anisotropic triangles excluded by minimum-angle or shape-regularity assumptions. For exact spherical triangles, however, the angles of the chordal affine core and the intrinsic spherical angles need not coincide, while radial geometry introduces curvature-scale distortion. We give an algebraic characterisation of the intrinsic spherical maximum-angle condition through a dimensionless quantity computed from the three vertex vectors. A uniform positive lower bound on this quantity is equivalent to a uniform spherical maximum-angle bound and requires neither spherical-angle nor spherical-area evaluation. We identify the support-plane geometry linking the chordal circumradius to radial distortion and derive sharp comparisons between the intrinsic spherical and chordal semi-regularity parameters. In particular, a locality-independent comparison holds with sharp constant $2/\sqrt3$ and a characterised equality case. An area-based parameter is shown to be smaller than the spherical semi-regularity parameter, with sharp constant one in the local flat limit. For spherical finite-element meshes, the vertex criterion implies uniform chordal semi-regularity with an explicit constant, while relative refinement makes the radial-distortion factors converge uniformly to one. Thus, the intrinsic criterion, together with relative refinement, provides the geometric controls used in anisotropic finite-element analysis without imposing a minimum-angle or shape-regularity condition.

</details>


### [166] [Linear-cost Polyharmonic Spline Interpolation of Arbitrary Degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11462)
*Christopher J. Geoga,Michael O'Neil*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合快速多极子方法（FMM）和Vecchia近似的高效多调和样条（PHS）插值方法，利用对数和距离两个核的FMM实现所有阶数的快速PHS插值，并通过Matérn协方差模型的稀疏逆近似进行预条件处理，使共轭梯度法在少于15次迭代内收敛（即使超过百万点），薄板样条插值可在约50-60次FMM的成本下达到与全稠密O(n³)计算相当的精度，并开源了二维奇数阶PHS插值的高性能软件库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多调和样条（PHS）插值，特别是薄板样条插值，在无参数调优的情况下具有广泛的应用价值，但传统全稠密计算方法复杂度高达O(n³)，难以扩展到大规模问题。现有方法要么依赖特定核函数的快速算法，要么在精度和效率间做出折中，缺少一种同时具备简单性、高性能和通用性的解决方案。

Method: 该方法基于Hadamard积和低秩矩阵的性质，证明仅使用对数和距离两种核的FMM即可实现所有阶数的PHS插值；同时引入Matérn协方差模型下的稀疏逆近似方法作为预条件器，配合对不允许子空间的精心管理，采用预条件共轭梯度（PCG）求解预测权重。

Result: 所提方法在超过一百万个点的规模下，预条件共轭梯度法在少于15次迭代内收敛。薄板样条插值在约50-60次FMM的计算成本下，达到了与全稠密O(n³)计算相当的精度。此外，作者发布了面向二维奇数阶PHS插值的高性能开源软件库。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过巧妙结合计算静电学中的FMM与高斯过程/稀疏近似逆文献中的Vecchia近似，可以在不损失精度的情况下大幅降低PHS插值的计算复杂度，使得薄板样条这类无需参数调优的插值方法能够高效处理百万级规模的问题，为该类方法的大规模实际应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: We introduce a simple and performant approach for rapidly and accurately performing polyharmonic spline (PHS) interpolation using a combination of the fast multipole method (FMM) from computational electrostatics and the Vecchia approximation from the Gaussian process and sparse approximate inverse literatures. Using basic properties about Hadamard products and low-rank matrices, we demonstrate that an FMM with two kernels, the logarithmic and distance kernels, results in fast PHS interpolation for all orders. Furthermore, we demonstrate the exceptional performance of sparse inverse approximation methods with the Matérn covariance model for preconditioning. Combined with careful management of disallowed subspaces, we describe a procedure for obtaining prediction weights using preconditioned conjugate gradient that converges in less than $15$ iterations, even for problem sizes with over one million points. As a result, thin-plate spline interpolation---a particularly popular method that does not require parameter tuning---that matches the fully dense $\mathcal{O}(n^3)$ computation in accuracy can be done at the cost of approximately $50-60$ FMMs. A high-performance software library for odd-order PHS interpolation in two dimensions is made available as a companion to this work.

</details>


### [167] [Contour Hankel dynamics and indicator fields for the Riemann $Ξ$-function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11520)
*Zhiliang Deng,Xiaomei Yang,Huazhong Lü*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个移动轮廓Hankel框架，用于编码Riemann Ξ函数的局部零点配置。通过加权轮廓积分将Ξ'/Ξ的对数导数表示为封闭零点坐标像上的有限原子测度的幂矩，建立了精确的无零点判据、秩判据和惯性公式，从而将黎曼假设转化为局部有限维Hankel正定性问题。轮廓移动时Hankel矩阵经历连续合同流和有限秩跳跃，并提出了从分段常数矩阵过程恢复零点位置的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼假设是数学中最核心的未解决问题之一，传统分析方法难以构造局部零点信息的有限维代数刻画。作者希望将零点配置问题与有限维矩阵理论（特别是Hankel矩阵与惯性定理）联系起来，从而为黎曼假设提供一个新的局部有限维代数表述视角，并建立可以数值验证的可计算框架。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 构造移动轮廓Hankel框架，将轮廓上Ξ'/Ξ的对数导数加权积分映射为谱坐标下的幂矩；(2) 利用幂矩与有限原子测度的一一对应，建立Hankel矩阵的无零点判据与秩判据；(3) 对满足共轭相容性的轮廓-坐标对，推导负惯性指数等于不同非实共轭对数量的惯性公式；(4) 分析轮廓移动时Hankel矩阵的连续合同流演化及其在零点穿越时的有限秩跳跃（孤立零点对应符号秩一跳跃，非实共轭对对应不定秩二事件）；(5) 通过去除连续坐标漂移得到分段常数矩阵过程，据此恢复穿越坐标、重数和零点位置；(6) 数值实验验证轮廓求积、指示场、连续流、穿越签名及恢复过程。

Result: 数值实验验证了轮廓求积的准确性、指示场的正确性、连续流的演化行为、零点穿越时的签名特征以及零点恢复过程的可行性。理论结果表明，一旦Hankel矩阵阶数达到或超过不同坐标节点数，负惯性指数恰好等于不同非实共轭对的数量。这为黎曼假设提供了局部有限维Hankel正定性表述，但独立于零点集建立该正定性仍然未解决。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个将Riemann Ξ函数零点配置与有限维Hankel矩阵理论联系起来的统一框架，给出了精确的代数判据和可计算的数值方案。黎曼假设被转化为局部Hankel正定性问题，但该正定性仍需独立验证。框架同时为轮廓移动过程中的矩阵演化（连续合同流与离散跳跃）提供了完整的描述，并展示了从矩阵过程恢复零点信息的能力。

Abstract: We develop a moving-contour Hankel framework for encoding local zero configurations of the Riemann $Ξ$-function. Weighted contour integrals of the logarithmic derivative $Ξ'/Ξ$, expressed in a holomorphic coordinate associated with the contour, are identified with the power moments of a finite atomic measure supported at the coordinate images of the enclosed zeros. This representation yields exact zero-free and rank criteria and, for conjugation-compatible contour-coordinate pairs, an inertia formula: once the matrix order is at least the number of distinct coordinate nodes, the negative index equals the number of distinct nonreal conjugate pairs. Consequently, the Riemann hypothesis admits a local finite-dimensional Hankel-positivity formulation, although establishing this positivity independently of the zero set remains unresolved. As the contour moves, the Hankel matrix evolves by a continuous congruence flow between zero crossings and undergoes finite-rank jumps at crossing events. An isolated zero produces a signed rank-one jump, whereas a nonreal conjugate pair produces a rank-two indefinite event in a real-axis circular scan that meets the pair. Removing the continuous coordinate drift yields a piecewise-constant matrix process from which crossing coordinates, multiplicities, and zero locations can be recovered. Numerical experiments validate the contour quadrature, indicator fields, continuous flow, crossing signatures, and recovery procedure.

</details>


### [168] [An Interface Green's Function Framework for Complete Discrete $W^{1,\infty}$ Analysis of Discontinuous Galerkin Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11559)
*Haitao Leng,Weifeng Qiu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个界面Green函数框架，用于对凸多面体域上DG方法的完整离散W^{1,∞}误差分析，通过新的局部能量估计（利用Taylor展开和相邻离散delta函数间的相消）实现了无额外对数因子的界面估计，填补了间断逼近完整离散W^{1,∞}理论的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去二十年DG方法的逐点误差分析已较为成熟，建立了多种DG方法在L^∞范数和broken W^{1,∞}半范数下的最优误差估计。然而，broken W^{1,∞}半范数无法控制网格界面上的间断（跳跃），导致间断逼近的完整离散W^{1,∞}理论一直缺失，这是该领域的根本性空白。

Method: 本文开发了一个界面Green函数框架：(1) 引入新的界面Green函数来表示间断逼近在网格界面上的跳跃；(2) 核心分析工具是一个新的局部能量估计，通过Taylor展开论证利用相邻离散delta函数之间的相消效应获得；(3) 与现有Green函数分析中的估计有本质区别，能够在不引入额外对数因子的情况下推导界面估计。

Result: 该框架成功在凸多面体域上实现了DG方法的完整离散W^{1,∞}分析，包括对界面跳跃的控制，且界面估计没有引入额外的对数因子，优于或至少不比现有分析结果差。

Conclusion: 本文填补了DG方法的完整离散W^{1,∞}理论空白，通过界面Green函数框架和新的局部能量估计，成功建立了包含界面间断控制的W^{1,∞}误差分析，为间断有限元方法的逐点误差理论提供了更完备的数学基础。

Abstract: Pointwise error analysis of discontinuous Galerkin (DG) methods has received considerable attention during the past two decades. Existing analyses establish optimal error estimates in the $L^{\infty}$ norm and the broken $W^{1,\infty}$ seminorm for several DG methods. However, since the broken $W^{1,\infty}$ seminorm does not control discontinuities across mesh interfaces, a complete discrete $W^{1,\infty}$ theory for discontinuous approximations has remained unavailable.
  In this paper, we develop an interface Green's function framework for the complete discrete $W^{1,\infty}$ analysis of DG methods on convex polyhedral domains. The proposed framework introduces new interface Green's functions that represent jumps of discontinuous approximations across mesh interfaces. Its central analytical ingredient is a new local energy estimate for these Green's functions, obtained through a Taylor-expansion argument that exploits cancellation between neighboring discrete delta functions. This estimate differs fundamentally from those in existing Green's function analyses and enables the derivation of interface estimates without introducing additional logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [169] [Variational Enrichment of Adaptive Finite Element Spaces Beyond Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11764)
*Matthew Francis Dixon*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"变分增广"(variational enrichment)的自适应有限元方法，将紧凑的问题相关信息函数与传统的h-和p-细化相结合，适用于Galerkin和最小残差格式。通过商-Schur分析去除冗余增广并度量其变分值，对对称强制问题可给出精确能量误差缩减及参考无关上界。实验表明该方法的"hc"细化在结构匹配PDE算子时有效，否则回退到普通hp细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自适应有限元方法仅能通过细化网格(h)或提高多项式阶数(p)来改进精度，但对于一些需要非多项式结构化表示的问题（如异质介质、随机系数、不规则域、奇异性、界面、输运等问题），这种策略在计算上可能代价高昂。作者希望引入一种能够利用问题感知信息的紧凑函数来补充传统细化方式，从而更高效地捕获无法由多项式有效表达的结构。

Method: 提出变分增广框架：(1) 在Galerkin和最小残差公式中引入紧凑的问题相关信息函数作为增广；(2) 使用商-Schur分析自动去除冗余增广，度量每个增广函数的变分价值；(3) 对对称强制问题推导精确的能量误差缩减公式和参考无关上界；(4) 形成hc细化策略——当增广函数与PDE算子结构匹配时用于细化，否则回退到普通hp细化。

Result: 在异质介质、随机系数、不规则域、奇异性、界面、输运和振荡测试等广泛场景中验证了方法。在SPE10模型1和2上，系数自适应函数在所有11个规定案例的等维度条件下均优于多项式增广，最大提升41.6%。正负实验证据共同表明hc策略能选择有用的算子兼容结构，否则回退到普通hp细化。

Conclusion: 变分增广提供了一种通用且灵活的框架，将问题驱动的非多项式函数纳入自适应有限元方法。该方法的有效性取决于所选择的紧凑函数是否与隐藏的未解析结构相匹配——匹配时显著增益，不匹配时也能安全回退到传统hp细化，从而不会造成性能损失。

Abstract: Adaptive finite elements refine meshes or increase polynomial degree, but coherent non-polynomial structure may remain expensive in either coordinate. We introduce variational enrichment, in which compact problem-informed functions complement ordinary h- and p-refinement. The framework applies to Galerkin and minimum-residual formulations whenever admissible functions and their residual value can be evaluated; it is not tied to one PDE class. Quotient-Schur analysis removes redundant additions and measures their variational value. For symmetric coercive problems, the present theory also gives an exact energy-error reduction and a reference-independent upper bound.
  Across heterogeneous media, stochastic coefficients, irregular domains, singularities, interfaces, transport, and oscillatory tests, hc helps when a compact function captures unresolved structure aligned with the PDE operator; it offers little benefit when that structure is redundant or mismatched. On SPE10 Models 1 and 2, coefficient-adapted functions improve polynomial enrichment in all eleven prescribed cases at equal dimension, by up to $41.6\%$. The wider positive and negative evidence supports the same conclusion: hc selects useful operator-compatible structure and otherwise falls back to ordinary hp refinement.

</details>


### [170] [Fast eight-order Pade schemes based on Chebyshev polynomials for direct Zakharov-Shabat problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11892)
*Sergey Medvedev,Igor Chekhovskoy,Irina Vaseva,Mikhail Fedoruk*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对直接Zakharov-Shabat散射问题，构造了快速八阶Padé格式。该格式基于Magnus展开得到的八阶指数积分器。直接将传统快速Padé表示扩展到八阶会在测试中精度不足，因此作者利用Joukowski映射将谱依赖重新表述在有限实区间上，并在Chebyshev多项式基中表示局部分子和分母，从而通过快速乘积树构建全局转移矩阵，同时保持紧凑的多项式表示。数值实验表明，该方案显著提高了精度，可高效计算连续非线性谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性谱（Zakharov-Shabat散射问题）的数值计算需要高精度和高效率的格式。传统快速Padé方法在低阶精度尚可，但直接扩展到八阶会出现精度不足的问题，因此需要构建一种兼具高阶精度和快速计算能力的格式。

Method: 基于Magnus展开构造八阶指数积分器，利用Joukowski映射将光谱依赖变换到有限实区间，并在Chebyshev多项式基中表示局部分子和分母，通过快速乘积树构建全局转移矩阵，避免直接扩展传统快速Padé表示所带来的精度损失。

Result: 针对正负两个色散方向的啁啾双曲正割势的数值实验表明，基于Chebyshev的快速格式相比对应的直接快速Padé变体，在精度上有显著提升，可高效计算连续非线性谱。

Conclusion: 所提出的Chebyshev基快速八阶Padé格式既能保持高阶（八阶）精度，又能通过快速乘积树实现高效计算，为连续非线性谱的数值求解提供了一种准确且高效的方法。

Abstract: In this work, we construct fast eighth-order Pade schemes for the direct Zakharov-Shabat scattering problem. The schemes are based on an eighth-order exponential integrator obtained from the Magnus expansion. A direct extension of the conventional fast Pade representation to the eighth-order case leads to insufficiently accurate fast variants in the considered tests. To overcome this difficulty, we reformulate the spectral dependence on a finite real interval using the Joukowski mapping and represent the local numerators and denominators in the Chebyshev polynomial basis. This makes it possible to construct the global transition matrix by a fast product tree while retaining a compact polynomial representation. Numerical experiments for chirped hyperbolic secant potentials with both signs of dispersion show that the proposed Chebyshev-based fast schemes substantially improve the accuracy of the corresponding direct fast variants and can be used for efficient computation of the continuous nonlinear spectrum.

</details>


### [171] [Positive quadrature and mobile sampling of multivariate trigonometric polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11915)
*Stefan Kunis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义求积方法（用曲线替代离散点与权重）的几何性质，给出了正广义求积对多元三角多项式精确时的覆盖半径上界，证明Korobov格点规则背后的秩1曲线在多种最优性准则下是准最优的，并展示了将其推广到2-球面的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典求积公式中点位的几何性质是数值分析中的传统课题，而广义求积方法（沿曲线积分替代离散点求积）近年来引起了越来越多的关注。本文旨在从几何角度深入理解这类新方法，并为多元三角多项式建立正广义求积的理论界限。

Method: 本文采用符号局部化测试函数（sign-localized test functions）技术推导正广义求积的覆盖半径上界，该方法可自然地推广到区间上的代数多项式、球面多项式以及双曲交叉三角多项式。在下界方面，作者比较了两种近期方法在多元三角多项式情境下的适用性。此外，针对秩1 Korobov格点规则所依赖的几何简单曲线，作者通过多种最优性准则证明了其准最优性，并将其推广至2-球面。

Result: 建立了正广义求积覆盖半径的上界；对求积曲线长度给出了下界比较分析；证明了秩1 Korobov格规则对应的曲线在多种最优性准则下是准最优的，并成功将其适应到2-球面上，展示了该方法的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 本文从几何角度系统分析了广义求积方法，建立了覆盖半径与曲线长度的上下界理论，并确立了Korobov秩1曲线的准最优性，将其推广至球面情形，为广义求积的现代发展提供了重要的理论与实用工具。

Abstract: The geometric properties of quadrature points in one and multiple dimensions are a classical topic in numerical analysis. Recently, generalized quadrature methods, where discrete points and weights are replaced by integration along curves, have attracted growing interest. This paper focuses on positive generalized quadratures that are exact for multivariate trigonometric polynomials. We derive upper bounds on the covering radius of such quadratures using sign-localized test functions, a technique that also extends naturally to algebraic polynomials on intervals, spherical polynomials, and hyperbolic cross trigonometric polynomials. For lower bounds on the length of quadrature curves, we compare two recent approaches in the context of multivariate trigonometric polynomials. The second part of the paper studies a well-known, geometrically simple curve that underlies rank-1 Korobov lattice rules for periodic functions. We prove that these curves are quasi-optimal with respect to multiple optimality criteria and demonstrate its adaptability to the 2-sphere.

</details>


### [172] [A Positivity-Preserving Expectation Scheme for Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman Equations with Oblique Robin Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11936)
*Haoran Xu,Xingye Yue*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对带混合导数的各向异性扩散问题（可能存在退化），提出了一种基于条件期望而非代数模板分解的正性保持数值格式，用于求解带受控斜Robin边界条件的Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程。格式通过反射Feynman-Kac恒等式构造，Rademacher向量生成2^m个等概率弱Euler分支，Robin系数仅通过衰减因子进入，不需要对角线占优或CFL条件即可保持非负解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准紧致坐标对齐模板、局部有限体积或有限元构造在处理带混合导数的各向异性扩散时，缺乏系数或网格条件约束下会失去非负系数的性质。现有方法通过代数模板分解来获取非负系数，但存在局限性。因此需要一种直接生成非负系数的新途径，以构建正性保持的数值格式。

Method: 基于一步反射Feynman-Kac恒等式构造格式：m维Rademacher向量生成P=2^m个等概率弱Euler分支；外部分支关于其斜投影镜像，其两倍过冲量作为离散边界局部时间D；Robin系数仅通过衰减因子e^{-κD}和De^{-κD/2}g进入，分支概率保持等概率。内部节点采用此构造；边界节点采用同一层的空间偏移ℓ_h≍h的闭式，可为隐式。均匀斜率和P_1插值保证内点权重独立于网格。

Result: 固定线性Robin数据下产生稀疏非奇异M-矩阵系统；一般控制集下产生单调压缩映射。对非负数据保持正性，无需对角线占优条件，也无需Δt与h之间的CFL型限制。

Conclusion: 该格式通过条件期望直接生成非负系数，绕过了代数模板分解的限制，在无CFL条件和对角线占优假设下仍能保持正性，适用于可能退化的Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程。

Abstract: For anisotropic diffusion with mixed derivatives, standard compact
  coordinate-aligned stencils and some local finite-volume or finite-element
  constructions can lose nonnegative coefficients unless suitable coefficient
  or mesh conditions are imposed. Here nonnegative coefficients are generated
  directly from conditional expectation rather than from an algebraic stencil
  decomposition. We construct a positivity-preserving scheme for
  possibly degenerate Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equations with controlled
  oblique Robin boundary conditions. At interior nodes the construction stems
  from a one-step reflected Feynman--Kac identity: an $m$-dimensional Rademacher
  vector generates $P=2^m$ equally probable weak-Euler branches, an exterior
  branch is mirrored about its oblique projection, and twice its overshoot
  serves as the discrete boundary local time $D$. The Robin coefficient enters
  only through the attenuation factors $e^{-κD}$ and $De^{-κD/2}g$,
  leaving the equal branch probabilities unchanged. Boundary nodes use a
  separate same-level closure over a spatial offset $\ell_h\asymp h$, which may
  be implicit; uniform obliqueness and $\mathbb P_1$ interpolation give a
  mesh-independent positive weight on interior nodes, so fixed linear Robin data
  yield a sparse nonsingular $M$-matrix system and the general control set
  yields a monotone contraction. Positivity is preserved for nonnegative data
  without a diagonal-dominance condition and without any CFL-type relation
  between $Δt$ and $h$.

</details>


### [173] [Numerical methods for the simulation of quantum walks and quantum annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12058)
*Asa Hopkins,Viv Kendon*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对埃尔米特矩阵函数计算中的多项式逼近问题，首次系统分析了真正极小极大（minimax）逼近方法的可行性，并提供了三种近似方法的高性能C++开源实现，附带严格的误差分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作主要基于切比雪夫多项式逼近（已知接近最优收敛率），但缺乏对真正极小极大逼近的讨论，也没有高性能实现和严格的误差分析。

Method: 论文提供了对三种多项式近似方法的严格误差分析，并给出了相应的C++开源高性能实现，其中包含对切比雪夫逼近与真正极小极大逼近方法的比较和分析。

Result: 完成了三种逼近方法的严格误差分析，并发布了高性能开源C++实现。

Conclusion: 填补了埃尔米特矩阵函数多项式逼近中关于真正极小极大方法及其高性能实现的空白。

Abstract: It is known that Chebyshev based polynomial approximation gives a near-optimal rate of convergence for calculating a function of a Hermitian matrix. However, previous work has not discussed the option of true minimax approximation, nor the specifics of writing a high performance implementation with a rigorous analysis of errors. This work provides such an analysis and an open-source implementation of three approximation methods in C++.

</details>


### [174] [Halving the size of skew-symmetric eigenvalue problems via the polar decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12153)
*Daniel Kressner,Simon Mataigne*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种计算稠密实反对称矩阵特征值与特征向量的新算法，核心是利用反对称且正交的极因子将原问题转化为规模减半的 Hermitian 特征值问题，从而可借助 LAPACK 等标准软件高效精确求解；数值实验证明方法稳定且运行时间与现有方法相当，该原理还可推广至将正交特征值问题约化为规模减半的酉特征值问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密实反对称矩阵的特征值问题在数值线性代数中具有重要应用，但现有方法在稳定性或效率上存在不足；此外，利用反对称矩阵的共轭对特征值结构，将问题约化为更小规模的对称/Hermitian 问题有望提升求解效率与精度。

Method: 首先计算矩阵 A 的极因子（polar factor），该极因子同时满足反对称性与正交性；利用该极因子将原反对称特征值问题变换为规模减半的 Hermitian 特征值问题，再调用 LAPACK 等标准库求解。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法数值稳定，运行时间与现有反对称特征值问题方法相当；并验证了该方法可推广用于将正交特征值问题约化为规模减半的酉特征值问题。

Conclusion: 该算法提供了一种稳定、高效且易于利用现有标准软件实现的反对称矩阵特征值求解途径，同时其核心思想具有更广泛的推广价值，可用于正交特征值问题的简化。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel algorithm for computing eigenvalues and eigenvectors of a dense real skew-symmetric matrix $A$. Its main ingredient is the computation of a polar factor of $A$ that is both skew-symmetric and orthogonal. This polar factor is then used to transform the original problem into a Hermitian eigenvalue problem of half the size, which can be solved accurately and efficiently with standard software such as LAPACK. Numerical experiments demonstrate the stability of the method and show that its running time is competitive with existing approaches for skew-symmetric eigenvalue problems. Finally, we show that the same principle can be used to reduce an orthogonal eigenvalue problem to a unitary eigenvalue problem of half the size.

</details>
